### apps\passenger-app\App.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { NavigationContainer } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useFonts } from 'expo-font';
import {
  PlusJakartaSans_400Regular,
  PlusJakartaSans_500Medium,
  PlusJakartaSans_600SemiBold,
  PlusJakartaSans_700Bold,
  PlusJakartaSans_800ExtraBold,
} from '@expo-google-fonts/plus-jakarta-sans';
import {
  JetBrainsMono_400Regular,
  JetBrainsMono_500Medium,
  JetBrainsMono_700Bold,
} from '@expo-google-fonts/jetbrains-mono';

import { ThemeProvider, useTheme } from './src/theme';
import { AuthProvider } from './src/lib/auth';
import { ToastProvider } from './src/components/ui';
import { TransitLoader } from './src/brand';
import { RootNavigator } from './src/navigation/RootNavigator';

const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: { staleTime: 30_000, retry: 1, refetchOnWindowFocus: false },
  },
});

const AppInner: React.FC = () => {
  const { tokens, theme } = useTheme();
  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
    PlusJakartaSans_400Regular,
    PlusJakartaSans_500Medium,
    PlusJakartaSans_600SemiBold,
    PlusJakartaSans_700Bold,
    PlusJakartaSans_800ExtraBold,
    JetBrainsMono_400Regular,
    JetBrainsMono_500Medium,
    JetBrainsMono_700Bold,
  });

  if (!fontsLoaded) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.boot, { backgroundColor: tokens.canvasBg }]}>
        <TransitLoader width={320} message="LOADING BUSVERSE" />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <StatusBar style={theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'} />
      <NavigationContainer>
        <RootNavigator />
      </NavigationContainer>
    </>
  );
};

export default function App() {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <ThemeProvider>
        <SafeAreaProvider>
          <AuthProvider>
            <ToastProvider>
              <AppInner />
            </ToastProvider>
          </AuthProvider>
        </SafeAreaProvider>
      </ThemeProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  boot: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
});

### apps\passenger-app\src\brand\animatedSvg.ts
================================================================================
import Animated from 'react-native-reanimated';
import type { ComponentType } from 'react';
import type { ViewStyle } from 'react-native';

// react-native-svg v16 component types don't declare `style` or `animatedProps`,
// but Reanimated's createAnimatedComponent() supplies both at runtime. The CSS
// error "Failed to set indexed property [0] on CSSStyleDeclaration" on web comes
// from passing `style` to animated SVG elements — always use `animatedProps` with
// a matrix transform string instead.

export type AnimatedSvgComponent<P> = ComponentType<
  P & {
    style?: ViewStyle | ViewStyle[] | ((...args: unknown[]) => ViewStyle);
    animatedProps?: Record<string, unknown>;
  }
>;

export function makeAnimatedSvg<P>(
  Component: ComponentType<P>,
): AnimatedSvgComponent<P> {
  return Animated.createAnimatedComponent(Component) as unknown as AnimatedSvgComponent<P>;
}

// ── Transform matrix builders ──
// matrix(a, b, c, d, e, f) maps (x, y) → (a*x + c*y + e, b*x + d*y + f).
// SVG applies the matrix with transform-origin 0 0 on every platform, so
// these are portable across native (react-native-svg) and web (react-native-web).

/** Rotate by `deg` around (cx, cy), then translate the result by (X, Y). */
export function rotateMatrix(X: number, Y: number, deg: number, cx: number, cy: number): string {
  const r = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(r);
  const sin = Math.sin(r);
  const e = X - cx * cos + cy * sin;
  const f = Y - cx * sin - cy * cos;
  return `matrix(${cos},${sin},${-sin},${cos},${e},${f})`;
}

/** Scale by (sx, sy) around (cx, cy), then translate the result by (X, Y). */
export function scaleMatrix(X: number, Y: number, sx: number, sy: number, cx: number, cy: number): string {
  return `matrix(${sx},0,0,${sy},${X - cx * sx},${Y - cy * sy})`;
}

/** Translate by (tx, ty). */
export function translateMatrix(tx: number, ty: number): string {
  return `matrix(1,0,0,1,${tx},${ty})`;
}

export const BusVerseLogo: React.FC<BusVerseLogoProps> = ({ size = 120, variant = 'full' }) => {
  const { tokens } = useTheme();
  const paths = useMemo(() => PATHS, []);

  return (
    <Svg viewBox="0 0 1254 1254" width={size} height={size} fill="none">
      <G fill={tokens.logoPrimary}>
        <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.road} />
      </G>
      <G fill={tokens.logoVehicle} opacity={0.15}>
        <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.busShadow} />
      </G>
      <G fill={tokens.logoVehicle}>
        <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.busNavy} />
      </G>
      <G fill={tokens.logoPin}>
        <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.pin} />
      </G>
      {variant === 'full' && (
        <>
          <G fill={tokens.logoTypoBus}>
            <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.wordmarkBus} />
          </G>
          <G fill={tokens.logoTypoVerse}>
            <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.wordmarkVerse} />
          </G>
        </>
      )}
    </Svg>
  );
};

export default BusVerseLogo;

// matrix() = SVG attribute â†’ respects transform-origin 0 0 on every platform.
// Chain translate(X,Y) rotate(R) translate(-cx,-cy) â†’ matrix(a,b,c,d,e,f):
//   a = cos R, b = sin R, c = -sin R, d = cos R
//   e = X - cx*cosR + cy*sinR
//   f = Y - cx*sinR - cy*cosR
function rotateMatrix(X: number, Y: number, deg: number, cx: number, cy: number): string {
  const r = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(r);
  const sin = Math.sin(r);
  const a = cos;
  const b = sin;
  const c = -sin;
  const d = cos;
  const e = X - cx * cos + cy * sin;
  const f = Y - cx * sin - cy * cos;
  return `matrix(${a},${b},${c},${d},${e},${f})`;
}

// translate(X,Y) scale(sx,sy) translate(-cx,-cy) â†’ matrix(sx, 0, 0, sy, cx*(1-sx), cy*(1-sy))
function scaleMatrix(X: number, Y: number, sx: number, sy: number, cx: number, cy: number): string {
  return `matrix(${sx},0,0,${sy},${X - cx * sx},${Y - cy * sy})`;
}

export const BusVerseLogoReveal: React.FC<Props> = ({ size = 240, loop = true, onComplete }) => {
  const { tokens } = useTheme();
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const progress = useSharedValue(0);
  const paths = useMemo(() => P, []);

  // Keep onComplete in a ref so the effect doesn't restart on parent re-renders.
  const onCompleteRef = useRef(onComplete);
  useEffect(() => {
    onCompleteRef.current = onComplete;
  }, [onComplete]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.log('[reveal] effect running', { loop, reducedMotion });
    // On web, useReducedMotion() reflects OS "Reduce motion". If set, show final frame.
    if (reducedMotion) {
      progress.value = 0.86;
      return;
    }
    const duration = 6000;
    const anim = withTiming(1, { duration, easing: Easing.linear }, (finished) => {
      if (finished) {
        const cb = onCompleteRef.current;
        if (cb) runOnJS(cb)();
      }
    });
    progress.value = loop ? withRepeat(anim, -1, true) : anim;
    // Deliberately exclude onComplete from deps â€” the ref keeps it fresh.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [loop, reducedMotion]);

  // Road â€” origin (332, 640)
  const roadProps = useAnimatedProps(() => {
    const p = progress.value;
    const opacity = interpolate(p, [0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.18, 0.86, 0.93, 1], [0, 0, 0.9, 1, 1, 0, 0]);
    const sx = interpolate(p, [0, 0.02, 0.18, 0.24, 0.86, 0.93, 1], [0.05, 0.05, 1.02, 1, 1, 0.2, 0.2]);
    const sy = interpolate(p, [0, 0.02, 0.18, 0.24, 0.86, 0.93, 1], [0.4, 0.4, 1.02, 1, 1, 0.6, 0.6]);
    return { opacity, transform: scaleMatrix(332, 640, sx, sy, 332, 640) };
  });

  // Bus â€” origin (666, 728)
  const busProps = useAnimatedProps(() => {
    const p = progress.value;
    const opacity = interpolate(p, [0, 0.05, 0.09, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 0]);
    const tx = interpolate(p, [0, 0.05, 0.22, 0.27, 0.32, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [-100, -100, 6, -2, 0, 0, 50, 50]);
    const ty = interpolate(p, [0, 0.05, 0.22, 0.27, 0.32, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [-30, -30, 3, -1, 0, 0, -15, -15]);
    const rot = interpolate(p, [0, 0.05, 0.22, 0.27, 0.32, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [-1.5, -1.5, 1, -0.4, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
    return { opacity, transform: rotateMatrix(666 + tx, 728 + ty, rot, 666, 728) };
  });

  // Pin â€” origin (980, 547)
  const pinProps = useAnimatedProps(() => {
    const p = progress.value;
    const opacity = interpolate(p, [0, 0.13, 0.17, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 0]);
    const ty = interpolate(p, [0, 0.13, 0.23, 0.27, 0.31, 0.35, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [-160, -160, 4, -8, 1, 0, 0, -30, -30]);
    const sx = interpolate(p, [0, 0.13, 0.23, 0.27, 0.31, 0.35, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [0.3, 0.3, 1.12, 0.95, 1.02, 1, 1, 0.6, 0.6]);
    const sy = interpolate(p, [0, 0.13, 0.23, 0.27, 0.31, 0.35, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [0.3, 0.3, 0.88, 1.06, 0.98, 1, 1, 0.6, 0.6]);
    return { opacity, transform: scaleMatrix(980, 547 + ty, sx, sy, 980, 547) };
  });

  // Shockwave â€” origin (980, 547)
  const shockProps = useAnimatedProps(() => {
    const p = progress.value;
    const opacity = interpolate(p, [0, 0.21, 0.23, 0.36, 1], [0, 0, 0.8, 0, 0]);
    const scale = interpolate(p, [0, 0.21, 0.36, 1], [0.05, 0.05, 1, 1]);
    return { opacity, transform: scaleMatrix(980, 547, scale, scale, 980, 547) };
  });

  const makeCharProps = (delaySec: number, cx: number, cy: number) => {
    return () => {
      'worklet';
      const d = delaySec / 6.0;
      const p = progress.value;
      const opacity = interpolate(p, [0, 0.22 + d, 0.27 + d, 0.31 + d, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 0, 0]);
      const ty = interpolate(p, [0, 0.22 + d, 0.27 + d, 0.31 + d, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [40, 40, -4, 0, 0, 15, 15]);
      const scale = interpolate(p, [0, 0.22 + d, 0.27 + d, 0.31 + d, 0.86, 0.92, 1], [0.9, 0.9, 1.04, 1, 1, 1, 1]);
      return { opacity, transform: scaleMatrix(cx, cy + ty, scale, scale, cx, cy) };
    };
  };

  const c1 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.00, 185, 990));
  const c2 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.08, 335, 992));
  const c3 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.16, 454, 990));
  const c4 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.24, 600, 991));
  const c5 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.32, 716, 991));
  const c6 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.40, 833, 989));
  const c7 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.48, 940, 989));
  const c8 = useAnimatedProps(makeCharProps(0.56, 1075, 989));

  // Sheen â€” origin-free translate + rotate via matrix
  const sheenProps = useAnimatedProps(() => {
    const p = progress.value;
    const opacity = interpolate(p, [0, 0.48, 0.50, 0.63, 0.64, 1], [0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 0]);
    const tx = interpolate(p, [0, 0.48, 0.63, 1], [-1000, -1000, 1150, 1150]);
    const r = (25 * Math.PI) / 180;
    const cos = Math.cos(r);
    const sin = Math.sin(r);
    return { opacity, transform: `matrix(${cos},${sin},${-sin},${cos},${tx},0)` };
  });

  return (
    <Svg viewBox="0 0 1254 1254" width={size} height={size} fill="none">
      <Defs>
        <LinearGradient id="sheenGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#FFFFFF" stopOpacity={0} />
          <Stop offset="50%" stopColor="#FFFFFF" stopOpacity={0.8} />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#FFFFFF" stopOpacity={0} />
        </LinearGradient>
      </Defs>
      <AnimatedG animatedProps={roadProps} fill={tokens.logoPrimary}>
        <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.road} />
      </AnimatedG>
      <AnimatedG animatedProps={busProps}>
        <G fill={tokens.logoVehicle} opacity={0.15}>
          <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.busShadow} />
        </G>
        <G fill={tokens.logoVehicle}>
          <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.busNavy} />
        </G>
      </AnimatedG>
      <AnimatedEllipse cx={980} cy={547} rx={65} ry={24} fill="none" stroke={tokens.logoPrimary} strokeWidth={4} animatedProps={shockProps} />
      <AnimatedG animatedProps={pinProps} fill={tokens.logoPin}>
        <Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.pin} />
      </AnimatedG>
      <G fill={tokens.logoTypoBus}>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c1}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char1} /></AnimatedG>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c2}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char2} /></AnimatedG>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c3}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char3} /></AnimatedG>
      </G>
      <G fill={tokens.logoTypoVerse}>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c4}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char4} /></AnimatedG>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c5}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char5} /></AnimatedG>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c6}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char6} /></AnimatedG>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c7}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char7} /></AnimatedG>
        <AnimatedG animatedProps={c8}><Path fillRule="evenodd" d={paths.char8} /></AnimatedG>
      </G>
      <AnimatedRect x={200} y={100} width={300} height={1100} fill="url(#sheenGrad)" animatedProps={sheenProps} />
    </Svg>
  );
};

export default BusVerseLogoReveal;

import React, { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import Svg, {
  G, Path, Circle, Rect, Ellipse, Line, Polygon,
  LinearGradient, RadialGradient, Defs, Stop, Text as SvgText, TSpan,
} from 'react-native-svg';
import {
  useSharedValue,
  useAnimatedProps,
  withTiming,
  withRepeat,
  withDelay,
  Easing,
  interpolate,
  useReducedMotion,
} from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { makeAnimatedSvg, rotateMatrix, translateMatrix } from './animatedSvg';

type Props = {
  width?: number;
  message?: string;
  showMessage?: boolean;
};

const AnimatedG = makeAnimatedSvg(G);
const AnimatedLine = makeAnimatedSvg(Line);
const AnimatedTSpan = makeAnimatedSvg(TSpan);

const PATHS = {
  roof: 'M 290,165 Q 310,154 360,154 L 465,154 Q 505,154 520,165 Z',
  body: 'M 215,178 Q 210,178 208,186 L 204,295 Q 204,310 218,310 L 255,310 A 38,38 0 0 1 331,310 L 477,310 A 38,38 0 0 1 553,310 L 588,310 Q 605,310 605,296 L 603,260 Q 601,230 582,198 L 568,184 Q 556,178 538,178 Z',
  bodyUnder: 'M 204,290 L 204,298 Q 204,310 218,310 L 255,310 A 38,38 0 0 1 331,310 L 477,310 A 38,38 0 0 1 553,310 L 588,310 Q 605,310 605,296 L 604,290 Z',
  cyanTrim: 'M 204,286 L 254,286 A 38,38 0 0 1 332,286 L 476,286 A 38,38 0 0 1 554,286 L 602,286',
  glass: 'M 220,188 L 535,188 Q 552,188 563,200 L 579,222 Q 586,232 588,246 L 589,252 L 220,252 Z',
  glassSheen1: 'M 280,252 L 325,188 L 350,188 L 305,252 Z',
  glassSheen2: 'M 440,252 L 485,188 L 500,188 L 455,252 Z',
  wheelArch1: 'M 258,310 A 35,35 0 0 1 328,310 Z',
  wheelArch2: 'M 480,310 A 35,35 0 0 1 550,310 Z',
} as const;

export const TransitLoader: React.FC<Props> = ({ width = 320, message = 'CONNECTING TRANSIT', showMessage = true }) => {
  const { tokens } = useTheme();
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const height = width * (500 / 800);
  const paths = useMemo(() => PATHS, []);

  const chassis = useSharedValue(0);
  const wheel = useSharedValue(0);
  const dash = useSharedValue(0);
  const edge = useSharedValue(0);
  const w1 = useSharedValue(0);
  const w2 = useSharedValue(0);
  const w3 = useSharedValue(0);
  const d1 = useSharedValue(0);
  const d2 = useSharedValue(0);
  const d3 = useSharedValue(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (reducedMotion) return;
    chassis.value = withRepeat(withTiming(1, { duration: 750, easing: Easing.linear }), -1, false);
    wheel.value = withRepeat(withTiming(360, { duration: 450, easing: Easing.linear }), -1, false);
    dash.value = withRepeat(withTiming(-130, { duration: 450, easing: Easing.linear }), -1, false);
    edge.value = withRepeat(withTiming(-60, { duration: 450, easing: Easing.linear }), -1, false);

    const windE = Easing.bezier(0.4, 0, 0.6, 1);
    w1.value = withRepeat(withTiming(1, { duration: 850, easing: windE }), -1, false);
    w2.value = withDelay(280, withRepeat(withTiming(1, { duration: 850, easing: windE }), -1, false));
    w3.value = withDelay(550, withRepeat(withTiming(1, { duration: 850, easing: windE }), -1, false));

    const dotE = Easing.inOut(Easing.ease);
    d1.value = withRepeat(withTiming(1, { duration: 1200, easing: dotE }), -1, false);
    d2.value = withDelay(200, withRepeat(withTiming(1, { duration: 1200, easing: dotE }), -1, false));
    d3.value = withDelay(400, withRepeat(withTiming(1, { duration: 1200, easing: dotE }), -1, false));
  }, [reducedMotion]);

  // Chassis rig: rotate(rot) around (400, 240) + translate (0, ty)
  const chassisProps = useAnimatedProps(() => {
    const v = chassis.value;
    const ty = interpolate(v, [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1], [0, -3, 1, -1.5, 0]);
    const rot = interpolate(v, [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1], [0, -0.4, 0.2, -0.1, 0]);
    return { transform: rotateMatrix(400, 240 + ty, rot, 400, 240) };
  });

  // Wheels: rotate around (293, 310) and (515, 310)
  const wheel1Props = useAnimatedProps(() => ({
    transform: rotateMatrix(293, 310, wheel.value, 293, 310),
  }));
  const wheel2Props = useAnimatedProps(() => ({
    transform: rotateMatrix(515, 310, wheel.value, 515, 310),
  }));

  // Wind: translate tx + fade opacity
  const makeWindProps = (val: typeof w1) => () => {
    'worklet';
    const tx = interpolate(val.value, [0, 1], [260, -420]);
    const op = interpolate(val.value, [0, 0.25, 0.8, 1], [0, 0.75, 0.6, 0]);
    return { opacity: op, transform: translateMatrix(tx, 0) };
  };
  const wind1Props = useAnimatedProps(makeWindProps(w1));
  const wind2Props = useAnimatedProps(makeWindProps(w2));
  const wind3Props = useAnimatedProps(makeWindProps(w3));

  // Dots: opacity only — TSpan transform support varies across SVG impls
  const makeDotProps = (val: typeof d1) => () => {
    'worklet';
    const op = interpolate(val.value, [0, 0.4, 0.8, 1], [0.2, 1, 0.2, 0.2]);
    return { opacity: op };
  };
  const dot1Props = useAnimatedProps(makeDotProps(d1));
  const dot2Props = useAnimatedProps(makeDotProps(d2));
  const dot3Props = useAnimatedProps(makeDotProps(d3));

  // Road dash + edge: strokeDashoffset is already a valid animated prop
  const dashProps = useAnimatedProps(() => ({ strokeDashoffset: dash.value }));
  const edgeProps = useAnimatedProps(() => ({ strokeDashoffset: edge.value }));

  return (
    <Svg viewBox="0 0 800 500" width={width} height={height} fill="none">
      <Defs>
        <LinearGradient id="bodyGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#003BB5" />
          <Stop offset="50%" stopColor="#0055ED" />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#1E70FF" />
        </LinearGradient>
        <LinearGradient id="roofGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#0B1830" />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#050C1A" />
        </LinearGradient>
        <LinearGradient id="glassGrad" x1="20%" y1="0%" x2="80%" y2="100%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#1E3050" />
          <Stop offset="50%" stopColor="#0E192D" />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#08101E" />
        </LinearGradient>
        <LinearGradient id="glassSheen" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#00F0FF" stopOpacity={0} />
          <Stop offset="50%" stopColor="#00F0FF" stopOpacity={0.35} />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#FFFFFF" stopOpacity={0} />
        </LinearGradient>
        <LinearGradient id="headlightBeam" x1="0%" y1="50%" x2="100%" y2="50%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#00E5FF" stopOpacity={0.8} />
          <Stop offset="30%" stopColor="#00E5FF" stopOpacity={0.25} />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#38BDF8" stopOpacity={0} />
        </LinearGradient>
        <RadialGradient id="underglowGrad" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="50%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#00E5FF" stopOpacity={0.6} />
          <Stop offset="60%" stopColor="#0055ED" stopOpacity={0.2} />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#0055ED" stopOpacity={0} />
        </RadialGradient>
        <RadialGradient id="taillightGlow" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="50%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#FF2A55" stopOpacity={0.9} />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#FF2A55" stopOpacity={0} />
        </RadialGradient>
        <LinearGradient id="rimGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
          <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#182538" />
          <Stop offset="50%" stopColor="#0B121E" />
          <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#24344D" />
        </LinearGradient>
      </Defs>

      {/* Wind streaks */}
      <G stroke={tokens.telemetryCyan} opacity={0.28} strokeWidth={2} strokeLinecap="round">
        <AnimatedLine x1={560} y1={135} x2={680} y2={135} strokeDasharray={[70, 50]} animatedProps={wind1Props} />
        <AnimatedLine x1={500} y1={168} x2={660} y2={168} strokeDasharray={[90, 60]} animatedProps={wind2Props} />
        <AnimatedLine x1={540} y1={305} x2={700} y2={305} strokeDasharray={[60, 40]} animatedProps={wind3Props} />
      </G>

      {/* Ground + road */}
      <Rect x={0} y={340} width={800} height={4} fill={tokens.textMuted} opacity={0.4} />
      <AnimatedLine x1={-100} y1={358} x2={900} y2={358} stroke="#0055ED" strokeWidth={4} strokeLinecap="round" opacity={0.85} strokeDasharray={[60, 70]} animatedProps={dashProps} />
      <AnimatedLine x1={-100} y1={372} x2={900} y2={372} stroke="#00E5FF" strokeWidth={1.5} strokeLinecap="round" opacity={0.35} strokeDasharray={[25, 35]} animatedProps={edgeProps} />

      {/* Underglow + lighting */}
      <Ellipse cx={400} cy={336} rx={210} ry={14} fill="url(#underglowGrad)" opacity={0.5} />
      <Ellipse cx={205} cy={275} rx={40} ry={18} fill="url(#taillightGlow)" opacity={0.7} />
      <Polygon points="596,275 800,225 800,352 602,305" fill="url(#headlightBeam)" opacity={0.6} />

      {/* Bus chassis rig */}
      <AnimatedG animatedProps={chassisProps}>
        <Path d={paths.roof} fill="url(#roofGrad)" />
        <Rect x={340} y={159} width={30} height={3} rx={1.5} fill="#1C2E4A" />
        <Rect x={385} y={159} width={30} height={3} rx={1.5} fill="#1C2E4A" />
        <Rect x={430} y={159} width={30} height={3} rx={1.5} fill="#1C2E4A" />
        <Path d={paths.body} fill="url(#bodyGrad)" />
        <Path d={paths.bodyUnder} fill="#0B1830" />
        <Path d={paths.cyanTrim} fill="none" stroke="#00F0FF" strokeWidth={2} opacity={0.85} />
        <Path d={paths.glass} fill="url(#glassGrad)" stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth={2} />
        <Path d={paths.glassSheen1} fill="url(#glassSheen)" />
        <Path d={paths.glassSheen2} fill="url(#glassSheen)" opacity={0.5} />
        <Line x1={275} y1={188} x2={275} y2={252} stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth={3} />
        <Line x1={340} y1={188} x2={340} y2={252} stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth={3} />
        <Line x1={410} y1={188} x2={410} y2={252} stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth={3} />
        <Line x1={480} y1={188} x2={480} y2={252} stroke="#0B1830" strokeWidth={3} />
        <Ellipse cx={600} cy={276} rx={4} ry={5} fill="#00E5FF" />
        <Rect x={202} y={262} width={3} height={20} rx={1.5} fill="#FFA3B2" />

        <G>
          <Path d={paths.wheelArch1} fill="#050912" />
          <Circle cx={293} cy={310} r={28} fill="#111824" stroke="#070C14" strokeWidth={2} />
          <AnimatedG animatedProps={wheel1Props}>
            <Circle cx={293} cy={310} r={19} fill="url(#rimGrad)" />
            <Circle cx={293} cy={310} r={13} fill="none" stroke="#00E5FF" strokeWidth={2} strokeDasharray={[6, 7]} />
            <Circle cx={293} cy={310} r={5} fill="#0055ED" />
          </AnimatedG>
        </G>
        <G>
          <Path d={paths.wheelArch2} fill="#050912" />
          <Circle cx={515} cy={310} r={28} fill="#111824" stroke="#070C14" strokeWidth={2} />
          <AnimatedG animatedProps={wheel2Props}>
            <Circle cx={515} cy={310} r={19} fill="url(#rimGrad)" />
            <Circle cx={515} cy={310} r={13} fill="none" stroke="#00E5FF" strokeWidth={2} strokeDasharray={[6, 7]} />
            <Circle cx={515} cy={310} r={5} fill="#0055ED" />
          </AnimatedG>
        </G>
      </AnimatedG>

      {/* Status message */}
      {showMessage && (
        <G transform="translate(400, 435)">
          <SvgText fill={tokens.textMuted} textAnchor="middle" fontFamily="System" fontSize={14} fontWeight="700" letterSpacing={6}>
            {message}
            <AnimatedTSpan animatedProps={dot1Props}>.</AnimatedTSpan>
            <AnimatedTSpan animatedProps={dot2Props}>.</AnimatedTSpan>
            <AnimatedTSpan animatedProps={dot3Props}>.</AnimatedTSpan>
          </SvgText>
        </G>
      )}
    </Svg>
  );
};

export default TransitLoader;

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\layout\AppHeader.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';
import { BusVerseLogo } from '../../brand';
import { Avatar } from '../ui/Avatar';

type Props = {
  title?: string;
  showLogo?: boolean;
  userName?: string | null;
  onAvatarPress?: () => void;
  onBackPress?: () => void;
  rightSlot?: React.ReactNode;
};

export const AppHeader: React.FC<Props> = ({
  title,
  showLogo = true,
  userName,
  onAvatarPress,
  onBackPress,
  rightSlot,
}) => {
  const { tokens, typography } = useTheme();
  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.wrap,
        {
          backgroundColor: tokens.canvasSubtle,
          borderBottomColor: tokens.borderSubtle,
          borderBottomWidth: 1,
        },
      ]}
    >
      <View style={styles.left}>
        {onBackPress ? (
          <Pressable
            onPress={onBackPress}
            style={styles.iconBtn}
            accessibilityLabel="Back"
            accessibilityRole="button"
          >
            <Text style={{ color: tokens.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: '300' }}>
              {'<'}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        ) : showLogo ? (
          <BusVerseLogo size={28} variant="icon" />
        ) : null}
        {title && (
          <Text
            style={[
              typography.h4,
              {
                color: tokens.textBright,
                marginLeft: showLogo && !onBackPress ? 10 : 8,
              },
            ]}
            numberOfLines={1}
          >
            {title}
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>
      <View style={styles.right}>
        {rightSlot}
        {onAvatarPress && (
          <Pressable
            onPress={onAvatarPress}
            style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}
            accessibilityLabel="Account"
            accessibilityRole="button"
          >
            <Avatar name={userName} size={32} />
          </Pressable>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: {
    height: 56,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
  },
  left: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', flex: 1 },
  right: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' },
  iconBtn: { width: 32, height: 32, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
});

export default AppHeader;

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\layout\ScreenContainer.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { ScrollView, View, StyleSheet, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

type Props = {
  scroll?: boolean;
  padded?: boolean;
  style?: ViewStyle;
  children: React.ReactNode;
};

export const ScreenContainer: React.FC<Props> = ({ scroll = false, padded = true, style, children }) => {
  const { tokens, spacing } = useTheme();
  const inner: ViewStyle = {
    paddingHorizontal: padded ? spacing.md : 0,
    paddingTop: padded ? spacing.md : 0,
    paddingBottom: padded ? spacing.xl : 0,
    ...(style ?? {}),
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: tokens.canvasBg }]} edges={['top', 'bottom']}>
      {scroll ? (
        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={inner} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
          {children}
        </ScrollView>
      ) : (
        <View style={[styles.fill, inner]}>{children}</View>
      )}
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safe: { flex: 1 },
  fill: { flex: 1 },
});

export default ScreenContainer;

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Avatar.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

type Props = { name?: string | null; size?: number };

function initials(name?: string | null): string {
  if (!name) return '?';
  const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/);
  if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  return (parts[0][0] + parts[parts.length - 1][0]).toUpperCase();
}

export const Avatar: React.FC<Props> = ({ name, size = 36 }) => {
  const { tokens, typography } = useTheme();
  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.wrap,
        {
          width: size,
          height: size,
          borderRadius: size / 2,
          backgroundColor: tokens.brandPrimarySubtle,
          borderColor: tokens.brandPrimaryBorder,
          borderWidth: 1,
        },
      ]}
    >
      <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.brandPrimary, fontSize: size * 0.38, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
        {initials(name)}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
});

export default Avatar;

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Badge.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

export type BadgeVariant = 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red' | 'slate' | 'cyan' | 'brand';

type Props = { variant?: BadgeVariant; children: React.ReactNode };

export const Badge: React.FC<Props> = ({ variant = 'slate', children }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const palette: Record<BadgeVariant, { bg: string; fg: string }> = {
    emerald: { bg: tokens.signalEmeraldSubtle, fg: tokens.signalEmerald },
    amber:   { bg: tokens.warningAmberSubtle, fg: tokens.warningAmber },
    red:     { bg: tokens.dangerCrimsonSubtle, fg: tokens.dangerCrimson },
    slate:   { bg: tokens.surfaceSunken, fg: tokens.textSecondary },
    cyan:    { bg: tokens.telemetryCyanSubtle, fg: tokens.telemetryCyan },
    brand:   { bg: tokens.brandPrimarySubtle, fg: tokens.brandPrimary },
  };
  const c = palette[variant];
  return (
    <View style={[styles.wrap, { backgroundColor: c.bg, borderRadius: radius.sm }]}>
      <Text style={[typography.monoSmall, { color: c.fg, fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 0.5 }]}>
        {typeof children === 'string' ? children.toUpperCase() : children}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 3, alignSelf: 'flex-start' },
});

export default Badge;

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Banner.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

export type BannerType = 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error';

type Props = {
  type?: BannerType;
  message: string;
  action?: { label: string; onPress: () => void };
  onDismiss?: () => void;
};

export const Banner: React.FC<Props> = ({ type = 'info', message, action, onDismiss }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const palette: Record<BannerType, { bg: string; fg: string; icon: string }> = {
    info:    { bg: tokens.telemetryCyanSubtle, fg: tokens.telemetryCyan, icon: 'i' },
    success: { bg: tokens.signalEmeraldSubtle, fg: tokens.signalEmerald, icon: 'v' },
    warning: { bg: tokens.warningAmberSubtle, fg: tokens.warningAmber, icon: '!' },
    error:   { bg: tokens.dangerCrimsonSubtle, fg: tokens.dangerCrimson, icon: 'x' },
  };
  const p = palette[type];
  return (
    <View
      style={[styles.wrap, { backgroundColor: p.bg, borderRadius: radius.md }]}
      accessibilityRole="alert"
    >
      <View style={[styles.iconCircle, { backgroundColor: p.fg }]}>
        <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{p.icon}</Text>
      </View>
      <Text
        style={[
          typography.body,
          { color: tokens.textPrimary, flex: 1, fontSize: 13, marginLeft: 10 },
        ]}
      >
        {message}
      </Text>
      {action && (
        <Pressable onPress={action.onPress} style={{ paddingLeft: 12 }}>
          <Text style={[typography.label, { color: p.fg, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
            {action.label}
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
      )}
      {onDismiss && (
        <Pressable onPress={onDismiss} style={{ paddingLeft: 8 }}>
          <Text style={{ color: tokens.textMuted, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '600' }}>
            {'x'}
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 12,
  },
  iconCircle: {
    width: 20,
    height: 20,
    borderRadius: 10,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
});

export default Banner;

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Button.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, View, Text, StyleSheet, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';
import { Spinner } from './Spinner';

export type ButtonVariant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'danger';
export type ButtonSize = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';

type Props = {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  fullWidth?: boolean;
  leftIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  rightIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  onPress?: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
};

const HEIGHTS: Record<ButtonSize, number> = { sm: 36, md: 44, lg: 52 };
const PADDING: Record<ButtonSize, number> = { sm: 14, md: 20, lg: 24 };
const FONT_SIZES: Record<ButtonSize, number> = { sm: 12, md: 14, lg: 15 };

export const Button: React.FC<Props> = ({
  variant = 'primary',
  size = 'md',
  loading = false,
  disabled = false,
  fullWidth = false,
  leftIcon,
  rightIcon,
  onPress,
  children,
}) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const isDisabled = disabled || loading;

  const palette: Record<ButtonVariant, { bg: string; fg: string; border: string; pressBg: string }> = {
    primary:   { bg: tokens.brandPrimary, fg: '#FFFFFF', border: tokens.brandPrimary, pressBg: tokens.brandPrimaryHover },
    secondary: { bg: tokens.surfaceCard, fg: tokens.textPrimary, border: tokens.borderSubtle, pressBg: tokens.surfaceHover },
    ghost:     { bg: 'transparent', fg: tokens.textPrimary, border: 'transparent', pressBg: tokens.surfaceHover },
    danger:    { bg: tokens.dangerCrimson, fg: '#FFFFFF', border: tokens.dangerCrimson, pressBg: tokens.dangerCrimson },
  };
  const c = palette[variant];

  const style: ViewStyle = {
    height: HEIGHTS[size],
    paddingHorizontal: PADDING[size],
    backgroundColor: c.bg,
    borderColor: c.border,
    borderWidth: variant === 'secondary' ? 1 : 0,
    borderRadius: radius.md,
    opacity: isDisabled ? 0.5 : 1,
    alignSelf: fullWidth ? 'stretch' : 'flex-start',
  };

  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={isDisabled ? undefined : onPress}
      disabled={isDisabled}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.base,
        style,
        pressed && !isDisabled ? { backgroundColor: c.pressBg } : null,
      ]}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityState={{ disabled: isDisabled, busy: loading }}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <Spinner size={size === 'lg' ? 'md' : 'sm'} color={c.fg} />
      ) : (
        <>
          {leftIcon && <View style={styles.icon}>{leftIcon}</View>}
          <Text style={[typography.label, { color: c.fg, fontSize: FONT_SIZES[size], fontWeight: '700' }]}>
            {children}
          </Text>
          {rightIcon && <View style={styles.icon}>{rightIcon}</View>}
        </>
      )}
    </Pressable>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  base: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    gap: 8,
  },
  icon: { marginHorizontal: 2 },
});

export default Button;

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Card.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, View, StyleSheet, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

type Props = {
  elevation?: 'flat' | 'elevated';
  padding?: number;
  onPress?: () => void;
  style?: ViewStyle;
  children: React.ReactNode;
};

export const Card: React.FC<Props> = ({ elevation = 'flat', padding, onPress, style, children }) => {
  const { tokens, radius, spacing } = useTheme();
  const elevationStyle = elevation === 'elevated' ? tokens.shadowFloating : tokens.shadowAmbient;
  const baseStyle: ViewStyle = {
    backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceCard,
    borderRadius: radius.lg,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: tokens.borderHairline,
    padding: padding ?? spacing.lg,
    ...elevationStyle,
    ...(style ?? {}),
  };

  if (onPress) {
    return (
      <Pressable
        onPress={onPress}
        style={({ pressed }) => [
          baseStyle,
          pressed ? { backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceHover } : null,
        ]}
        accessibilityRole="button"
      >
        {children}
      </Pressable>
    );
  }
  return <View style={baseStyle}>{children}</View>;
};

export default Card;

import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, Text } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

type Props = {
  label: string;
  selected?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onPress?: () => void;
};

export const Chip: React.FC<Props> = ({ label, selected = false, disabled = false, onPress }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityState={{ selected, disabled }}
      disabled={disabled}
      onPress={onPress}
      style={{
        paddingVertical: 8,
        paddingHorizontal: 14,
        borderRadius: radius.pill,
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderColor: selected ? tokens.brandPrimary : tokens.borderSubtle,
        backgroundColor: selected ? tokens.brandPrimarySubtle : tokens.surfaceCard,
        opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1,
      }}
    >
      <Text style={[typography.label, { color: selected ? tokens.brandPrimary : tokens.textSecondary }]}>{label}</Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
};

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Input.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, StyleSheet, KeyboardTypeOptions } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

type Props = {
  label?: string;
  value: string;
  onChangeText: (v: string) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  error?: string;
  hint?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  keyboardType?: KeyboardTypeOptions;
  autoCapitalize?: 'none' | 'sentences' | 'words' | 'characters';
  secureTextEntry?: boolean;
  maxLength?: number;
  leftIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  rightIcon?: React.ReactNode;
};

export const Input: React.FC<Props> = ({
  label,
  value,
  onChangeText,
  placeholder,
  error,
  hint,
  disabled = false,
  keyboardType,
  autoCapitalize = 'sentences',
  secureTextEntry,
  maxLength,
  leftIcon,
  rightIcon,
}) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);

  const borderColor = error
    ? tokens.dangerCrimson
    : focused
    ? tokens.borderFocus
    : tokens.borderSubtle;

  return (
    <View style={styles.wrap}>
      {label && (
        <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 6 }]}>
          {label}
        </Text>
      )}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.field,
          {
            borderColor,
            borderWidth: focused ? 2 : 1,
            backgroundColor: disabled ? tokens.surfaceSunken : tokens.surfaceCard,
            borderRadius: radius.sm,
          },
        ]}
      >
        {leftIcon && <View style={styles.icon}>{leftIcon}</View>}
        <TextInput
          value={value}
          onChangeText={onChangeText}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          placeholderTextColor={tokens.textMuted}
          editable={!disabled}
          keyboardType={keyboardType}
          autoCapitalize={autoCapitalize}
          secureTextEntry={secureTextEntry}
          maxLength={maxLength}
          onFocus={() => setFocused(true)}
          onBlur={() => setFocused(false)}
          style={[
            typography.body,
            {
              flex: 1,
              color: tokens.textPrimary,
              paddingVertical: 10,
              paddingHorizontal: 12,
              outlineWidth: 0,
            },
          ]}
          accessibilityLabel={label}
        />
        {rightIcon && <View style={styles.icon}>{rightIcon}</View>}
      </View>
      {(error || hint) && (
        <Text
          style={[
            typography.caption,
            {
              color: error ? tokens.dangerCrimson : tokens.textMuted,
              marginTop: 4,
            },
          ]}
          accessibilityLiveRegion="polite"
        >
          {error ?? hint}
        </Text>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { width: '100%' },
  field: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    minHeight: 44,
  },
  icon: { paddingHorizontal: 10 },
});

export default Input;

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Modal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

// Conditional module loading — RNModal only used on native, createPortal only on web.
let RNModal: React.ComponentType<any> | null = null;
let createPortal: ((children: React.ReactNode, container: Element) => React.ReactPortal) | null = null;

if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
  try {
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    createPortal = require('react-dom').createPortal;
  } catch {
    createPortal = null;
  }
} else {
  try {
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    RNModal = require('react-native').Modal;
  } catch {
    RNModal = null;
  }
}

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
};

// Web needs position:fixed to cover the viewport regardless of scroll.
// Native uses absolute fill inside a full-screen modal.
const OVERLAY_STYLE: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web'
    ? { position: 'fixed', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, zIndex: 1000 }
    : { ...StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject, zIndex: 1000 };

const Content: React.FC<{
  title?: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}> = ({ title, onClose, children }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius, spacing } = useTheme();
  return (
    <View style={OVERLAY_STYLE}>
      <Pressable
        style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)' }]}
        onPress={onClose}
      />
      <View
        style={[
          StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject,
          { alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: 20 },
        ]}
        pointerEvents="box-none"
      >
        <View
          style={{
            width: '100%',
            maxWidth: 480,
            backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceCard,
            borderWidth: 1,
            borderColor: tokens.borderStrong,
            borderRadius: radius.lg,
            padding: spacing.lg,
          }}
        >
          {title && (
            <View style={{ marginBottom: spacing.md }}>
              <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{title}</Text>
            </View>
          )}
          {children}
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

export const Modal: React.FC<Props> = ({ open, onClose, title, children }) => {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  if (!open) return null;

  // Web: portal into document.body with fixed overlay (bypasses RNModal entirely)
  if (
    Platform.OS === 'web' &&
    mounted &&
    typeof document !== 'undefined' &&
    createPortal
  ) {
    return createPortal(
      <Content title={title} onClose={onClose}>
        {children}
      </Content>,
      document.body,
    );
  }

  // Native: standard RNModal
  if (RNModal) {
    return (
      <RNModal visible transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={onClose}>
        <Content title={title} onClose={onClose}>
          {children}
        </Content>
      </RNModal>
    );
  }

  // Fallback (should never happen)
  return (
    <Content title={title} onClose={onClose}>
      {children}
    </Content>
  );
};

export default Modal;

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Select.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';
import { Modal } from './Modal';

export type SelectOption = { value: string; label: string };

type Props = {
  label?: string;
  value: string | null;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  options: SelectOption[];
  placeholder?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
};

export const Select: React.FC<Props> = ({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  placeholder = 'Select...',
  disabled,
}) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const selected = options.find((o) => o.value === value);

  return (
    <View style={{ width: '100%' }}>
      {label && (
        <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 6 }]}>
          {label}
        </Text>
      )}
      <Pressable
        onPress={() => !disabled && setOpen(true)}
        style={[
          styles.field,
          {
            borderColor: tokens.borderSubtle,
            borderWidth: 1,
            borderRadius: radius.sm,
            backgroundColor: disabled ? tokens.surfaceSunken : tokens.surfaceCard,
            opacity: disabled ? 0.6 : 1,
          },
        ]}
        accessibilityRole="button"
      >
        <Text
          style={[
            typography.body,
            {
              flex: 1,
              color: selected ? tokens.textPrimary : tokens.textMuted,
              paddingVertical: 10,
              paddingHorizontal: 12,
            },
          ]}
        >
          {selected?.label ?? placeholder}
        </Text>
        <View
          style={{
            width: 0,
            height: 0,
            borderLeftWidth: 5,
            borderRightWidth: 5,
            borderTopWidth: 6,
            borderLeftColor: 'transparent',
            borderRightColor: 'transparent',
            borderTopColor: tokens.textMuted,
            marginRight: 12,
          }}
        />
      </Pressable>

      <Modal open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title={label}>
        <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 400 }}>
          {options.map((o) => {
            const isSelected = o.value === value;
            return (
              <Pressable
                key={o.value}
                onPress={() => {
                  onChange(o.value);
                  setOpen(false);
                }}
                style={[
                  styles.option,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: isSelected ? tokens.brandPrimarySubtle : 'transparent',
                    borderRadius: radius.sm,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    typography.body,
                    { color: isSelected ? tokens.brandPrimary : tokens.textPrimary },
                  ]}
                >
                  {o.label}
                </Text>
              </Pressable>
            );
          })}
        </ScrollView>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  field: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', minHeight: 44 },
  option: { paddingVertical: 12, paddingHorizontal: 12 },
});

export default Select;

### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Skeleton.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import Animated, {
  useSharedValue,
  useAnimatedStyle,
  withRepeat,
  withTiming,
  Easing,
  interpolate,
} from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

type Props = { width?: number | string; height?: number; borderRadius?: number };

export const Skeleton: React.FC<Props> = ({ width = '100%', height = 16, borderRadius }) => {
  const { tokens, radius } = useTheme();
  const progress = useSharedValue(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    progress.value = withRepeat(
      withTiming(1, { duration: 1500, easing: Easing.linear }),
      -1,
      false,
    );
  }, []);

  const animStyle = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const opacity = interpolate(progress.value, [0, 0.5, 1], [0.35, 0.75, 0.35]);
    return { opacity };
  });

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={[
        {
          width: width as number | string,
          height,
          backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceSunken,
          borderRadius: borderRadius ?? radius.sm,
        },
        animStyle,
      ]}
    />
  );
};

export default Skeleton;

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\components\ui\Toast.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Pressable } from 'react-native';
import Animated, { FadeInDown, FadeOutUp } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { useTheme } from '../../theme';

export type ToastType = 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error';

type ToastEntry = { id: number; message: string; type: ToastType; durationMs: number };

type ToastContextValue = {
  show: (message: string, type?: ToastType, durationMs?: number) => void;
};

const ToastContext = createContext<ToastContextValue | undefined>(undefined);
let nextId = 1;

export const ToastProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastEntry[]>([]);
  const { tokens, typography, radius, spacing } = useTheme();

  const show = useCallback(
    (message: string, type: ToastType = 'info', durationMs = 4000) => {
      const id = nextId++;
      setToasts((prev) => [...prev.slice(-2), { id, message, type, durationMs }]);
    },
    [],
  );

  const dismiss = useCallback((id: number) => {
    setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timers = toasts.map((t) => setTimeout(() => dismiss(t.id), t.durationMs));
    return () => timers.forEach(clearTimeout);
  }, [toasts, dismiss]);

  const palettes: Record<ToastType, { bg: string; fg: string; icon: string }> = {
    info:    { bg: tokens.telemetryCyanSubtle, fg: tokens.telemetryCyan, icon: 'i' },
    success: { bg: tokens.signalEmeraldSubtle, fg: tokens.signalEmerald, icon: 'v' },
    warning: { bg: tokens.warningAmberSubtle, fg: tokens.warningAmber, icon: '!' },
    error:   { bg: tokens.dangerCrimsonSubtle, fg: tokens.dangerCrimson, icon: 'x' },
  };

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ show }}>
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        {children}
        <View pointerEvents="box-none" style={styles.host}>
          {toasts.map((t) => {
            const p = palettes[t.type];
            return (
              <Animated.View
                key={t.id}
                entering={FadeInDown.duration(200)}
                exiting={FadeOutUp.duration(150)}
                style={[
                  styles.toast,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceCard,
                    borderColor: p.fg,
                    borderRadius: radius.md,
                    marginBottom: spacing.xs,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={[styles.iconCircle, { backgroundColor: p.fg }]}>
                  <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>
                    {p.icon}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <Text
                  style={[
                    typography.body,
                    {
                      color: tokens.textPrimary,
                      flex: 1,
                      fontSize: 13,
                      marginLeft: 10,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  {t.message}
                </Text>
                <Pressable onPress={() => dismiss(t.id)} style={{ paddingLeft: 8 }}>
                  <Text style={{ color: tokens.textMuted, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '600' }}>
                    {'x'}
                  </Text>
                </Pressable>
              </Animated.View>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      </View>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useToast(): ToastContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useToast must be used within ToastProvider');
  return ctx;
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  host: {
    position: 'absolute',
    bottom: 24,
    left: 16,
    right: 16,
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  toast: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    maxWidth: 480,
    width: '100%',
  },
  iconCircle: {
    width: 20,
    height: 20,
    borderRadius: 10,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
});

export default ToastProvider;

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\auth\api\auth.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api, EP } from '../../../lib/api';
import type { LoginResult, PassengerMeDTO } from '../../../types';

export type OtpIntent = 'LOGIN' | 'SIGNUP';

export interface OtpRequestPayload {
  phone?: string;
  email?: string;
  intent: OtpIntent;
}

export interface OtpVerifyPayload {
  phone?: string;
  email?: string;
  otp: string;
}

export interface OtpRequestResponse {
  expiresInSeconds: number;
}

export async function requestOtp(payload: OtpRequestPayload): Promise<OtpRequestResponse> {
  return api.post<OtpRequestResponse>(EP.otpRequest, payload);
}

export async function verifyOtp(payload: OtpVerifyPayload): Promise<LoginResult> {
  return api.post<LoginResult>(EP.otpVerify, payload);
}

export async function getMe(): Promise<PassengerMeDTO> {
  return api.get<PassengerMeDTO>(EP.me);
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\auth\components\AuthModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { Modal, Button, Input, Spinner } from '../../../components/ui';
import { formatPhone } from '../../../lib/format';
import { useOtpFlow } from '../hooks/useOtpFlow';

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess?: () => void;
};

const OtpField: React.FC<{
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  error?: string | null;
}> = ({ value, onChange, error }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  return (
    <View>
      <TextInput
        value={value}
        onChangeText={(v) => onChange(v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6))}
        keyboardType="number-pad"
        maxLength={6}
        autoFocus
        style={{
          fontSize: 28,
          fontWeight: '700',
          letterSpacing: 16,
          textAlign: 'center',
          fontFamily: 'monospace',
          color: tokens.textPrimary,
          backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceCard,
          borderWidth: error ? 2 : 1,
          borderColor: error ? tokens.dangerCrimson : tokens.borderSubtle,
          borderRadius: radius.sm,
          paddingVertical: 14,
          paddingHorizontal: 8,
        }}
        placeholder="------"
        placeholderTextColor={tokens.textMuted}
        accessibilityLabel="6-digit verification code"
      />
      {error && (
        <Text
          style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.dangerCrimson, marginTop: 4 }]}
          accessibilityLiveRegion="polite"
        >
          {error}
        </Text>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

export const AuthModal: React.FC<Props> = ({ open, onClose, onSuccess }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const flow = useOtpFlow();

  // On success, close after brief delay
  useEffect(() => {
    if (flow.step === 'success') {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        onSuccess?.();
        onClose();
        flow.reset();
      }, 800);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [flow.step, onSuccess, onClose, flow]);

  // Reset when closed from non-phone step
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open && flow.step !== 'phone') {
      flow.reset();
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [open]);

  const title = flow.step === 'otp' ? 'Enter your code' : 'Sign in';

  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title={title}>
      {flow.step === 'phone' && (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
          <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>
            We will text you a 6-digit code to verify your number.
          </Text>
          <Input
            label="Phone number"
            value={flow.phone}
            onChangeText={flow.setPhone}
            placeholder="98765 43210"
            keyboardType="phone-pad"
            maxLength={10}
            error={flow.error ?? undefined}
            hint="Indian mobile number, 10 digits"
          />
          <Button
            onPress={() => {
              void flow.requestCode();
            }}
            loading={flow.loading}
            disabled={flow.loading || flow.phone.length !== 10}
            fullWidth
          >
            Send code
          </Button>
        </View>
      )}

      {flow.step === 'otp' && (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
          <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>
            Enter the 6-digit code sent to {formatPhone(flow.phone)}
          </Text>
          <OtpField value={flow.otp} onChange={flow.setOtp} error={flow.error} />
          <Button
            onPress={() => {
              void flow.verify();
            }}
            loading={flow.loading}
            disabled={flow.loading || flow.otp.length !== 6}
            fullWidth
          >
            Verify
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            onPress={() => {
              void flow.resend();
            }}
            disabled={flow.resendIn > 0 || flow.loading}
            fullWidth
          >
            {flow.resendIn > 0 ? `Resend in ${flow.resendIn}s` : 'Resend code'}
          </Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" onPress={flow.reset} fullWidth>
            Change number
          </Button>
          {process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DEV_MODE === 'true' && (
            <Text
              style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, textAlign: 'center' }]}
            >
              Dev: if MOCK_OTP=true on API server, use 123456
            </Text>
          )}
        </View>
      )}

      {flow.step === 'success' && (
        <View style={{ paddingVertical: spacing.lg, alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.md }}>
          <Spinner size="lg" />
          <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>
            Signing you in...
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({});

export default AuthModal;

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { Modal, Button, Avatar } from '../../../components/ui';
import { useAuth } from '../../../lib/auth';
import { formatPhone } from '../../../lib/format';

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
};

export const ProfileMenuModal: React.FC<Props> = ({ open, onClose }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout();
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Account">
      {user ? (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: spacing.sm }}>
            <Avatar name={user.name ?? user.phone} size={64} />
            <Text
              style={[
                typography.h4,
                { color: tokens.textBright, marginTop: spacing.sm },
              ]}
            >
              {user.name ?? 'Passenger'}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }]}
            >
              {formatPhone(user.phone)}
            </Text>
            {user.email && (
              <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>
                {user.email}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
          <Button variant="danger" onPress={handleLogout} fullWidth>
            Log out
          </Button>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>
          Not signed in.
        </Text>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default ProfileMenuModal;

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\auth\hooks\useOtpFlow.ts
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { requestOtp, verifyOtp } from '../api/auth.api';
import { useAuth } from '../../../lib/auth';
import { useToast } from '../../../components/ui';
import { phoneToE164 } from '../../../lib/format';
import { ApiError } from '../../../lib/api';

export type OtpStep = 'phone' | 'otp' | 'success';

const RESEND_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 30;

export interface UseOtpFlowResult {
  step: OtpStep;
  phone: string;
  otp: string;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  resendIn: number;
  setPhone: (v: string) => void;
  setOtp: (v: string) => void;
  requestCode: () => Promise<boolean>;
  verify: () => Promise<boolean>;
  resend: () => Promise<boolean>;
  reset: () => void;
}

export function useOtpFlow(): UseOtpFlowResult {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<OtpStep>('phone');
  const [phone, setPhoneState] = useState('');
  const [otp, setOtpState] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resendIn, setResendIn] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (resendIn <= 0) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setResendIn((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1)), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [resendIn]);

  const setPhone = useCallback((v: string) => {
    setPhoneState(v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 10));
    if (error) setError(null);
  }, [error]);

  const setOtp = useCallback((v: string) => {
    setOtpState(v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6));
    if (error) setError(null);
  }, [error]);

  const requestCode = useCallback(async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    if (phone.length !== 10) {
      setError('Enter a 10-digit mobile number');
      return false;
    }
    const e164 = phoneToE164(phone);
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await requestOtp({ phone: e164, intent: 'LOGIN' });
      setStep('otp');
      setResendIn(RESEND_COOLDOWN_SECONDS);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      const err = e instanceof ApiError ? e : null;
      setError(err?.message ?? 'Could not send code. Try again.');
      return false;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [phone]);

  const verify = useCallback(async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    if (otp.length !== 6) {
      setError('Enter the 6-digit code');
      return false;
    }
    const e164 = phoneToE164(phone);
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await verifyOtp({ phone: e164, otp });
      await login(result);
      setStep('success');
      toast.show('Signed in', 'success', 2000);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      const err = e instanceof ApiError ? e : null;
      setError(err?.message ?? 'Invalid code. Try again.');
      return false;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [phone, otp, login, toast]);

  const resend = useCallback(async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    if (resendIn > 0 || loading) return false;
    const e164 = phoneToE164(phone);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await requestOtp({ phone: e164, intent: 'LOGIN' });
      setResendIn(RESEND_COOLDOWN_SECONDS);
      toast.show('New code sent', 'success', 2000);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      const err = e instanceof ApiError ? e : null;
      toast.show(err?.message ?? 'Could not resend', 'error');
      return false;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [phone, resendIn, loading, toast]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setStep('phone');
    setOtpState('');
    setError(null);
    setResendIn(0);
  }, []);

  return {
    step,
    phone,
    otp,
    loading,
    error,
    resendIn,
    setPhone,
    setOtp,
    requestCode,
    verify,
    resend,
    reset,
  };
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\booking\api\booking.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from '../../lib/api/client';
import {
  ScheduleDetail, CreateBookingPayload, CreateBookingResponse, Stand,
} from '../types';

function unwrap<T = any>(raw: any): T {
  if (raw?.data !== undefined) return raw.data as T;
  return raw as T;
}

function toFarePaise(raw: any): number {
  if (typeof raw?.fare === 'number') return raw.fare;
  if (raw?.fare?.amountPaise) return raw.fare.amountPaise;
  if (typeof raw?.fareAmountPaise === 'number') return raw.fareAmountPaise;
  if (typeof raw?.totalFarePaise === 'number') return raw.totalFarePaise;
  return 0;
}

function toStops(raw: any): Stand[] {
  const list: any[] = raw?.route?.stands ?? raw?.route?.stops ?? [];
  const mapped = list.map((s: any, idx: number) => {
    const nested = s?.stand ?? {};
    return {
      standId: String(s?.standId ?? nested.id ?? nested.standId ?? s?.id ?? ''),
      name: String(s?.name ?? nested.name ?? s?.standName ?? `Stop ${idx + 1}`),
      sequence: Number(s?.sequence ?? s?.seq ?? idx + 1),
    };
  }).filter((s) => !!s.standId);
  mapped.sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence);
  return mapped;
}

function toScheduleDetail(raw: any): ScheduleDetail {
  return {
    scheduleId: String(raw?.scheduleId ?? raw?.id ?? ''),
    departureTime: raw?.departureTime ?? '',
    arrivalTime: raw?.arrivalTime ?? '',
    farePaise: toFarePaise(raw),
    operatorName:
      raw?.operatorName ?? raw?.operator?.companyName ?? raw?.operator?.name ?? 'Operator',
    originCityName: raw?.route?.originCity?.name ?? raw?.originCity?.name ?? 'Origin',
    destinationCityName:
      raw?.route?.destinationCity?.name ?? raw?.destinationCity?.name ?? 'Destination',
    busType: raw?.busType ?? raw?.bus?.busType,
    stops: toStops(raw),
  };
}

export async function fetchScheduleDetail(scheduleId: string): Promise<ScheduleDetail> {
  const raw = unwrap(await api.get(`/public/schedules/${scheduleId}`));
  return toScheduleDetail(raw);
}

export async function createBooking(payload: CreateBookingPayload): Promise<CreateBookingResponse> {
  const raw = unwrap(await api.post('/passenger/bookings', payload));
  return {
    bookingId: String(raw?.bookingId ?? raw?.id ?? ''),
    status: String(raw?.status ?? 'PENDING'),
    expiresAt: raw?.expiresAt,
  };
}

import React from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { Badge, Card } from '../../../components/ui';
import { formatBookingReference, formatDate, formatMoney, formatTime } from '../../../lib/format';
import type { BookingSummaryDTO } from '../../../types';
import { statusMeta } from '../status';

type Props = { booking: BookingSummaryDTO; onPress: () => void };

export const BookingCard: React.FC<Props> = ({ booking, onPress }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const meta = statusMeta(booking.status);
  const seats = booking.passengers.map((p) => p.seatLabel).join(', ');
  return (
    <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.md} onPress={onPress}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <Text style={[typography.monoSmall, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>{formatBookingReference(booking.bookingReference)}</Text>
        <Badge variant={meta.variant}>{meta.label}</Badge>
      </View>
      <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright, marginTop: 8 }]}>
        {`${booking.routeSummary.originCity} \u2192 ${booking.routeSummary.destinationCity}`}
      </Text>
      <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginTop: 2 }]}>
        {`${formatDate(booking.departureTime, 'short')}, ${formatTime(booking.departureTime)}`}
      </Text>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginTop: 10 }}>
        <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>{`Seats ${seats}`}</Text>
        <Text style={[typography.monoData, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{formatMoney(booking.totalFarePaise)}</Text>
      </View>
    </Card>
  );
};

import { View, Text, TextInput, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';

export function PassengerCard({ index, passenger, onChange }: Props) {
  const theme = useCleanTheme();

  return (
    <View style={[styles.card, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface, borderColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
      <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: theme.colors.borderSubtle }]}>

        placeholder="e.g. Alice Sharma"

        autoComplete="name"
      />

      <View style={styles.row}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>

            placeholder="28"
            placeholderTextColor={theme.colors.textMuted}
            keyboardType="number-pad"
            maxLength={3}
          />
        </View>
        <View style={{ flex: 2 }}>
          <Text style={[styles.label, { color: theme.colors.text }]}>Gender</Text>
          <View style={[styles.genderRow, { borderColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
            {(['MALE', 'FEMALE', 'OTHER'] as const).map((g) => (
              <Text
                key={g}
                onPress={() => onChange({ gender: g })}
                style={[
                  styles.genderOption,
                  passenger.gender === g
                    ? { backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary, color: theme.isDark ? '#000' : '#FFF' }
                    : { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceElevated, color: theme.colors.textMuted },
                ]}
              >
                {g === 'MALE' ? 'M' : g === 'FEMALE' ? 'F' : 'O'}
              </Text>
            ))}
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

});

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\booking\hooks\useBookings.ts
================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { getBooking, getRefundPreview, getTicket, listBookings } from '../api/booking.api';

export const bookingKeys = {
  list: ['bookings'] as const,
  detail: (id: string) => ['booking', id] as const,
  ticket: (id: string) => ['ticket', id] as const,
  refund: (id: string) => ['refund-preview', id] as const,
};

export function useBookings(enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: bookingKeys.list, queryFn: () => listBookings(50), enabled, staleTime: 30_000 });
}

/** `refetchInterval` is for screens waiting on a payment to land. */
export function useBooking(id: string, refetchInterval: number | false = false) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: bookingKeys.detail(id), queryFn: () => getBooking(id), refetchInterval, staleTime: 5_000 });
}

export function useTicket(id: string, enabled: boolean) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: bookingKeys.ticket(id), queryFn: () => getTicket(id), enabled, staleTime: 60_000 });
}

export function useRefundPreview(id: string) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: bookingKeys.refund(id), queryFn: () => getRefundPreview(id), staleTime: 0, gcTime: 0 });
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\booking\status.ts
================================================================================
import type { BadgeVariant } from '../../components/ui';
import type { BookingStatus } from '../../types';

const META: Record<BookingStatus, { label: string; variant: BadgeVariant }> = {
  PENDING: { label: 'Awaiting payment', variant: 'amber' },
  CONFIRMED: { label: 'Confirmed', variant: 'emerald' },
  CHECKED_IN: { label: 'Boarded', variant: 'cyan' },
  COMPLETED: { label: 'Completed', variant: 'slate' },
  CANCELLED: { label: 'Cancelled', variant: 'red' },
  OPERATOR_CANCELLED: { label: 'Cancelled by operator', variant: 'red' },
  REFUNDED: { label: 'Refunded', variant: 'slate' },
  EXPIRED: { label: 'Expired', variant: 'slate' },
  FAILED: { label: 'Failed', variant: 'red' },
};

export const statusMeta = (s: BookingStatus): { label: string; variant: BadgeVariant } => META[s];

export type Bucket = 'upcoming' | 'past' | 'cancelled';

const DEAD: ReadonlySet<BookingStatus> = new Set<BookingStatus>(['CANCELLED', 'OPERATOR_CANCELLED', 'REFUNDED', 'EXPIRED', 'FAILED']);

export function bucketOf(b: { status: BookingStatus; departureTime: string }, now = Date.now()): Bucket {
  if (DEAD.has(b.status)) return 'cancelled';
  if (b.status === 'COMPLETED' || new Date(b.departureTime).getTime() < now) return 'past';
  return 'upcoming';
}

/** A confirmed booking can be cancelled until the bus leaves. */
export const canCancel = (b: { status: BookingStatus; departureTime: string }, now = Date.now()): boolean =>
  b.status === 'CONFIRMED' && new Date(b.departureTime).getTime() > now;

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\payment\components\RazorpayCheckout.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Linking } from 'react-native';
import { WebView } from 'react-native-webview';

export type CheckoutMessage =
  | { type: 'success'; paymentId: string; orderId: string; signature: string }
  | { type: 'failed'; code?: string; description?: string }
  | { type: 'dismiss' };

type Props = {
  keyId: string;
  orderId: string;
  amountPaise: number;
  description: string;
  contact?: string;
  email?: string | null;
  onMessage: (m: CheckoutMessage) => void;
};

/** JSON that is safe to embed inside a <script> tag. */
const embed = (v: unknown): string => JSON.stringify(v).replace(/</g, '\\u003c').replace(/>/g, '\\u003e').replace(/&/g, '\\u0026');

/**
 * Razorpay Standard Checkout in a WebView. The result is only a hint for the UI:
 * the booking becomes CONFIRMED when the server receives the gateway webhook.
 */
export const RazorpayCheckout: React.FC<Props> = ({ keyId, orderId, amountPaise, description, contact, email, onMessage }) => {
  const html = useMemo(() => {
    const options = {
      key: keyId,
      order_id: orderId,
      amount: amountPaise,
      currency: 'INR',
      name: 'BusVerse',
      description,
      prefill: { contact: contact ?? '', email: email ?? '' },
      theme: { color: '#0055ED' },
    };
    return `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"></head>
<body style="margin:0;background:#0B1830">
<script src="https://checkout.razorpay.com/v1/checkout.js"></script>
<script>
  var post = function (m) { window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage(JSON.stringify(m)); };
  var options = ${embed(options)};
  options.handler = function (r) {
    post({ type: 'success', paymentId: r.razorpay_payment_id, orderId: r.razorpay_order_id, signature: r.razorpay_signature });
  };
  options.modal = { ondismiss: function () { post({ type: 'dismiss' }); } };
  window.onload = function () {
    try {
      var rzp = new Razorpay(options);
      rzp.on('payment.failed', function (r) {
        post({ type: 'failed', code: r.error && r.error.code, description: r.error && r.error.description });
      });
      rzp.open();
    } catch (e) { post({ type: 'failed', description: String(e) }); }
  };
</script></body></html>`;
  }, [keyId, orderId, amountPaise, description, contact, email]);

  return (
    <WebView
      originWhitelist={['*']}
      source={{ html, baseUrl: 'https://checkout.razorpay.com' }}
      javaScriptEnabled
      domStorageEnabled
      setSupportMultipleWindows={false}
      onShouldStartLoadWithRequest={(req: { url: string }) => {
        if (/^(https?:|about:|data:)/i.test(req.url)) return true;
        // UPI / intent links open the user's payment app.
        void Linking.openURL(req.url).catch(() => undefined);
        return false;
      }}
      onMessage={(e: { nativeEvent: { data: string } }) => {
        try {
          onMessage(JSON.parse(e.nativeEvent.data) as CheckoutMessage);
        } catch {
          /* ignore unrelated messages */
        }
      }}
    />
  );
};
