### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\api\cities.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api, EP } from '../../../lib/api';
import type { CityDTO } from '../../../types';

export type ListCitiesParams = {
  q?: string;
  limit?: number;
};

function buildQuery(params: Record<string, string | number | undefined>): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (v === undefined || v === '') continue;
    parts.push(`${encodeURIComponent(k)}=${encodeURIComponent(String(v))}`);
  }
  return parts.join('&');
}

export async function listCities(params: ListCitiesParams = {}): Promise<CityDTO[]> {
  const qs = buildQuery({ q: params.q, limit: params.limit ?? 30 });
  return api.get<CityDTO[]>(`${EP.cities}${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`);
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\api\search.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api, EP } from '../../../lib/api';
import type { ScheduleSearchResultDTO } from '../../../types';

export type SearchParams = {
  originCityId: string;
  destinationCityId: string;
  date: string;
  sortBy?: 'departureTime' | 'fare';
};

function buildQuery(params: Record<string, string | undefined>): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (v === undefined || v === '') continue;
    parts.push(`${encodeURIComponent(k)}=${encodeURIComponent(v)}`);
  }
  return parts.join('&');
}

export async function searchSchedules(params: SearchParams): Promise<ScheduleSearchResultDTO[]> {
  const qs = buildQuery({
    originCityId: params.originCityId,
    destinationCityId: params.destinationCityId,
    date: params.date,
    sortBy: params.sortBy,
  });
  return api.get<ScheduleSearchResultDTO[]>(`${EP.search}?${qs}`);
}

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\components\CalendarPickerModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { Modal, Button } from '../../../components/ui';

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  value: string;
  onSelect: (iso: string) => void;
};

const WEEKDAYS = ['S', 'M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S'];
const MONTHS = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];

function istToday(): { y: number; m: number; d: number } {
  const now = new Date();
  const ist = new Date(now.getTime() + 5.5 * 3600 * 1000);
  return { y: ist.getUTCFullYear(), m: ist.getUTCMonth(), d: ist.getUTCDate() };
}

function istIso(y: number, m: number, d: number): string {
  return `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function parseIso(iso: string): { y: number; m: number; d: number } | null {
  const match = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso);
  if (!match) return null;
  return { y: Number(match[1]), m: Number(match[2]) - 1, d: Number(match[3]) };
}

export const CalendarPickerModal: React.FC<Props> = ({ open, onClose, value, onSelect }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing, radius } = useTheme();
  const today = istToday();
  const initial = parseIso(value) ?? today;
  const [view, setView] = useState({ y: initial.y, m: initial.m });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      const v = parseIso(value) ?? istToday();
      setView({ y: v.y, m: v.m });
    }
  }, [open, value]);

  const cells = useMemo(() => {
    const firstDay = new Date(Date.UTC(view.y, view.m, 1));
    const lastDay = new Date(Date.UTC(view.y, view.m + 1, 0));
    const startWeekday = firstDay.getUTCDay();
    const daysInMonth = lastDay.getUTCDate();
    const result: Array<number | null> = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < startWeekday; i++) result.push(null);
    for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) result.push(d);
    while (result.length % 7 !== 0) result.push(null);
    return result;
  }, [view]);

  const todayIso = istIso(today.y, today.m, today.d);
  const canGoPrev = view.y > today.y || (view.y === today.y && view.m > today.m);

  const goPrev = () =>
    setView((v) => (v.m === 0 ? { y: v.y - 1, m: 11 } : { y: v.y, m: v.m - 1 }));
  const goNext = () =>
    setView((v) => (v.m === 11 ? { y: v.y + 1, m: 0 } : { y: v.y, m: v.m + 1 }));

  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Select travel date">
      {/* Month nav */}
      <View
        style={{
          flexDirection: 'row',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          marginBottom: spacing.md,
        }}
      >
        <Pressable
          onPress={goPrev}
          disabled={!canGoPrev}
          style={[
            styles.navBtn,
            { borderColor: tokens.borderSubtle, opacity: canGoPrev ? 1 : 0.3 },
          ]}
          accessibilityLabel="Previous month"
          accessibilityRole="button"
        >
          <Text style={{ color: tokens.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '700' }}>
            {'<'}
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>
          {MONTHS[view.m]} {view.y}
        </Text>
        <Pressable
          onPress={goNext}
          style={[styles.navBtn, { borderColor: tokens.borderSubtle }]}
          accessibilityLabel="Next month"
          accessibilityRole="button"
        >
          <Text style={{ color: tokens.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '700' }}>
            {'>'}
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      {/* Weekday header */}
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', marginBottom: 4 }}>
        {WEEKDAYS.map((w, i) => (
          <View key={`w-${i}`} style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 6 }}>
            <Text
              style={[typography.monoSmall, { color: tokens.textMuted, fontSize: 10 }]}
            >
              {w}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>

      {/* Day grid */}
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        {cells.map((d, idx) => {
          if (d === null) {
            return <View key={`b-${idx}`} style={styles.cell} />;
          }
          const iso = istIso(view.y, view.m, d);
          const isPast = iso < todayIso;
          const isSelected = iso === value;
          const isToday = iso === todayIso;
          return (
            <Pressable
              key={iso}
              disabled={isPast}
              onPress={() => {
                onSelect(iso);
                onClose();
              }}
              style={[
                styles.cell,
                {
                  borderRadius: radius.sm,
                  backgroundColor: isSelected
                    ? tokens.brandPrimary
                    : isToday
                    ? tokens.brandPrimarySubtle
                    : 'transparent',
                  opacity: isPast ? 0.3 : 1,
                },
              ]}
              accessibilityLabel={`${d} ${MONTHS[view.m]} ${view.y}`}
              accessibilityState={{ selected: isSelected, disabled: isPast }}
              accessibilityRole="button"
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  typography.body,
                  {
                    color: isSelected
                      ? '#FFFFFF'
                      : isToday
                      ? tokens.brandPrimary
                      : tokens.textPrimary,
                    fontWeight: isSelected || isToday ? '700' : '400',
                    fontSize: 14,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {d}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
        <Button variant="ghost" onPress={onClose} fullWidth>
          Cancel
        </Button>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  navBtn: {
    width: 36,
    height: 36,
    borderRadius: 18,
    borderWidth: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  cell: {
    width: '14.2857%',
    height: 40,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    marginVertical: 2,
  },
});

export default CalendarPickerModal;

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\components\CityPickerModal.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { Modal, Input, Spinner } from '../../../components/ui';
import { useCities } from '../hooks/useCities';
import type { CityDTO } from '../../../types';

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSelect: (city: CityDTO) => void;
  title: string;
};

export const CityPickerModal: React.FC<Props> = ({ open, onClose, onSelect, title }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing, radius } = useTheme();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const { data: cities = [], isLoading } = useCities(query);

  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title={title}>
      <Input
        value={query}
        onChangeText={setQuery}
        placeholder="Search cities…"
        autoCapitalize="words"
      />
      <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 420, marginTop: spacing.sm }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {isLoading ? (
          <View style={{ paddingVertical: 40, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <Spinner />
          </View>
        ) : cities.length === 0 ? (
          <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textMuted, textAlign: 'center', paddingVertical: 40 }]}>
            No cities found
          </Text>
        ) : (
          cities.map((city) => (
            <Pressable
              key={city.id}
              onPress={() => {
                onSelect(city);
                setQuery('');
              }}
              style={({ pressed }) => [
                styles.row,
                {
                  borderRadius: radius.sm,
                  borderBottomWidth: 1,
                  borderBottomColor: tokens.borderHairline,
                  backgroundColor: pressed ? tokens.surfaceHover : 'transparent',
                },
              ]}
              accessibilityRole="button"
            >
              <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textPrimary, fontWeight: '600' }]}>
                {city.name}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }]}>
                {city.state}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          ))
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  row: { paddingVertical: 14, paddingHorizontal: 12 },
});

export default CityPickerModal;

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\components\DateField.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { CalendarPickerModal } from './CalendarPickerModal';

type Props = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (iso: string) => void;
};

const MONTHS_SHORT = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun',
  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
];
const WEEKDAYS_SHORT = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];

function formatDisplay(iso: string): string {
  const match = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso);
  if (!match) return iso;
  const d = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(match[1]), Number(match[2]) - 1, Number(match[3])));
  return `${WEEKDAYS_SHORT[d.getUTCDay()]}, ${match[3]} ${MONTHS_SHORT[Number(match[2]) - 1]} ${match[1]}`;
}

export const DateField: React.FC<Props> = ({ label, value, onChange }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <View>
      <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 6 }]}>
        {label}
      </Text>
      <Pressable
        onPress={() => setOpen(true)}
        style={({ pressed }) => [
          styles.field,
          {
            borderColor: pressed ? tokens.borderFocus : tokens.borderSubtle,
            borderWidth: 1,
            borderRadius: radius.sm,
            backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceCard,
          },
        ]}
        accessibilityRole="button"
        accessibilityLabel={`${label}: ${formatDisplay(value)}`}
      >
        <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textPrimary, flex: 1 }]}>
          {formatDisplay(value)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.brandPrimary, fontWeight: '600' }]}>
          Change
        </Text>
      </Pressable>
      <CalendarPickerModal
        open={open}
        onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        value={value}
        onSelect={onChange}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  field: {
    minHeight: 44,
    paddingVertical: 12,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 8,
  },
});

export default DateField;

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\components\ScheduleCard.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { Card, Badge, Button } from '../../../components/ui';
import { formatMoney } from '../../../lib/format/money';
import type { ScheduleSearchResultDTO } from '../../../types';

type Props = {
  schedule: ScheduleSearchResultDTO;
  originCityName: string;
  destinationCityName: string;
  onSelect: () => void;
};

const COACH_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  SEATER: 'Seater',
  AC_SEATER: 'AC Seater',
  SLEEPER: 'Sleeper',
  AC_SLEEPER: 'AC Sleeper',
  SEMI_SLEEPER: 'Semi-Sleeper',
};

function coachLabel(t: string): string {
  return COACH_LABELS[t] ?? t;
}

function fmtTime(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '--:--';
  const ist = new Date(d.getTime() + 5.5 * 3600 * 1000);
  return `${String(ist.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(ist.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function fmtDuration(minutes: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(minutes) || minutes <= 0) return '—';
  const h = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  const m = minutes % 60;
  return h === 0 ? `${m}m` : `${h}h ${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}m`;
}

export const ScheduleCard: React.FC<Props> = ({
  schedule,
  originCityName,
  destinationCityName,
  onSelect,
}) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const seatPct = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(1, schedule.availableSeats / 40));

  return (
    <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.md}>
      {/* Header */}
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
        <Text
          style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright, fontSize: 15, flex: 1 }]}
          numberOfLines={1}
        >
          {schedule.operatorName}
        </Text>
        <Badge variant="brand">{coachLabel(schedule.busType)}</Badge>
      </View>

      {/* Timeline */}
      <View
        style={{
          flexDirection: 'row',
          alignItems: 'center',
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingTop: 14,
          borderTopWidth: 1,
          borderTopColor: tokens.borderHairline,
        }}
      >
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-start', minWidth: 56 }}>
          <Text style={[typography.monoData, { color: tokens.textBright, fontSize: 18 }]}>
            {fmtTime(schedule.departureTime)}
          </Text>
          <Text
            style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }]}
            numberOfLines={1}
          >
            {originCityName}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 8 }}>
          <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>
            {fmtDuration(schedule.durationMinutes)}
          </Text>
          <View
            style={{
              flexDirection: 'row',
              alignItems: 'center',
              marginVertical: 4,
              gap: 3,
              width: '100%',
            }}
          >
            <View style={{ flex: 1, height: 1, backgroundColor: tokens.borderStrong }} />
            <View
              style={{
                width: 4,
                height: 4,
                borderRadius: 2,
                backgroundColor: tokens.brandPrimary,
              }}
            />
            <View style={{ flex: 1, height: 1, backgroundColor: tokens.borderStrong }} />
          </View>
          <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.signalEmerald, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
            Direct
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end', minWidth: 56 }}>
          <Text style={[typography.monoData, { color: tokens.textBright, fontSize: 18 }]}>
            {fmtTime(schedule.arrivalTime)}
          </Text>
          <Text
            style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }]}
            numberOfLines={1}
          >
            {destinationCityName}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Fare + seats + CTA */}
      <View
        style={{
          flexDirection: 'row',
          alignItems: 'flex-end',
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingTop: 14,
          borderTopWidth: 1,
          borderTopColor: tokens.borderHairline,
          gap: 12,
        }}
      >
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'baseline' }}>
            <Text style={[typography.h3, { color: tokens.textBright, fontSize: 22 }]}>
              {formatMoney(schedule.fare.amountPaise)}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginLeft: 4 }]}>
              / seat
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginTop: 6 }}>
            <View
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                height: 3,
                borderRadius: 2,
                backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceSunken,
                overflow: 'hidden',
              }}
            >
              <View
                style={{
                  width: `${seatPct * 100}%`,
                  height: '100%',
                  backgroundColor: tokens.signalEmerald,
                }}
              />
            </View>
            <Text
              style={[
                typography.caption,
                { color: tokens.signalEmerald, fontWeight: '700' },
              ]}
            >
              {schedule.availableSeats} left
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <Button size="sm" onPress={onSelect}>
          Select
        </Button>
      </View>
    </Card>
  );
};

export default ScheduleCard;

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\hooks\useCities.ts
================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { listCities } from '../api/cities.api';

export function useCities(query: string) {
  const trimmed = query.trim();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['cities', trimmed],
    queryFn: () => listCities({ q: trimmed, limit: 30 }),
    staleTime: 60_000,
    gcTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });
}

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\search\hooks\useSearch.ts
================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { searchSchedules, type SearchParams } from '../api/search.api';

export function useSearch(params: SearchParams) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [
      'search',
      params.originCityId,
      params.destinationCityId,
      params.date,
      params.sortBy ?? 'departureTime',
    ],
    queryFn: () => searchSchedules(params),
    staleTime: 30_000,
    gcTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\api\seats.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from '../../lib/api/client';
import { Seat, SeatLayout, SeatLock, AvailabilityResponse, SeatType, SeatStatus } from '../types';

function toRowLetter(v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v === 'number') return String.fromCharCode(64 + v);
  return String(v ?? '');
}

function mapSeat(raw: any): Seat {
  return {
    id: String(raw.id ?? raw.seatId ?? ''),
    seatLabel: String(raw.label ?? raw.seatLabel ?? ''),
    seatRow: toRowLetter(raw.row ?? raw.seatRow ?? ''),
    seatColumn: Number(raw.column ?? raw.seatColumn ?? 0),
    seatType: (raw.type ?? raw.seatType ?? 'SEATER') as SeatType,
    isFemaleOnly: !!raw.isFemaleOnly,
    status: (raw.state ?? raw.status ?? 'AVAILABLE') as SeatStatus,
    lockedUntil: raw.lockedUntil ?? null,
  };
}

function unwrap<T = any>(raw: any): T {
  if (raw?.data !== undefined) return raw.data as T;
  return raw as T;
}

export async function fetchAvailability(scheduleId: string): Promise<AvailabilityResponse> {
  const raw = unwrap(await api.get(`/passenger/schedules/${scheduleId}/availability`));
  const layoutRaw = raw?.layout ?? raw ?? {};
  const seatsRaw: any[] = raw?.seats ?? raw?.seatMap?.seats ?? [];
  const seats = seatsRaw.map(mapSeat);

  const aisleCells = (layoutRaw?.aisleCells ?? []).map((c: any) => ({
    row: toRowLetter(c?.row ?? ''),
    column: Number(c?.column ?? 0),
  }));

  return {
    seats,
    layout: {
      rowsCount: Number(layoutRaw?.rowsCount ?? 0),
      columnsCount: Number(layoutRaw?.columnsCount ?? 0),
      aisleCells,
    },
  };
}

export async function lockSeat(scheduleId: string, seatId: string): Promise<SeatLock> {
  const raw = unwrap(await api.post('/passenger/seat-locks', { scheduleId, seatId }));
  return {
    lockId: String(raw.lockId ?? ''),
    seatId,
    seatLabel: String(raw.seatLabel ?? ''),
    expiresAt: String(raw.expiresAt ?? ''),
  };
}

export async function releaseLock(lockId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api.delete(`/passenger/seat-locks/${lockId}`);
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\components\FixtureTile.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Circle, Line } from 'react-native-svg';
import type { PlanFixture } from '@busverse/seat-engine';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';

const SHORT: Record<string, string> = { DOOR: 'DR', DRIVER: '', CREW: 'CR', STAIRS: 'ST', WC: 'WC', LUGGAGE: 'LG', VOID: '' };

export const FixtureTile: React.FC<{ item: PlanFixture }> = ({ item }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  if (item.fixture === 'VOID') return null;
  const walkable = item.fixture === 'DOOR' || item.fixture === 'STAIRS';
  const text = item.w >= 40 ? item.label : SHORT[item.fixture] ?? '';
  const color = walkable ? tokens.brandPrimary : tokens.textMuted;
  return (
    <View
      accessible
      accessibilityLabel={item.label || item.fixture}
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',
        left: item.x,
        top: item.y,
        width: item.w,
        height: item.h,
        borderRadius: radius.sm,
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderStyle: 'dashed',
        borderColor: walkable ? tokens.brandPrimaryBorder : tokens.borderSubtle,
        backgroundColor: walkable ? tokens.brandPrimarySubtle : 'transparent',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
      }}
    >
      {item.fixture === 'DRIVER' ? (
        <Svg width={Math.min(item.w, item.h) * 0.6} height={Math.min(item.w, item.h) * 0.6} viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <Circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2" fill="none" />
          <Circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.5" fill={color} />
          <Line x1="12" y1="14.5" x2="12" y2="21" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2" />
          <Line x1="3.5" y1="10" x2="9.5" y2="11" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2" />
          <Line x1="20.5" y1="10" x2="14.5" y2="11" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2" />
        </Svg>
      ) : text ? (
        <Text numberOfLines={1} style={[typography.caption, { color, fontSize: 9, letterSpacing: 0.4 }]}>
          {text}
        </Text>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
};


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\components\LockCountdown.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../../../theme/passengerAdapter';

interface Props {
  expiresAt: string;
  onExpire: () => void;
}

export function LockCountdown({ expiresAt, onExpire }: Props) {
  const theme = useCleanTheme();
  const [remaining, setRemaining] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let fired = false;
    const tick = () => {
      const ms = new Date(expiresAt).getTime() - Date.now();
      setRemaining(Math.max(0, ms));
      if (ms <= 0 && !fired) {
        fired = true;
        onExpire();
      }
    };
    tick();
    const id = setInterval(tick, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [expiresAt, onExpire]);

  const total = Math.floor(remaining / 1000);
  const mm = String(Math.floor(total / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
  const ss = String(total % 60).padStart(2, '0');

  let color = theme.colors.textMuted;
  if (remaining < 60_000) color = theme.colors.warning;
  if (remaining < 30_000) color = theme.colors.danger;

  return (
    <Text style={[styles.text, { color }]} accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
      ⏱ {mm}:{ss} to complete
    </Text>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  text: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '700', fontFamily: 'monospace', textAlign: 'center' },
});

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\components\SeatCell.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../../../theme/passengerAdapter';
import { Seat } from '../types';

interface Props {
  seat: Seat;
  isSelectedByMe: boolean;
  onToggle: (seat: Seat) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function SeatCell({ seat, isSelectedByMe, onToggle, disabled = false }: Props) {
  const theme = useCleanTheme();
  const isBooked = seat.status === 'BOOKED';
  const isLockedByOther = seat.status === 'LOCKED' && !isSelectedByMe;
  const isInactive = isBooked || isLockedByOther || disabled;

  let bg = theme.colors.surface;
  let border = theme.colors.border;
  let text = theme.colors.text;

  if (isBooked) {
    bg = theme.colors.surfaceElevated;
    border = theme.colors.borderSubtle;
    text = theme.colors.textMuted;
  } else if (isSelectedByMe) {
    bg = theme.colors.primary;
    border = theme.colors.primary;
    text = theme.isDark ? '#000' : '#FFF';
  } else if (isLockedByOther) {
    bg = theme.isDark ? 'rgba(245,158,11,0.15)' : 'rgba(216,168,96,0.2)';
    border = theme.colors.warning;
    text = theme.colors.warning;
  } else if (seat.isFemaleOnly) {
    bg = theme.isDark ? 'rgba(236,72,153,0.15)' : 'rgba(219,39,119,0.1)';
    border = '#DB2777';
    text = theme.isDark ? '#F472B6' : '#BE185D';
  }

  const height = seat.seatType === 'SLEEPER' ? 62 : seat.seatType === 'SEMI_SLEEPER' ? 54 : 44;

  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={() => !isInactive && onToggle(seat)}
      disabled={isInactive}
      style={[
        styles.cell,
        { height, backgroundColor: bg, borderColor: border, opacity: isBooked ? 0.5 : 1 },
      ]}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityState={{ disabled: isInactive, selected: isSelectedByMe }}
      accessibilityLabel={`Seat ${seat.seatLabel}, ${isBooked ? 'booked' : isLockedByOther ? 'locked' : isSelectedByMe ? 'selected' : 'available'}${seat.isFemaleOnly ? ', female only' : ''}`}
    >
      <Text style={[styles.label, { color: text }]}>{seat.seatLabel}</Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  cell: {
    width: 42,
    borderRadius: 8,
    borderWidth: 1.5,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: '700',
    fontFamily: 'monospace',
  },
});

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\components\SeatDeck.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../../../theme/passengerAdapter';
import { Seat, SeatLayout } from '../types';
import { SeatCell } from './SeatCell';

interface Props {
  seats: Seat[];
  layout: SeatLayout;
  selectedSeatIds: string[];
  onSeatClick: (seat: Seat) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function SeatDeck({ seats, layout, selectedSeatIds, onSeatClick, disabled = false }: Props) {
  const theme = useCleanTheme();

  const seatByKey = new Map<string, Seat>();
  seats.forEach((s) => seatByKey.set(`${s.seatRow}${s.seatColumn}`, s));

  const rowLetters: string[] = [];
  for (let r = 0; r < layout.rowsCount; r++) {
    rowLetters.push(String.fromCharCode(65 + r));
  }

  const cols = layout.columnsCount;
  const leftCols = Math.min(2, Math.max(1, cols - 1));
  const selectedSet = new Set(selectedSeatIds);

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.deck,
        { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface, borderColor: theme.colors.border },
      ]}
    >
      <View style={[styles.shield, { borderBottomColor: theme.colors.borderSubtle }]}>
        <Text style={[styles.shieldText, { color: theme.colors.textMuted }]}>WINDSCREEN</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.shieldText, { color: theme.colors.textMuted }]}>DRIVER</Text>
      </View>

      {rowLetters.map((rowChar) => {
        const leftSeats: Seat[] = [];
        const rightSeats: Seat[] = [];
        for (let c = 1; c <= leftCols; c++) {
          const s = seatByKey.get(`${rowChar}${c}`);
          if (s) leftSeats.push(s);
        }
        for (let c = leftCols + 1; c <= cols; c++) {
          const s = seatByKey.get(`${rowChar}${c}`);
          if (s) rightSeats.push(s);
        }

        return (
          <View key={rowChar} style={styles.row}>
            <Text style={[styles.rowLabel, { color: theme.colors.textMuted }]}>{rowChar}</Text>
            <View style={styles.seats}>
              {leftSeats.map((s) => (
                <SeatCell
                  key={s.id}
                  seat={s}
                  isSelectedByMe={selectedSet.has(s.id)}
                  onToggle={onSeatClick}
                  disabled={disabled}
                />
              ))}
            </View>
            <View style={styles.aisle} />
            <View style={styles.seats}>
              {rightSeats.map((s) => (
                <SeatCell
                  key={s.id}
                  seat={s}
                  isSelectedByMe={selectedSet.has(s.id)}
                  onToggle={onSeatClick}
                  disabled={disabled}
                />
              ))}
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.rowLabel, { color: theme.colors.textMuted }]}>{rowChar}</Text>
          </View>
        );
      })}

      <View style={[styles.rearExit, { borderTopColor: theme.colors.borderSubtle }]}>
        <Text style={[styles.shieldText, { color: theme.colors.textMuted }]}>REAR EXIT</Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  deck: { padding: 16, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 2 },
  shield: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingBottom: 12,
    marginBottom: 12,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderStyle: 'dashed',
  },
  shieldText: { fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '700', letterSpacing: 1, fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: 8 },
  rowLabel: { width: 20, textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '700' },
  seats: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 },
  aisle: { width: 20 },
  rearExit: { paddingTop: 12, marginTop: 12, borderTopWidth: 1, alignItems: 'center' },
});

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\components\SeatMap.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import {
  buildRenderPlan,
  fitToWidth,
  statusOf,
  type DeckId,
  type Layout,
  type RuntimeMap,
} from '@busverse/seat-engine';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { SeatTile } from './SeatTile';
import { FixtureTile } from './FixtureTile';

type Props = {
  layout: Layout;
  runtime: RuntimeMap;
  /** Keys of the seats I have selected (held). */
  selected: ReadonlySet<string>;
  /** Keys with a request in flight. */
  pending: ReadonlySet<string>;
  onToggle: (key: string) => void;
  /** Width the bus may use. */
  availableWidth: number;
};

const FRAME = 14;

/**
 * Draws any layout the engine can describe: 2+2, 3+2, 1+1, sleepers, double berths,
 * rear benches, cut-outs, double-deckers. No layout-specific code lives here.
 */
export const SeatMap: React.FC<Props> = ({ layout, runtime, selected, pending, onToggle, availableWidth }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const firstDeck = layout.decks[0]?.id ?? 'MAIN';
  const [deckId, setDeckId] = useState<DeckId>(firstDeck);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!layout.decks.some((d) => d.id === deckId)) setDeckId(firstDeck);
  }, [layout, deckId, firstDeck]);

  const fit = useMemo(() => fitToWidth(layout, Math.max(120, availableWidth - FRAME * 2 - 4)), [layout, availableWidth]);
  const plan = useMemo(() => buildRenderPlan(layout, fit.options), [layout, fit]);
  const deck = plan.decks.find((d) => d.id === deckId) ?? plan.decks[0];
  if (!deck) return null;

  const availableOn = (id: DeckId): number =>
    layout.seats.filter((s) => s.deck === id && statusOf(runtime, s.key) === 'AVAILABLE').length;

  const body = (
    <View
      style={{
        width: deck.width + FRAME * 2,
        paddingHorizontal: FRAME,
        paddingTop: FRAME,
        paddingBottom: FRAME + 4,
        backgroundColor: tokens.surfaceCard,
        borderColor: tokens.borderStrong,
        borderWidth: 2,
        borderTopLeftRadius: 36,
        borderTopRightRadius: 36,
        borderBottomLeftRadius: radius.lg,
        borderBottomRightRadius: radius.lg,
      }}
    >
      <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 10, letterSpacing: 2 }]}>FRONT</Text>
      <View style={{ width: deck.width, height: deck.height }}>
        {deck.fixtures.map((f, i) => (
          <FixtureTile key={`f${i}`} item={f} />
        ))}
        {deck.seats.map((s) => (
          <SeatTile
            key={s.key}
            item={s}
            status={statusOf(runtime, s.key)}
            selected={selected.has(s.key)}
            pending={pending.has(s.key)}
            onPress={onToggle}
          />
        ))}
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
      {plan.decks.length > 1 ? (
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', marginBottom: 12, gap: 8 }}>
          {plan.decks.map((d) => {
            const active = d.id === deck.id;
            return (
              <Pressable
                key={d.id}
                accessibilityRole="tab"
                accessibilityState={{ selected: active }}
                onPress={() => setDeckId(d.id)}
                style={{
                  paddingVertical: 8,
                  paddingHorizontal: 14,
                  borderRadius: radius.pill,
                  borderWidth: 1,
                  borderColor: active ? tokens.brandPrimary : tokens.borderSubtle,
                  backgroundColor: active ? tokens.brandPrimarySubtle : tokens.surfaceCard,
                }}
              >
                <Text style={[typography.label, { color: active ? tokens.brandPrimary : tokens.textSecondary }]}>
                  {`${d.title} (${availableOn(d.id)})`}
                </Text>
              </Pressable>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      ) : null}
      {fit.overflow ? (
        <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
          {body}
        </ScrollView>
      ) : (
        body
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\components\SeatTile.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { PlanSeat, SeatStatus } from '@busverse/seat-engine';
import { isSelectable } from '@busverse/seat-engine';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';

type Props = {
  item: PlanSeat;
  status: SeatStatus;
  selected: boolean;
  pending: boolean;
  onPress: (key: string) => void;
};

const STATUS_WORD: Record<SeatStatus, string> = {
  AVAILABLE: 'available',
  LOCKED: 'held by another passenger',
  BOOKED: 'booked',
  MINE: 'held for you',
  BLOCKED: 'not for sale',
};

export const SeatTile: React.FC<Props> = React.memo(({ item, status, selected, pending, onPress }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, radius } = useTheme();
  const tappable = (isSelectable(status) || selected) && !pending;

  let bg = tokens.seatAvailableBg;
  let border = tokens.seatAvailableBorder;
  let fg = tokens.seatAvailableText;
  if (item.femaleOnly) {
    bg = tokens.seatFemaleBg;
    border = tokens.seatFemaleBorder;
    fg = tokens.seatFemaleText;
  }
  if (status === 'LOCKED') {
    bg = tokens.seatLockedBg;
    border = tokens.seatLockedBorder;
    fg = tokens.seatLockedText;
  }
  if (status === 'BOOKED' || status === 'BLOCKED') {
    bg = tokens.seatBookedBg;
    border = tokens.seatBookedBorder;
    fg = tokens.seatBookedText;
  }
  if (selected || status === 'MINE') {
    bg = tokens.seatSelectedBg;
    border = tokens.seatSelectedBorder;
    fg = tokens.seatSelectedText;
  }

  const berth = item.unit !== 'SEAT';
  const where = item.window ? 'window' : item.aisleSide ? 'aisle' : item.middle ? 'middle' : '';
  const word = selected ? 'selected' : STATUS_WORD[status];
  const label = `${berth ? 'Berth' : 'Seat'} ${item.label}${where ? `, ${where}` : ''}${item.femaleOnly ? ', women only' : ''}, ${word}`;

  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      accessibilityState={{ selected, disabled: !tappable }}
      disabled={!tappable}
      onPress={() => onPress(item.key)}
      hitSlop={4}
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',
        left: item.x,
        top: item.y,
        width: item.w,
        height: item.h,
        backgroundColor: bg,
        borderColor: border,
        borderWidth: selected ? 2 : 1,
        borderRadius: berth ? radius.xs + 2 : radius.sm,
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        opacity: pending ? 0.55 : status === 'BLOCKED' ? 0.5 : 1,
      }}
    >
      {berth ? (
        <View style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 4, width: '55%', height: 5, borderRadius: 3, backgroundColor: border, opacity: 0.45 }} />
      ) : null}
      <Text style={[typography.monoSmall, { color: fg, fontSize: 11 }]} numberOfLines={1}>
        {item.label}
      </Text>
      {item.capacity > 1 ? <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: fg, fontSize: 9, marginTop: 1 }]}>{`x${item.capacity}`}</Text> : null}
    </Pressable>
  );
});
SeatTile.displayName = 'SeatTile';

        </Button>

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\hooks\useAvailability.ts
================================================================================
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  fromLegacy,
  hasErrors,
  parseLayout,
  validateLayout,
  type Layout,
  type RuntimeMap,
  type SeatRuntime,
} from '@busverse/seat-engine';
import type { AvailabilityDTO, AvailabilitySeatDTO } from '../../../types';
import { getAvailability } from '../api/seats.api';

export interface SeatIndex {
  byId: Map<string, AvailabilitySeatDTO>;
  byKey: Map<string, AvailabilitySeatDTO>;
}

const keyOf = (s: AvailabilitySeatDTO): string => s.key ?? s.label;

/**
 * The layout the server sent (v2 spec), or the legacy rows upgraded losslessly.
 * Never a made-up layout: if neither is usable the screen shows an error instead of a fake bus.
 */
export function layoutFromAvailability(data: AvailabilityDTO): Layout | null {
  const parsed = parseLayout(data.layout.spec);
  if (parsed.layout) return parsed.layout;
  const legacy = fromLegacy({
    rowsCount: data.layout.rowsCount,
    columnsCount: data.layout.columnsCount,
    seats: data.seats.map((s) => ({
      seatRow: s.row,
      seatColumn: s.column,
      seatLabel: s.label,
      seatType: s.type,
      isFemaleOnly: s.isFemaleOnly,
      key: keyOf(s),
    })),
  });
  return hasErrors(validateLayout(legacy, { access: 'off' })) ? null : legacy;
}

export function useAvailability(scheduleId: string, enabled = true) {
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['availability', scheduleId],
    queryFn: () => getAvailability(scheduleId),
    enabled: enabled && !!scheduleId,
    staleTime: 15_000,
    refetchInterval: 60_000, // safety net when the socket is down
    refetchOnReconnect: true,
    retry: 1,
  });
  const data = query.data;
  const rev = data?.layout.rev ?? 0;
  const seatCount = data?.seats.length ?? 0;

  // Geometry only changes when the layout revision changes, not on every seat lock.
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const layout = useMemo(() => (data ? layoutFromAvailability(data) : null), [rev, seatCount, scheduleId]);

  const index = useMemo<SeatIndex>(() => {
    const byId = new Map<string, AvailabilitySeatDTO>();
    const byKey = new Map<string, AvailabilitySeatDTO>();
    for (const s of data?.seats ?? []) {
      byId.set(s.id, s);
      byKey.set(keyOf(s), s);
    }
    return { byId, byKey };
  }, [data?.seats]);

  const runtime = useMemo<RuntimeMap>(() => {
    const out: Record<string, SeatRuntime> = {};
    for (const s of data?.seats ?? []) out[keyOf(s)] = { status: s.state, lockedUntil: s.lockedUntil };
    return out;
  }, [data?.seats]);

  return { ...query, layout, index, runtime };
}


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\hooks\useSeatDeck.ts
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Seat, SeatLayout, SeatLock } from '../types';
import { fetchAvailability, lockSeat, releaseLock } from '../api/seats.api';

export function useSeatDeck(scheduleId: string | undefined, enabled = true) {
  const [seats, setSeats] = useState<Seat[]>([]);
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState<SeatLayout | null>(null);
  const [locks, setLocks] = useState<Map<string, SeatLock>>(() => new Map());
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [togglingSeatId, setTogglingSeatId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!scheduleId) return;
    try {
      const data = await fetchAvailability(scheduleId);
      setSeats(data.seats);
      setLayout(data.layout);
      setError(null);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.warn('[useSeatDeck] availability failed:', e?.message);
      setError(e?.message ?? 'Could not load seats');
    }
  }, [scheduleId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!scheduleId || !enabled) return;
    setLoading(true);
    refresh().finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [scheduleId, enabled, refresh]);

  const selectedSeatIds = useMemo(() => Array.from(locks.keys()), [locks]);

  const earliestExpiry = useMemo(() => {
    let earliest: string | null = null;
    locks.forEach((l) => {
      if (!earliest || new Date(l.expiresAt) < new Date(earliest!)) earliest = l.expiresAt;
    });
    return earliest;
  }, [locks]);

  const toggle = useCallback(
    async (seat: Seat) => {
      if (!scheduleId || togglingSeatId) return;
      setTogglingSeatId(seat.id);
      try {
        if (locks.has(seat.id)) {
          const lock = locks.get(seat.id)!;
          try { await releaseLock(lock.lockId); } catch {}
          setLocks((prev) => {
            const next = new Map(prev);
            next.delete(seat.id);
            return next;
          });
        } else {
          const lock = await lockSeat(scheduleId, seat.id);
          setLocks((prev) => new Map(prev).set(seat.id, { ...lock, seatLabel: seat.seatLabel }));
        }
      } catch (e: any) {
        const code = e?.code as string | undefined;
        if (code === 'SEAT_LOCKED' || code === 'SEAT_ALREADY_BOOKED') {
          setError('That seat was just taken. Pick another.');
          await refresh();
        } else {
          setError(e?.message ?? 'Could not lock seat');
        }
      } finally {
        setTogglingSeatId(null);
      }
    },
    [scheduleId, locks, togglingSeatId, refresh],
  );

  const handleExpire = useCallback(() => {
    setLocks(new Map());
    setError('Your seat lock expired. Please pick again.');
    refresh();
  }, [refresh]);

  return {
    seats, layout, locks, loading, error, togglingSeatId,
    selectedSeatIds, earliestExpiry,
    toggle, handleExpire, refresh,
    clearError: () => setError(null),
  };
}

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\hooks\useSeatLock.ts
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { MAX_SEATS_PER_BOOKING } from '@busverse/seat-engine';
import { useToast } from '../../../components/ui';
import { ApiError } from '../../../lib/api';
import { wsManager } from '../../../lib/ws';
import type { SeatLockDTO, SeatReleasedEvent } from '../../../types';
import { getActiveLocks, lockSeat as apiLockSeat, releaseLock as apiReleaseLock } from '../api/seats.api';

type LocksMap = Record<string, SeatLockDTO>;

export interface UseSeatLockResult {
  /** My locks, by seatId. */
  locks: LocksMap;
  pending: ReadonlySet<string>;
  earliestExpiry: number | null;
  /** Holds from an earlier visit. Shown to the user, never restored silently. */
  resumable: SeatLockDTO[];
  toggleSeat: (seatId: string) => Promise<void>;
  /** All-or-nothing: if one seat cannot be locked, the ones just locked are released again. */
  lockMany: (seatIds: string[]) => Promise<boolean>;
  releaseSeat: (seatId: string) => Promise<void>;
  releaseAll: () => Promise<void>;
  resume: () => void;
  discardResumable: () => Promise<void>;
  dropExpired: () => void;
}

const messageFor = (err: unknown): string => {
  if (err instanceof ApiError && (err.code === 'SEAT_LOCKED' || err.code === 'SEAT_ALREADY_BOOKED')) return err.message;
  return 'Could not hold that seat. Please try again.';
};

export function useSeatLock(scheduleId: string, maxSeats: number = MAX_SEATS_PER_BOOKING): UseSeatLockResult {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [locks, setLocks] = useState<LocksMap>({});
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const [resumable, setResumable] = useState<SeatLockDTO[]>([]);
  const locksRef = useRef<LocksMap>({});
  locksRef.current = locks;

  const mark = useCallback((ids: string[], on: boolean) => {
    setPending((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      for (const id of ids) (on ? next.add(id) : next.delete(id));
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const acquire = useCallback(
    async (seatId: string): Promise<SeatLockDTO | null> => {
      mark([seatId], true);
      try {
        const lock = await apiLockSeat(scheduleId, seatId);
        setLocks((prev) => ({ ...prev, [seatId]: lock }));
        return lock;
      } catch (err) {
        toast.show(messageFor(err), 'error');
        return null;
      } finally {
        mark([seatId], false);
      }
    },
    [scheduleId, toast, mark],
  );

  const releaseSeat = useCallback(
    async (seatId: string) => {
      const lock = locksRef.current[seatId];
      if (!lock) return;
      setLocks((prev) => {
        const next = { ...prev };
        delete next[seatId];
        return next;
      });
      try {
        await apiReleaseLock(lock.lockId);
      } catch {
        setLocks((prev) => ({ ...prev, [seatId]: lock }));
        toast.show('Could not release the seat. Please try again.', 'error');
      }
    },
    [toast],
  );

  const toggleSeat = useCallback(
    async (seatId: string) => {
      if (locksRef.current[seatId]) return releaseSeat(seatId);
      if (Object.keys(locksRef.current).length >= maxSeats) {
        toast.show(`You can book at most ${maxSeats} seats at a time.`, 'warning');
        return;
      }
      await acquire(seatId);
    },
    [acquire, releaseSeat, maxSeats, toast],
  );

  const lockMany = useCallback(
    async (seatIds: string[]): Promise<boolean> => {
      if (Object.keys(locksRef.current).length + seatIds.length > maxSeats) {
        toast.show(`You can book at most ${maxSeats} seats at a time.`, 'warning');
        return false;
      }
      const got: SeatLockDTO[] = [];
      for (const id of seatIds) {
        const lock = await acquire(id);
        if (!lock) {
          for (const g of got) await releaseSeat(g.seatId);
          return false;
        }
        got.push(lock);
      }
      return true;
    },
    [acquire, releaseSeat, maxSeats, toast],
  );

  const releaseAll = useCallback(async () => {
    const entries = Object.values(locksRef.current);
    if (entries.length === 0) return;
    setLocks({});
    await Promise.allSettled(entries.map((l) => apiReleaseLock(l.lockId)));
  }, []);

  const resume = useCallback(() => {
    setLocks((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      for (const l of resumable) next[l.seatId] = l;
      return next;
    });
    setResumable([]);
  }, [resumable]);

  const discardResumable = useCallback(async () => {
    const list = resumable;
    setResumable([]);
    await Promise.allSettled(list.map((l) => apiReleaseLock(l.lockId)));
  }, [resumable]);

  const dropExpired = useCallback(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    setLocks((prev) => {
      const next: LocksMap = {};
      for (const [id, l] of Object.entries(prev)) if (new Date(l.expiresAt).getTime() > now) next[id] = l;
      return Object.keys(next).length === Object.keys(prev).length ? prev : next;
    });
  }, []);

  // Someone else (or the server) released one of my seats.
  useEffect(() => {
    return wsManager.on('seat:released', (data) => {
      const evt = data as unknown as SeatReleasedEvent;
      if (!locksRef.current[evt.seatId]) return;
      setLocks((prev) => {
        const next = { ...prev };
        delete next[evt.seatId];
        return next;
      });
      toast.show('One of your seat holds ended. Please pick again.', 'warning', 4000);
    });
  }, [toast]);

  // Offer, never silently restore, holds left over from an earlier visit.
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    getActiveLocks()
      .then((active) => {
        if (!cancelled) setResumable(active.filter((l) => l.scheduleId === scheduleId && !locksRef.current[l.seatId]));
      })
      .catch(() => undefined);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [scheduleId]);

  const earliestExpiry = useMemo(() => {
    const times = Object.values(locks)
      .map((l) => new Date(l.expiresAt).getTime())
      .filter((t) => Number.isFinite(t));
    return times.length > 0 ? Math.min(...times) : null;
  }, [locks]);

  return { locks, pending, earliestExpiry, resumable, toggleSeat, lockMany, releaseSeat, releaseAll, resume, discardResumable, dropExpired };
}


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\seats\hooks\useSeatRealtime.ts
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { wsManager } from '../../../lib/ws';
import type {
  AvailabilityDTO,
  SeatBookedEvent,
  SeatLockedEvent,
  SeatReleasedEvent,
} from '../../../types';

export function useSeatRealtime(scheduleId: string): void {
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!scheduleId) return;
    const topic = `seat:availability:${scheduleId}`;
    wsManager.subscribe(topic);

    const patch = (
      seatId: string,
      state: 'AVAILABLE' | 'LOCKED' | 'BOOKED',
      lockedUntil: string | null,
    ) => {
      qc.setQueryData<AvailabilityDTO>(['availability', scheduleId], (prev) => {
        if (!prev) return prev;
        const idx = prev.seats.findIndex((s) => s.id === seatId);
        if (idx === -1) return prev;
        const nextSeats = [...prev.seats];
        nextSeats[idx] = { ...nextSeats[idx], state, lockedUntil };
        return { ...prev, seats: nextSeats };
      });
    };

    const offLocked = wsManager.on('seat:locked', (data) => {
      const evt = data as unknown as SeatLockedEvent;
      if (evt.lockedByMe) return;
      patch(evt.seatId, 'LOCKED', evt.lockedUntil);
    });
    const offReleased = wsManager.on('seat:released', (data) => {
      const evt = data as unknown as SeatReleasedEvent;
      patch(evt.seatId, 'AVAILABLE', null);
    });
    const offBooked = wsManager.on('seat:booked', (data) => {
      const evt = data as unknown as SeatBookedEvent;
      patch(evt.seatId, 'BOOKED', null);
    });

    return () => {
      wsManager.unsubscribe(topic);
      offLocked();
      offReleased();
      offBooked();
    };
  }, [scheduleId, qc]);
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\features\tracking\components\LiveMap.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import MapView, { Marker, Polyline } from 'react-native-maps';
import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';

type Stand = { standId: string; standName: string; lat: number; lng: number; sequence: number };

type Props = {
  stands: Stand[];
  position: { lat: number; lng: number } | null;
  nextStandId: string | null;
};

export const LiveMap: React.FC<Props> = ({ stands, position, nextStandId }) => {
  const { tokens } = useTheme();
  const ref = useRef<MapView | null>(null);
  const ordered = [...stands].sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence);
  const coords = ordered.map((s) => ({ latitude: s.lat, longitude: s.lng }));

  const fitted = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (fitted.current || coords.length === 0) return;
    fitted.current = true;
    const all = position ? [...coords, { latitude: position.lat, longitude: position.lng }] : coords;
    ref.current?.fitToCoordinates(all, { edgePadding: { top: 80, right: 50, bottom: 80, left: 50 }, animated: false });
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [coords.length]);

  const first = coords[0];
  return (
    <MapView
      ref={ref}
      style={{ flex: 1 }}
      initialRegion={first ? { latitude: first.latitude, longitude: first.longitude, latitudeDelta: 1.5, longitudeDelta: 1.5 } : undefined}
    >
      {coords.length > 1 ? <Polyline coordinates={coords} strokeColor={tokens.brandPrimary} strokeWidth={4} /> : null}
      {ordered.map((s) => (
        <Marker
          key={s.standId}
          coordinate={{ latitude: s.lat, longitude: s.lng }}
          title={s.standName}
          description={s.standId === nextStandId ? 'Next stop' : undefined}
          pinColor={s.standId === nextStandId ? tokens.warningAmber : tokens.textMuted}
        />
      ))}
      {position ? <Marker coordinate={{ latitude: position.lat, longitude: position.lng }} title="Your bus" pinColor={tokens.brandPrimary} /> : null}
    </MapView>
  );
};


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\features\tracking\hooks\useLiveTrip.ts
================================================================================
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useWsEvent, useWsTopic } from '../../../lib/ws';
import type { BusPositionEvent, LiveTripDTO, MapDTO } from '../../../types';
import { getLiveTrip, getTripMap } from '../api/tracking.api';

/** Route + live state of a trip. Positions arrive over the socket; polling is only the fallback. */
export function useLiveTrip(tripId: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const live = useQuery({ queryKey: ['trip-live', tripId], queryFn: () => getLiveTrip(tripId), refetchInterval: 20_000 });
  const map = useQuery({ queryKey: ['trip-map', tripId], queryFn: () => getTripMap(tripId), staleTime: 60_000 });

  useWsTopic(`trip:live:${tripId}`);

  const onPosition = useCallback(
    (data: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      const p = data as unknown as BusPositionEvent;
      if (p.tripId !== tripId) return;
      qc.setQueryData<LiveTripDTO>(['trip-live', tripId], (prev) =>
        prev
          ? {
              ...prev,
              currentPosition: { lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng, recordedAt: p.recordedAt },
              speedKmh: p.speedKmh,
              lastUpdatedAt: p.recordedAt,
              isStale: false,
              nextStandId: p.nextStandId,
            }
          : prev,
      );
      qc.setQueryData<MapDTO>(['trip-map', tripId], (prev) =>
        prev ? { ...prev, currentPosition: { lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng, recordedAt: p.recordedAt }, lastUpdatedAt: p.recordedAt } : prev,
      );
    },
    [qc, tripId],
  );
  const onTripChange = useCallback(() => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['trip-live', tripId] });
  }, [qc, tripId]);

  useWsEvent('bus:position', onPosition);
  useWsEvent('trip:started', onTripChange);
  useWsEvent('trip:ended', onTripChange);
  useWsEvent('trip:cancelled', onTripChange);

  return { live, map };
}


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\api\client.ts
================================================================================
import { CONFIG } from '../config';
import type { ApiEnvelope } from '../../types';
import { userMessageFor } from './errors';

let accessToken: string | null = null;

export function setAccessToken(t: string | null): void {
  accessToken = t;
}
export function getAccessToken(): string | null {
  return accessToken;
}

let refreshInFlight: Promise<string | null> | null = null;
let refreshHandler: (() => Promise<string | null>) | null = null;

export function setRefreshHandler(h: () => Promise<string | null>): void {
  refreshHandler = h;
}

async function runRefresh(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!refreshHandler) return null;
  if (!refreshInFlight) {
    refreshInFlight = refreshHandler()
      .catch(() => null)
      .finally(() => {
        refreshInFlight = null;
      });
  }
  return refreshInFlight;
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly status: number,
    public readonly code: string,
    message: string,
    public readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>,
    public readonly requestId?: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
  }
}

export type RequestOptions = {
  method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE';
  body?: unknown;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  idempotencyKey?: string;
  skipRefresh?: boolean;
};

async function rawFetch<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOptions, token: string | null): Promise<Response> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'X-Request-Id': `req_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
    ...(opts.headers ?? {}),
  };
  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  if (opts.idempotencyKey) headers['Idempotency-Key'] = opts.idempotencyKey;

  const url = `${CONFIG.API_BASE}${path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`}`;
  
  // BUG FIX: Add a 10-second timeout so the app never hangs forever
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 10000);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      method: opts.method ?? 'GET',
      headers,
      body: opts.body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(opts.body) : undefined,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    return res;
  } catch (err: any) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    if (err.name === 'AbortError') {
      throw new ApiError(408, 'TIMEOUT', 'The request took too long. Check your connection.');
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

export async function request<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  let res = await rawFetch<T>(path, opts, accessToken);

  if (res.status === 401 && !opts.skipRefresh) {
    const newToken = await runRefresh();
    if (newToken) {
      setAccessToken(newToken);
      res = await rawFetch<T>(path, opts, newToken);
    }
  }

  if (res.status === 204) return null as unknown as T;

  const text = await res.text();
  let parsed: unknown = null;
  if (text) {
    try { parsed = JSON.parse(text); } catch { parsed = null; }
  }

  if (!res.ok) {
    const err = (parsed as any)?.error;
    const code = err?.code ?? `HTTP_${res.status}`;
    const message = err?.message ?? userMessageFor(code);
    throw new ApiError(res.status, code, message, err?.details, err?.requestId);
  }

  const env = parsed as ApiEnvelope<T> | T | null;
  if (env && typeof env === 'object' && 'data' in env) {
    return (env as ApiEnvelope<T>).data;
  }
  return env as T;
}

export const api = {
  get: <T>(path: string, opts?: Omit<RequestOptions, 'method' | 'body'>) =>
    request<T>(path, { ...opts, method: 'GET' }),
  post: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, opts?: Omit<RequestOptions, 'method' | 'body'>) =>
    request<T>(path, { ...opts, method: 'POST', body }),
  patch: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, opts?: Omit<RequestOptions, 'method' | 'body'>) =>
    request<T>(path, { ...opts, method: 'PATCH', body }),
  put: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, opts?: Omit<RequestOptions, 'method' | 'body'>) =>
    request<T>(path, { ...opts, method: 'PUT', body }),
  del: <T>(path: string, opts?: Omit<RequestOptions, 'method' | 'body'>) =>
    request<T>(path, { ...opts, method: 'DELETE' }),
};

// Central endpoint paths — from 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS + backend router dump.

export const EP = {
  // Auth
  otpRequest: '/passenger/auth/otp/request',
  otpVerify: '/passenger/auth/otp/verify',
  refresh: (surface: 'passenger' | 'operator' | 'driver' | 'admin') => `/${surface}/auth/refresh`,
  logout: '/passenger/auth/logout',
  me: '/passenger/me',

  // Public
  cities: '/public/cities',
  cityStands: (cityId: string) => `/public/cities/${cityId}/stands`,
  search: '/public/search',
  scheduleDetail: (id: string) => `/public/schedules/${id}`,

  // Seat locks + availability
  availability: (scheduleId: string) => `/passenger/schedules/${scheduleId}/availability`,
  seatLockCreate: '/passenger/seat-locks',
  seatLocksActive: '/passenger/seat-locks/active',
  seatLockRelease: (lockId: string) => `/passenger/seat-locks/${lockId}`,

  // Bookings
  bookingCreate: '/passenger/bookings',
  bookings: '/passenger/bookings',
  booking: (id: string) => `/passenger/bookings/${id}`,
  bookingTicket: (id: string) => `/passenger/bookings/${id}/ticket`,
  refundPreview: (id: string) => `/passenger/bookings/${id}/refund-preview`,
  cancelBooking: (id: string) => `/passenger/bookings/${id}/cancel`,

  // Payments
  paymentCreate: '/passenger/payments',
  payment: (id: string) => `/passenger/payments/${id}`,

  // Trip live
  tripLive: (id: string) => `/passenger/trips/${id}/live`,
  tripMap: (id: string) => `/passenger/trips/${id}/map`,
} as const;

// 52 error codes from 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS §9.2, mapped to user-facing copy from 05-UI-SPEC §19.3.

export const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  // Auth
  INVALID_OTP: 'Incorrect code. Try again.',
  OTP_EXPIRED: 'Code expired. Request a new one.',
  OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED: 'Too many attempts. Try again in 15 min.',
  EMAIL_ALREADY_REGISTERED: 'This email is already registered.',
  PHONE_ALREADY_REGISTERED: 'This phone is already registered.',
  INVALID_EMAIL_FORMAT: 'Enter a valid email address.',
  INVALID_PHONE_FORMAT: 'Enter a valid phone number.',

  // Generic
  UNAUTHORIZED: 'Please log in to continue.',
  FORBIDDEN: "You don't have access to that.",
  NOT_FOUND: "We couldn't find that.",
  VALIDATION_ERROR: 'Some fields need your attention.',
  INVALID_REQUEST: "That request didn't look right.",
  INTERNAL_ERROR: 'Something went wrong. Try again.',
  RATE_LIMITED: 'Too many requests. Wait a moment.',
  SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE: 'Service temporarily unavailable.',
  GATEWAY_TIMEOUT: 'Payment gateway timed out.',

  // Booking / seats
  SEAT_LOCKED: 'This seat was just taken. Pick another.',
  SEAT_ALREADY_BOOKED: 'This seat is already booked.',
  LOCK_EXPIRED: 'Your seat lock expired. Pick again.',
  LOCK_NOT_FOUND: 'Seat lock not found. Pick again.',
  INVALID_SEAT: 'That seat is not valid for this trip.',
  INVALID_SCHEDULE: 'This trip is no longer available.',
  SCHEDULE_DEPARTED: 'This trip has already departed.',
  BOOKING_IMMUTABLE: "Booking can't be modified after confirmation.",
  INVALID_TRANSITION: "Can't do that right now.",
  PAYMENT_NOT_ALLOWED: 'Payment window closed. Book again.',
  PAYMENT_ALREADY_SUCCEEDED: 'Already paid. Redirecting…',

  // Idempotency
  IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED: 'Please refresh and try again.',

  // Refunds
  REFUND_EXCEEDS_PAYMENT: 'Refund amount exceeds payment.',
  PAYMENT_NOT_REFUNDABLE: 'This payment is not refundable.',
};

export function userMessageFor(code: string, fallback = 'Something went wrong.'): string {
  return ERROR_MESSAGES[code] ?? fallback;
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\auth\AuthContext.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  api,
  setAccessToken,
  getAccessToken,
  setRefreshHandler,
  EP,
} from '../api';
import { wsManager } from '../ws';
import { sessionStorage } from './sessionStorage';
import type { AuthTokens, LoginResult, UserDTO } from '../../types';

interface AuthContextValue {
  user: UserDTO | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  isReady: boolean;
  login: (result: LoginResult) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refresh: () => Promise<boolean>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserDTO | null>(null);
  const [isReady, setIsReady] = useState(false);
  const refreshTokenRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  // Wire the API client's refresh handler once
  useEffect(() => {
    setRefreshHandler(async () => {
      const refresh = refreshTokenRef.current ?? (await sessionStorage.getRefreshToken());
      if (!refresh) return null;
      try {
        const res = await api.post<AuthTokens>(EP.refresh('passenger'), { refreshToken: refresh });
        setAccessToken(res.accessToken);
        refreshTokenRef.current = res.refreshToken;
        await sessionStorage.setRefreshToken(res.refreshToken);
        wsManager.setTokens(res.accessToken, res.refreshToken);
        return res.accessToken;
      } catch {
        setAccessToken(null);
        refreshTokenRef.current = null;
        await sessionStorage.clearRefreshToken();
        return null;
      }
    });
  }, []);

  // Boot: try to restore session from stored refresh token
  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const stored = await sessionStorage.getRefreshToken();
        if (!stored) {
          setIsReady(true);
          return;
        }
        refreshTokenRef.current = stored;
        const res = await api.post<AuthTokens>(EP.refresh('passenger'), { refreshToken: stored });
        setAccessToken(res.accessToken);
        refreshTokenRef.current = res.refreshToken;
        await sessionStorage.setRefreshToken(res.refreshToken);

        const me = await api.get<{ userId: string; phone: string; email: string | null; emailVerified: boolean; name: string | null }>(EP.me);
        setUser({
          id: me.userId,
          phone: me.phone,
          email: me.email,
          emailVerified: me.emailVerified,
          name: me.name,
        });
        wsManager.setTokens(res.accessToken, res.refreshToken);
        wsManager.connect();
      } catch {
        // Silent fail — user sees logged-out state
      } finally {
        setIsReady(true);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(async (result: LoginResult) => {
    setAccessToken(result.accessToken);
    refreshTokenRef.current = result.refreshToken;
    await sessionStorage.setRefreshToken(result.refreshToken);
    setUser(result.user);
    wsManager.setTokens(result.accessToken, result.refreshToken);
    wsManager.connect();
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await api.post(EP.logout);
    } catch {
      // best-effort
    }
    setAccessToken(null);
    refreshTokenRef.current = null;
    await sessionStorage.clearRefreshToken();
    setUser(null);
    wsManager.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const refresh = useCallback(async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    const token = getAccessToken();
    if (token) return true;
    const stored = refreshTokenRef.current ?? (await sessionStorage.getRefreshToken());
    if (!stored) return false;
    try {
      const res = await api.post<AuthTokens>(EP.refresh('passenger'), { refreshToken: stored });
      setAccessToken(res.accessToken);
      refreshTokenRef.current = res.refreshToken;
      await sessionStorage.setRefreshToken(res.refreshToken);
      wsManager.setTokens(res.accessToken, res.refreshToken);
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }, []);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        isAuthenticated: !!user,
        isReady,
        login,
        logout,
        refresh,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\auth\sessionStorage.ts
================================================================================
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

// On native: use expo-secure-store (encrypted, hardware-backed).
// On web: expo-secure-store is unavailable — fall back to AsyncStorage
//         (localStorage under the hood). Acceptable for dev / demo.

const KEY_REFRESH = '@busverse/refresh_token';

type NativeSecureStore = {
  getItemAsync: (k: string) => Promise<string | null>;
  setItemAsync: (k: string, v: string) => Promise<void>;
  deleteItemAsync: (k: string) => Promise<void>;
};

let SecureStore: NativeSecureStore | null = null;
if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
  // Lazy require so web bundling doesn't try to load the native module
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
  const mod = require('expo-secure-store');
  SecureStore = {
    getItemAsync: mod.getItemAsync,
    setItemAsync: mod.setItemAsync,
    deleteItemAsync: mod.deleteItemAsync,
  };
}

export const sessionStorage = {
  async getRefreshToken(): Promise<string | null> {
    if (SecureStore) return SecureStore.getItemAsync(KEY_REFRESH);
    return AsyncStorage.getItem(KEY_REFRESH);
  },

  async setRefreshToken(token: string): Promise<void> {
    if (SecureStore) {
      await SecureStore.setItemAsync(KEY_REFRESH, token);
      return;
    }
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(KEY_REFRESH, token);
  },

  async clearRefreshToken(): Promise<void> {
    if (SecureStore) {
      await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(KEY_REFRESH);
      return;
    }
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(KEY_REFRESH);
  },
};

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\config.ts

import { Platform } from 'react-native';

// Per 05-UI-SPEC §8, all API and WS URLs route through this config.
// Matches backend contracts in 07-API-AND-REALTIME-CONTRACTS §2.

const DEV_HOST = Platform.OS === 'android' ? '10.0.2.2' : 'localhost';

function baseUrl(envKey: string, fallbackHost: string): string {
  const raw = (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>)[envKey];
  if (raw && raw.length > 0) return raw.replace(/\/+$/, '');
  return fallbackHost;
}

export const CONFIG = {
  API_BASE: baseUrl('EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE', `http://${DEV_HOST}:4310/v1`),
  WS_URL: baseUrl('EXPO_PUBLIC_WS_URL', `http://${DEV_HOST}:4310`),
  WS_PATH: '/v1/ws',
  DEV_MODE: process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DEV_MODE === 'true',
  // Per 07 §10, seat lock TTL is 5 min, booking payment window 15 min.
  SEAT_LOCK_TTL_SECONDS: 300,
  PAYMENT_WINDOW_MINUTES: 15,
  // Per 04 §11.4, WS-to-poll fallback after 5 retries.
  WS_MAX_RETRIES: 5,
} as const;

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\format\date.ts
================================================================================
// Date formatter per 05-UI-SPEC §8.3.
// Every date is displayed in IST (Asia/Kolkata) unless noted.

const IST = 'Asia/Kolkata';

const MONTHS_SHORT = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
const MONTHS_LONG = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
const DAYS_SHORT = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];

function partsInIst(iso: string) {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  // Convert to IST
  const istMs = d.getTime() + 5.5 * 3600 * 1000;
  const ist = new Date(istMs);
  return {
    year: ist.getUTCFullYear(),
    month: ist.getUTCMonth(),
    day: ist.getUTCDate(),
    weekday: ist.getUTCDay(),
    hours: ist.getUTCHours(),
    minutes: ist.getUTCMinutes(),
  };
}

export type DateStyle = 'full' | 'short' | 'day' | 'relative';

export function formatDate(iso: string, style: DateStyle = 'full'): string {
  const p = partsInIst(iso);
  if (!p) return '—';
  if (style === 'short') return `${String(p.day).padStart(2, '0')} ${MONTHS_SHORT[p.month]}`;
  if (style === 'day') return `${DAYS_SHORT[p.weekday]}, ${String(p.day).padStart(2, '0')} ${MONTHS_SHORT[p.month]}`;
  if (style === 'relative') {
    const now = new Date();
    const istNow = new Date(now.getTime() + 5.5 * 3600 * 1000);
    const dayMs = 24 * 3600 * 1000;
    const todayIst = Date.UTC(istNow.getUTCFullYear(), istNow.getUTCMonth(), istNow.getUTCDate());
    const targetIst = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month, p.day);
    const diffDays = Math.round((targetIst - todayIst) / dayMs);
    if (diffDays === 0) return 'Today';
    if (diffDays === 1) return 'Tomorrow';
    if (diffDays === -1) return 'Yesterday';
    if (diffDays > 1 && diffDays <= 7) return `In ${diffDays} days`;
    return `${String(p.day).padStart(2, '0')} ${MONTHS_SHORT[p.month]} ${p.year}`;
  }
  return `${String(p.day).padStart(2, '0')} ${MONTHS_LONG[p.month]} ${p.year}`;
}

export function todayIsoDateIst(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  const istNow = new Date(now.getTime() + 5.5 * 3600 * 1000);
  return istNow.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\format\money.ts
================================================================================
// Money formatter per 05-UI-SPEC §8.2.
// Source of truth: integer paise → display string with ₹ symbol.

const INR = 'INR';

function groupIndian(numStr: string): string {
  // Indian numbering: last 3 digits, then groups of 2 (e.g. 12,34,567).
  if (numStr.length <= 3) return numStr;
  const last3 = numStr.slice(-3);
  const rest = numStr.slice(0, -3);
  const groups: string[] = [];
  let cursor = rest;
  while (cursor.length > 2) {
    groups.unshift(cursor.slice(-2));
    cursor = cursor.slice(0, -2);
  }
  if (cursor.length > 0) groups.unshift(cursor);
  return `${groups.join(',')},${last3}`;
}

export function formatMoney(amountPaise: number, currency: string = INR): string {
  const safe = Number.isFinite(amountPaise) ? amountPaise : 0;
  const negative = safe < 0;
  const abs = Math.abs(Math.round(safe));
  const rupees = Math.floor(abs / 100);
  const paise = abs % 100;
  const rupeeStr = groupIndian(String(rupees));
  const paiseStr = String(paise).padStart(2, '0');
  const symbol = currency === 'INR' ? '₹' : currency + ' ';
  return `${negative ? '-' : ''}${symbol}${rupeeStr}.${paiseStr}`;
}

export function formatMoneyCompact(amountPaise: number): string {
  const safe = Number.isFinite(amountPaise) ? amountPaise : 0;
  const rupees = Math.round(safe / 100);
  if (rupees >= 100000) return `₹${(rupees / 100000).toFixed(1)}L`;
  if (rupees >= 1000) return `₹${(rupees / 1000).toFixed(1)}K`;
  return `₹${rupees}`;
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\format\phone.ts
================================================================================
// Phone formatter per 05-UI-SPEC §8.5.
// Backend stores E.164 (e.g. +919876543210).

export function formatPhone(e164: string): string {
  if (!e164) return '';
  const digits = e164.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('91')) {
    return `+91 ${digits.slice(2, 7)} ${digits.slice(7)}`;
  }
  if (digits.length === 10) {
    return `${digits.slice(0, 5)} ${digits.slice(5)}`;
  }
  return e164;
}

export function maskPhone(e164: string): string {
  const digits = e164.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
  if (digits.length >= 4) {
    const last4 = digits.slice(-4);
    const prefix = digits.startsWith('91') ? '+91 ' : '';
    return `${prefix}•••••• ${last4}`;
  }
  return e164;
}

export function phoneToE164(raw: string): string {
  const digits = raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
  if (digits.startsWith('91') && digits.length === 12) return `+${digits}`;
  if (digits.length === 10) return `+91${digits}`;
  return raw.startsWith('+') ? raw : `+${digits}`;
}
