### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\format\reference.ts
================================================================================
// Reference formatters per 05-UI-SPEC §8.6.
// Backend format: BKG-YYYY-NNNNN (booking_reference_seq_{year}).

export function isValidBookingReference(ref: string): boolean {
  return /^BKG-\d{4}-\d{5}$/.test(ref);
}

export function formatBookingReference(ref: string): string {
  // Backend already returns canonical form; this is just a sanity display helper.
  return isValidBookingReference(ref) ? ref : ref;
}

// Per Design_guide §7 illustration, seating label like "2B (Window)".
// Window vs aisle is derived from column relative to aisle cells.
export function seatPositionLabel(column: number, aisleColumns: number[]): 'Window' | 'Aisle' | 'Middle' {
  const minAisle = Math.min(...aisleColumns.filter((c) => c > 0), Infinity);
  if (!Number.isFinite(minAisle)) return 'Window';
  if (column < minAisle) return 'Window';
  if (column === minAisle || column === minAisle + 1) return 'Aisle';
  return 'Window';
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\format\time.ts
================================================================================
// Time formatter per 05-UI-SPEC §8.4.
// All times displayed in IST. Server stores UTC.

function istParts(iso: string) {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  const istMs = d.getTime() + 5.5 * 3600 * 1000;
  const ist = new Date(istMs);
  return { hours: ist.getUTCHours(), minutes: ist.getUTCMinutes() };
}

export function formatTime(iso: string): string {
  const p = istParts(iso);
  if (!p) return '—';
  return `${String(p.hours).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(p.minutes).padStart(2, '0')} IST`;
}

export function formatTimeRange(departIso: string, arriveIso: string): string {
  const dep = istParts(departIso);
  const arr = istParts(arriveIso);
  if (!dep || !arr) return '—';
  const fmt = (p: { hours: number; minutes: number }) =>
    `${String(p.hours).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(p.minutes).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  return `${fmt(dep)} → ${fmt(arr)} IST`;
}

export function formatDuration(minutes: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(minutes) || minutes <= 0) return '—';
  const h = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  const m = minutes % 60;
  if (h === 0) return `${m}m`;
  return `${h}h ${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}m`;
}

export function formatCountdown(ms: number): string {
  const safe = Math.max(0, ms);
  const totalSeconds = Math.floor(safe / 1000);
  const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
  const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
  return `${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\storage\asyncStore.ts

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

export const asyncStore = {
  async get<T>(key: string): Promise<T | null> {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return null;
    try {
      return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  },
  async set<T>(key: string, value: T): Promise<void> {
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  },
  async remove(key: string): Promise<void> {
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(key);
  },
  async clear(prefix: string): Promise<void> {
    const keys = await AsyncStorage.getAllKeys();
    const matching = keys.filter((k) => k.startsWith(prefix));
    if (matching.length > 0) await AsyncStorage.multiRemove(matching);
  },
};

### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\storage\offlineCache.ts
================================================================================
import { asyncStore } from './asyncStore';
import type { BookingDetailDTO, TicketDTO } from '../../types';

// Offline ticket cache per 04-TICKET-AND-TRACKING §9.
// QR payload is immutable after issuance (invariant #17), so cache TTL is generous.

const TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 30 days
const KEY_TICKET = (bookingId: string) => `@busverse/ticket/${bookingId}`;

type CachedTicket = {
  booking: BookingDetailDTO;
  ticket: TicketDTO;
  cachedAt: number;
};

export async function cacheTicket(booking: BookingDetailDTO, ticket: TicketDTO): Promise<void> {
  const payload: CachedTicket = { booking, ticket, cachedAt: Date.now() };
  await asyncStore.set(KEY_TICKET(booking.bookingId), payload);
}

export async function getCachedTicket(bookingId: string): Promise<CachedTicket | null> {
  const cached = await asyncStore.get<CachedTicket>(KEY_TICKET(bookingId));
  if (!cached) return null;
  if (Date.now() - cached.cachedAt > TTL_MS) {
    await asyncStore.remove(KEY_TICKET(bookingId));
    return null;
  }
  return cached;
}

export async function clearTicketCache(bookingId: string): Promise<void> {
  await asyncStore.remove(KEY_TICKET(bookingId));
}

export async function clearAllTicketCache(): Promise<void> {
  await asyncStore.clear('@busverse/ticket/');
}

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\ws\socket.ts
================================================================================
import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client';
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

type Envelope = { event: string; data: Record<string, unknown>; ts: string };
type Listener = (data: Record<string, unknown>, ts: string) => void;

class WsManager {
  private socket: Socket | null = null;
  private token: string | null = null;
  private refreshToken: string | null = null;
  private topics = new Set<string>();
  private listeners = new Map<string, Set<Listener>>();
  private reconnecting = false;

  setTokens(access: string | null, refresh: string | null): void {
    this.token = access;
    this.refreshToken = refresh;
  }

  connect(): void {
    if (this.socket?.connected) return;
    if (!this.token) return;

    this.socket?.disconnect();
    this.socket = io(CONFIG.WS_URL, {
      path: CONFIG.WS_PATH,
      transports: ['websocket', 'polling'],
      reconnection: true,
      reconnectionAttempts: CONFIG.WS_MAX_RETRIES,
      reconnectionDelay: 1000,
      reconnectionDelayMax: 16000,
    });

    this.socket.on('connect', () => {
      this.reconnecting = false;
      this.socket?.emit('message', { action: 'auth', token: this.token });
    });

    this.socket.on('auth:ok', () => {
      if (this.topics.size > 0) {
        this.socket?.emit('message', { action: 'subscribe', topics: Array.from(this.topics) });
      }
    });

    this.socket.on('auth:expiring', () => {
      if (this.refreshToken) {
        this.socket?.emit('message', { action: 'auth:refresh', refreshToken: this.refreshToken });
      }
    });

    this.socket.on('auth:refreshed', (payload: { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }) => {
      this.token = payload.accessToken;
      this.refreshToken = payload.refreshToken;
    });

    this.socket.on('message', (env: Envelope) => {
      if (!env?.event) return;
      const set = this.listeners.get(env.event);
      if (!set) return;
      set.forEach((fn) => {
        try {
          fn(env.data ?? {}, env.ts ?? new Date().toISOString());
        } catch (err) {
          // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
          console.warn('[ws] listener threw', env.event, err);
        }
      });
    });

    this.socket.on('disconnect', () => {
      this.reconnecting = true;
    });
  }

  subscribe(topic: string): void {
    this.topics.add(topic);
    if (this.socket?.connected) {
      this.socket.emit('message', { action: 'subscribe', topics: [topic] });
    }
  }

  unsubscribe(topic: string): void {
    this.topics.delete(topic);
    if (this.socket?.connected) {
      this.socket.emit('message', { action: 'unsubscribe', topics: [topic] });
    }
  }

  on(event: string, listener: Listener): () => void {
    if (!this.listeners.has(event)) this.listeners.set(event, new Set());
    this.listeners.get(event)!.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners.get(event)?.delete(listener);
    };
  }

  disconnect(): void {
    this.topics.clear();
    this.listeners.clear();
    this.socket?.disconnect();
    this.socket = null;
    this.token = null;
    this.refreshToken = null;
    this.reconnecting = false;
  }

  isConnected(): boolean {
    return this.socket?.connected ?? false;
  }
}

export const wsManager = new WsManager();

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\lib\ws\useWsEvent.ts
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { wsManager } from './socket';

/** Subscribe to a single WS event for the lifetime of the component. */
export function useWsEvent(
  event: string,
  handler: (data: Record<string, unknown>, ts: string) => void,
): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    const off = wsManager.on(event, handler);
    return off;
  }, [event, handler]);
}

/** Subscribe to a WS topic for the lifetime of the component. */
export function useWsTopic(topic: string | null): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!topic) return;
    wsManager.subscribe(topic);
    return () => wsManager.unsubscribe(topic);
  }, [topic]);
}

### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\CancelBookingScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { CancelBookingResultDTO, RefundTier, RootStackParamList } from '../types';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { ApiError } from '../lib/api';
import { formatDate, formatMoney, formatTime } from '../lib/format';
import { AppHeader, ScreenContainer } from '../components/layout';
import { Banner, Button, Card, Skeleton, useToast } from '../components/ui';
import { bookingKeys, useRefundPreview } from '../features/booking/hooks/useBookings';
import { cancelBooking } from '../features/booking/api/booking.api';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Cancel'>;

const TIER_TEXT: Record<RefundTier, string> = {
  TIER_FULL: 'Full refund',
  TIER_75: '75% refund',
  TIER_50: '50% refund',
  TIER_ZERO: 'No refund',
  TIER_NOT_ELIGIBLE: 'This booking can no longer be cancelled',
};

export const CancelBookingScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation, route }) => {
  const { bookingId } = route.params;
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const toast = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const preview = useRefundPreview(bookingId);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<CancelBookingResultDTO | null>(null);

  const doCancel = async (): Promise<void> => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      setDone(await cancelBooking(bookingId));
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: bookingKeys.list });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: bookingKeys.detail(bookingId) });
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Could not cancel the booking. Please try again.', 'error');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const confirm = (): void => {
    Alert.alert('Cancel this booking?', 'This cannot be undone.', [
      { text: 'Keep booking', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Cancel booking', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => void doCancel() },
    ]);
  };

  const Line: React.FC<{ label: string; value: string; strong?: boolean }> = ({ label, value, strong = false }) => (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingVertical: 4 }}>
      <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>{label}</Text>
      <Text style={[strong ? typography.monoData : typography.body, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{value}</Text>
    </View>
  );

  const body = (): React.ReactNode => {
    if (done) {
      return (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <Banner type="success" message="Your booking is cancelled." />
          <Card padding={spacing.md}>
            <Line label="Refund" value={TIER_TEXT[done.refund.tier]} />
            <Line label="Refund amount" value={formatMoney(done.refund.amountPaise)} strong />
          </Card>
          <Button onPress={() => navigation.navigate('MyTickets')} fullWidth>
            Back to my tickets
          </Button>
        </View>
      );
    }
    if (preview.isError) {
      return <Banner type="error" message="Could not work out your refund." action={{ label: 'Retry', onPress: () => void preview.refetch() }} />;
    }
    const p = preview.data;
    if (!p) return <Skeleton height={240} borderRadius={18} />;
    const eligible = p.tier !== 'TIER_NOT_ELIGIBLE';
    return (
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.lg}>
          <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{TIER_TEXT[p.tier]}</Text>
          {p.cancellationDeadlineAt ? (
            <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 4 }]}>
              {`Free changes end ${formatDate(p.cancellationDeadlineAt, 'short')}, ${formatTime(p.cancellationDeadlineAt)}`}
            </Text>
          ) : null}
        </Card>
        {eligible ? (
          <Card padding={spacing.md}>
            <Line label="You paid" value={formatMoney(p.totalPaidPaise)} />
            <Line label="Fare refund" value={formatMoney(p.fareRefundPaise)} />
            <Line label="Tax refund" value={formatMoney(p.taxRefundPaise)} />
            <Line label="Fee refund" value={formatMoney(p.feeRefundPaise)} />
            <Line label="Total refund" value={formatMoney(p.totalRefundPaise)} strong />
            <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 8 }]}>
              {`Back to ${p.paymentMethod ?? 'your payment method'}${p.paymentMethodLast4 ? ` ending ${p.paymentMethodLast4}` : ''} in ${p.expectedRefundDays.min} to ${p.expectedRefundDays.max} days.`}
            </Text>
          </Card>
        ) : null}
        <Button variant="danger" onPress={confirm} loading={busy} disabled={busy || !eligible} fullWidth>
          Cancel booking
        </Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} fullWidth>
          Keep my booking
        </Button>
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <ScreenContainer scroll padded={false}>
      <AppHeader title="Cancel booking" onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.xl }}>{body()}</View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default CancelBookingScreen;

### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\LiveTrackingScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import type { RootStackParamList, TripStatus } from '../types';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { ApiError } from '../lib/api';
import { formatDuration, formatTime } from '../lib/format';
import { AppHeader } from '../components/layout';
import { Badge, Banner, Card, Spinner } from '../components/ui';
import { LiveMap } from '../features/tracking/components/LiveMap';
import { useLiveTrip } from '../features/tracking/hooks/useLiveTrip';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'LiveTracking'>;

const STATUS_TEXT: Record<TripStatus, string> = {
  SCHEDULED: 'Not started yet',
  IN_PROGRESS: 'On the way',
  COMPLETED: 'Trip completed',
  CANCELLED: 'Trip cancelled',
};

export const LiveTrackingScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation, route }) => {
  const { tripId } = route.params;
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const { live, map } = useLiveTrip(tripId);

  const failed = map.error ?? live.error;
  const header = <AppHeader title="Track your bus" onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />;

  if (failed) {
    const denied = failed instanceof ApiError && (failed.status === 403 || failed.status === 404);
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: tokens.canvasBg }}>
        {header}
        <View style={{ padding: spacing.sm }}>
          <Banner
            type={denied ? 'info' : 'error'}
            message={denied ? 'Tracking is available for confirmed bookings once the trip exists.' : 'Could not load the live position.'}
            action={denied ? undefined : { label: 'Retry', onPress: () => { void live.refetch(); void map.refetch(); } }}
          />
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
  if (!map.data || !live.data) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: tokens.canvasBg }}>
        {header}
        <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
          <Spinner size="lg" />
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const l = live.data;
  const next = l.eta.slice(0, 3);
  const started = l.status === 'IN_PROGRESS';
  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: tokens.canvasBg }}>
      {header}
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <LiveMap
          stands={map.data.route.stands}
          position={l.currentPosition ? { lat: l.currentPosition.lat, lng: l.currentPosition.lng } : null}
          nextStandId={l.nextStandId}
        />
        <View style={{ position: 'absolute', left: spacing.sm, right: spacing.sm, bottom: spacing.sm }}>
          <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.md}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{STATUS_TEXT[l.status]}</Text>
              <Badge variant={started ? (l.isStale ? 'amber' : 'emerald') : 'slate'}>{started ? (l.isStale ? 'Signal weak' : 'Live') : l.status.toLowerCase()}</Badge>
            </View>
            {started && l.lastUpdatedAt ? (
              <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 4 }]}>
                {`Updated ${formatTime(l.lastUpdatedAt)}${l.speedKmh !== null ? `, ${Math.round(l.speedKmh)} km/h` : ''}`}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
            {!started && l.status === 'SCHEDULED' ? (
              <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 4 }]}>The map updates here as soon as the driver starts the trip.</Text>
            ) : null}
            {started && next.length > 0 ? (
              <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
                {next.map((e) => (
                  <View key={e.standId} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingVertical: 3 }}>
                    <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>{e.standName}</Text>
                    <Text style={[typography.monoData, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{formatDuration(e.etaMinutes)}</Text>
                  </View>
                ))}
              </View>
            ) : null}
          </Card>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

export default LiveTrackingScreen;


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\MyTicketsScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import type { RootStackParamList } from '../types';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth';
import { AppHeader, ScreenContainer } from '../components/layout';
import { Banner, Button, Chip, Skeleton } from '../components/ui';
import { SignInRequired } from '../features/auth';
import { BookingCard } from '../features/booking/components/BookingCard';
import { useBookings } from '../features/booking/hooks/useBookings';
import { bucketOf, type Bucket } from '../features/booking/status';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'MyTickets'>;

const TABS: Array<{ id: Bucket; label: string; empty: string }> = [
  { id: 'upcoming', label: 'Upcoming', empty: 'No upcoming trips yet.' },
  { id: 'past', label: 'Past', empty: 'Your completed trips will show up here.' },
  { id: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled', empty: 'Nothing cancelled.' },
];

export const MyTicketsScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Bucket>('upcoming');
  const bookings = useBookings(isAuthenticated);

  // Always show fresh data when the screen comes back into view (e.g. after paying or cancelling).
  useEffect(() => {
    const off = navigation.addListener('focus', () => {
      if (isAuthenticated) void bookings.refetch();
    });
    return off;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [navigation, isAuthenticated]);

  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const out: Record<Bucket, NonNullable<typeof bookings.data>> = { upcoming: [], past: [], cancelled: [] };
    for (const b of bookings.data ?? []) out[bucketOf(b, now)].push(b);
    out.upcoming.sort((a, b) => a.departureTime.localeCompare(b.departureTime));
    out.past.sort((a, b) => b.departureTime.localeCompare(a.departureTime));
    out.cancelled.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt));
    return out;
  }, [bookings.data]);

  const current = TABS.find((t) => t.id === tab) ?? TABS[0];
  const list = grouped[tab];

  const body = (): React.ReactNode => {
    if (!isAuthenticated) return <SignInRequired message="Sign in to see the tickets you have booked." />;
    return (
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
          {TABS.map((t) => (
            <Chip key={t.id} label={`${t.label} (${grouped[t.id].length})`} selected={tab === t.id} onPress={() => setTab(t.id)} />
          ))}
        </View>
        {bookings.isError ? (
          <Banner type="error" message="Could not load your tickets." action={{ label: 'Retry', onPress: () => void bookings.refetch() }} />
        ) : bookings.isLoading ? (
          <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
            <Skeleton height={120} borderRadius={18} />
            <Skeleton height={120} borderRadius={18} />
          </View>
        ) : list.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.sm }}>
            <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>{current?.empty}</Text>
            {tab === 'upcoming' ? (
              <Button onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Search')}>Find a bus</Button>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        ) : (
          list.map((b) => <BookingCard key={b.bookingId} booking={b} onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Ticket', { bookingId: b.bookingId })} />)
        )}
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <ScreenContainer scroll padded={false}>
      <AppHeader title="My tickets" onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.xl }}>{body()}</View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default MyTicketsScreen;


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\PassengerFormScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, ScrollView, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator } from 'react-native';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../theme/passengerAdapter';
import { PassengerCard, StandsPicker, fetchScheduleDetail } from '../features/booking';
import { createBooking } from '../features/booking/api/booking.api';
import { PassengerInput, Stand } from '../features/booking/types';

export function PassengerFormScreen({ route, navigation }: any) {
  const theme = useCleanTheme();
  const { scheduleId, locks } = route.params;

  const { data: schedule, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedule', scheduleId],
    queryFn: () => fetchScheduleDetail(scheduleId),
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });

  const sortedStops = useMemo(() => [...(schedule?.stops ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence), [schedule]);
  const boardingStops = sortedStops.slice(0, Math.max(1, sortedStops.length - 1));
  const droppingStops = sortedStops.slice(1);

  const [boardingStandId, setBoardingStandId] = useState<string>(boardingStops[0]?.standId ?? '');
  const [droppingStandId, setDroppingStandId] = useState<string>(droppingStops[droppingStops.length - 1]?.standId ?? '');
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState<PassengerInput[]>(
    (locks ?? []).map((l: any) => ({
      seatId: l.seatId,
      seatLabel: l.seatLabel ?? l.seatId.slice(0, 4),
      name: '',
      age: '',
      gender: '',
    })),
  );
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const updatePassenger = (idx: number, patch: Partial<PassengerInput>) => {
    setPassengers((prev) => prev.map((p, i) => (i === idx ? { ...p, ...patch } : p)));
  };

  const validate = (): string | null => {
    if (!boardingStandId) return 'Select a boarding stand.';
    if (!droppingStandId) return 'Select a dropping stand.';
    const bIdx = sortedStops.findIndex((s) => s.standId === boardingStandId);
    const dIdx = sortedStops.findIndex((s) => s.standId === droppingStandId);
    if (bIdx < 0 || dIdx < 0) return 'Invalid stand selection.';
    if (bIdx >= dIdx) return 'Dropping stand must come after boarding.';
    for (let i = 0; i < passengers.length; i++) {
      const p = passengers[i];
      if (!p.name.trim() || p.name.trim().length < 2) return `Passenger ${i + 1}: enter a valid name.`;
      const age = parseInt(p.age, 10);
      if (isNaN(age) || age < 1 || age > 119) return `Passenger ${i + 1}: enter age 1–119.`;
      if (!p.gender) return `Passenger ${i + 1}: select a gender.`;
    }
    return null;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    setError(null);
    const err = validate();
    if (err) {
      setError(err);
      return;
    }
    if (!locks || locks.length === 0) {
      setError('No seats selected.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      // Backend schema expects one lockId + passengers array
      const payload = {
        lockId: locks[0].lockId,
        boardingStandId,
        droppingStandId,
        passengers: passengers.map((p) => ({
          seatId: p.seatId,
          name: p.name.trim(),
          age: parseInt(p.age, 10),
          gender: p.gender as 'MALE' | 'FEMALE' | 'OTHER',
        })),
      };
      const result = await createBooking(payload as any);
      navigation.replace('Payment', { bookingId: result.bookingId });
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.warn('[PassengerForm] submit failed:', e?.message);
      setError(e?.message ?? 'Could not create booking. Try again.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading || !schedule) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.center, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.bg }]}>
        <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={theme.colors.primary} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (sortedStops.length < 2) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.center, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.bg, padding: 24 }]}>
        <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.text, textAlign: 'center' }}>
          This route has {sortedStops.length} stop{sortedStops.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} — need at least 2.
        </Text>
        <Pressable onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
          <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.primary, fontWeight: '700' }}>← Back to seats</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const totalPaise = schedule.farePaise * passengers.length;

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.bg }}>
      <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} hitSlop={12}>
          <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.primary, fontSize: 22 }}>←</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: theme.colors.text }]}>Passenger details</Text>
        <View style={{ width: 22 }} />
      </View>

      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, paddingBottom: 120 }}>
        <View style={[styles.summary, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface, borderColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
          <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.text, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800' }}>
            {schedule.originCityName} → {schedule.destinationCityName}
          </Text>
          <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, marginTop: 4 }}>
            {schedule.operatorName}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <StandsPicker label="Boarding stand" stops={boardingStops} value={boardingStandId} onChange={setBoardingStandId} />
        <StandsPicker label="Dropping stand" stops={droppingStops} value={droppingStandId} onChange={setDroppingStandId} />

        {passengers.map((p, idx) => (
          <PassengerCard key={p.seatId} index={idx} passenger={p} onChange={(patch) => updatePassenger(idx, patch)} />
        ))}

        {error && (
          <View style={[styles.err, { backgroundColor: theme.isDark ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.15)' : 'rgba(209,106,106,0.15)', borderColor: theme.colors.danger }]}>
            <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.danger, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '600' }}>{error}</Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </ScrollView>

      <View style={[styles.bottomBar, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface, borderTopColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
        <View style={styles.bottomRow}>
          <View>
            <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 11 }}>Total</Text>
            <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.text, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '800', fontFamily: 'monospace' }}>
              ₹{(totalPaise / 100).toFixed(0)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable
            onPress={handleSubmit}
            disabled={submitting}
            style={[styles.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary, opacity: submitting ? 0.6 : 1 }]}
          >
            <Text style={{ color: theme.isDark ? '#000' : '#FFF', fontWeight: '800' }}>
              {submitting ? 'Creating…' : 'Continue to pay'}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  center: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  header: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', padding: 16, paddingTop: 56, borderBottomWidth: 1 },
  title: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '700' },
  summary: { padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 1, marginBottom: 16 },
  err: { padding: 12, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 8 },
  bottomBar: { position: 'absolute', left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, padding: 16, borderTopWidth: 1, paddingBottom: 32 },
  bottomRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },
  primaryBtn: { paddingHorizontal: 24, paddingVertical: 14, borderRadius: 10 },
});

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\PassengerManifestScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { RootStackParamList, ScheduleDetailDTO } from '../types';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth';
import { ApiError, EP, api } from '../lib/api';
import { formatDate, formatMoney, formatTime } from '../lib/format';
import { AppHeader, ScreenContainer } from '../components/layout';
import { Banner, Button, Card, Chip, Input, Select, Skeleton, useToast } from '../components/ui';
import { LockCountdown } from '../features/seats';
import { createBooking } from '../features/booking/api/booking.api';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'PassengerManifest'>;
type Gender = 'MALE' | 'FEMALE' | 'OTHER';
interface Row {
  name: string;
  age: string;
  gender: Gender | null;
}

const GENDERS: Array<{ value: Gender; label: string }> = [
  { value: 'MALE', label: 'Male' },
  { value: 'FEMALE', label: 'Female' },
  { value: 'OTHER', label: 'Other' },
];
const LOCK_CODES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['LOCK_EXPIRED', 'LOCK_NOT_FOUND', 'SEAT_LOCKED', 'SEAT_ALREADY_BOOKED']);

function rowErrors(r: Row, femaleOnly: boolean): { name?: string; age?: string; gender?: string } {
  const out: { name?: string; age?: string; gender?: string } = {};
  if (r.name.trim().length === 0) out.name = 'Enter the passenger name';
  else if (r.name.trim().length > 100) out.name = 'Name is too long';
  if (r.age.length > 0) {
    const n = Number(r.age);
    if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > 119) out.age = 'Enter an age between 1 and 119';
  }
  if (femaleOnly && r.gender !== null && r.gender !== 'FEMALE') out.gender = 'This seat is reserved for women';
  return out;
}

export const PassengerManifestScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation, route }) => {
  const { scheduleId, farePaise, seats, lockExpiresAt } = route.params;
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const toast = useToast();

  const detail = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedule', scheduleId],
    queryFn: () => api.get<ScheduleDetailDTO>(EP.scheduleDetail(scheduleId)),
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });
  const stands = useMemo(() => [...(detail.data?.route.stands ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence), [detail.data]);

  const [boarding, setBoarding] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dropping, setDropping] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>(() =>
    seats.map((s, i) => ({ name: i === 0 ? user?.name ?? '' : '', age: '', gender: s.femaleOnly ? 'FEMALE' : null })),
  );
  const [showErrors, setShowErrors] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [lost, setLost] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const first = stands[0];
    const last = stands[stands.length - 1];
    if (stands.length >= 2 && first && last && !boarding && !dropping) {
      setBoarding(first.stand.id);
      setDropping(last.stand.id);
    }
  }, [stands, boarding, dropping]);

  const seqOf = (id: string | null): number => stands.find((s) => s.stand.id === id)?.sequence ?? -1;
  const boardingOptions = stands.slice(0, -1).map((s) => ({ value: s.stand.id, label: s.stand.name }));
  const droppingOptions = stands.filter((s) => s.sequence > seqOf(boarding)).map((s) => ({ value: s.stand.id, label: s.stand.name }));

  const pickBoarding = (id: string): void => {
    setBoarding(id);
    if (seqOf(dropping) <= seqOf(id)) setDropping(stands[stands.length - 1]?.stand.id ?? null);
  };
  const update = (i: number, patch: Partial<Row>): void => setRows((prev) => prev.map((r, k) => (k === i ? { ...r, ...patch } : r)));

  const errors = rows.map((r, i) => rowErrors(r, seats[i]?.femaleOnly ?? false));
  const invalid = errors.some((e) => e.name || e.age || e.gender) || !boarding || !dropping;

  const submit = async (): Promise<void> => {
    setShowErrors(true);
    const first = seats[0];
    if (invalid || !first || !boarding || !dropping) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await createBooking({
        lockId: first.lockId,
        boardingStandId: boarding,
        droppingStandId: dropping,
        passengers: rows.map((r, i) => ({
          seatId: seats[i]?.seatId ?? '',
          name: r.name.trim(),
          ...(r.age ? { age: Number(r.age) } : {}),
          ...(r.gender ? { gender: r.gender } : {}),
        })),
      });
      navigation.replace('Payment', {
        bookingId: res.bookingId,
        amountPaise: res.totalFarePaise,
        reference: res.bookingReference,
        expiresAt: res.expiresAt,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && LOCK_CODES.has(err.code)) setLost(true);
      else toast.show(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Could not create the booking. Please try again.', 'error');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const subtotal = seats.length * farePaise;

  return (
    <ScreenContainer scroll padded={false}>
      <AppHeader title="Passenger details" onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.sm }}>
        {lost ? (
          <Banner
            type="error"
            message="Your seats are no longer held. Please choose them again."
            action={{ label: 'Choose seats', onPress: () => navigation.goBack() }}
          />
        ) : (
          <LockCountdown expiresAt={new Date(lockExpiresAt).getTime()} onExpire={() => setLost(true)} />
        )}

        {detail.data ? (
          <Card padding={spacing.md}>
            <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>
              {`${detail.data.route.originCity.name} \u2192 ${detail.data.route.destinationCity.name}`}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginTop: 2 }]}>
              {`${formatDate(detail.data.departureTime, 'short')}, ${formatTime(detail.data.departureTime)} - ${detail.data.operatorName}`}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 6 }]}>
              {`Seats ${seats.map((s) => s.label).join(', ')}`}
            </Text>
          </Card>
        ) : detail.isError ? (
          <Banner type="error" message="Could not load the route." action={{ label: 'Retry', onPress: () => void detail.refetch() }} />
        ) : (
          <Skeleton height={88} borderRadius={12} />
        )}

        {stands.length >= 2 ? (
          <Card padding={spacing.md}>
            <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 8 }]}>Boarding and dropping</Text>
            <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
              <Select label="Boarding point" value={boarding} onChange={pickBoarding} options={boardingOptions} placeholder="Choose where you board" />
              <Select label="Dropping point" value={dropping} onChange={setDropping} options={droppingOptions} placeholder="Choose where you get off" />
            </View>
          </Card>
        ) : null}

        {rows.map((r, i) => {
          const seat = seats[i];
          const e = showErrors ? errors[i] : undefined;
          return (
            <Card key={seat?.seatId ?? i} padding={spacing.md}>
              <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textBright, marginBottom: 8 }]}>
                {`Seat ${seat?.label ?? ''}${seat?.femaleOnly ? ' (women only)' : ''}`}
              </Text>
              <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
                <Input label="Full name" value={r.name} onChangeText={(v) => update(i, { name: v })} autoCapitalize="words" maxLength={100} placeholder="As on ID" {...(e?.name ? { error: e.name } : {})} />
                <Input label="Age (optional)" value={r.age} onChangeText={(v) => update(i, { age: v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 3) })} keyboardType="number-pad" maxLength={3} {...(e?.age ? { error: e.age } : {})} />
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  {GENDERS.map((g) => (
                    <Chip
                      key={g.value}
                      label={g.label}
                      selected={r.gender === g.value}
                      disabled={(seat?.femaleOnly ?? false) && g.value !== 'FEMALE'}
                      onPress={() => update(i, { gender: r.gender === g.value ? null : g.value })}
                    />
                  ))}
                </View>
                {e?.gender ? <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.dangerCrimson }]}>{e.gender}</Text> : null}
              </View>
            </Card>
          );
        })}

        <Card padding={spacing.md}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
            <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>{`${seats.length} seat${seats.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} x ${formatMoney(farePaise)}`}</Text>
            <Text style={[typography.monoData, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{formatMoney(subtotal)}</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 6 }]}>Taxes and fees are added on the next step.</Text>
        </Card>

        <Button onPress={() => void submit()} loading={busy} disabled={busy || lost} fullWidth>
          Continue to payment
        </Button>
      </View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default PassengerManifestScreen;


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\PaymentScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { PaymentDTO, RootStackParamList } from '../types';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth';
import { ApiError } from '../lib/api';
import { useWsEvent, useWsTopic } from '../lib/ws';
import { formatBookingReference, formatMoney } from '../lib/format';
import { AppHeader, ScreenContainer } from '../components/layout';
import { Banner, Button, Card, Spinner, useToast } from '../components/ui';
import { LockCountdown } from '../features/seats';
import { bookingKeys, useBooking } from '../features/booking/hooks/useBookings';
import { initiatePayment, mockConfirmPayment } from '../features/payment/api/payment.api';
import { RazorpayCheckout, type CheckoutMessage } from '../features/payment/components/RazorpayCheckout';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Payment'>;
type Phase = 'starting' | 'ready' | 'checkout' | 'confirming' | 'failed';

export const PaymentScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation, route }) => {
  const { bookingId, amountPaise, reference, expiresAt } = route.params;
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const toast = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const [payment, setPayment] = useState<PaymentDTO | null>(null);
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>('starting');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [slow, setSlow] = useState(false);

  const waiting = phase === 'ready' || phase === 'checkout' || phase === 'confirming';
  const booking = useBooking(bookingId, waiting ? 3000 : false);
  const status = booking.data?.status;

  const start = useCallback(async () => {
    setPhase('starting');
    setMessage(null);
    try {
      setPayment(await initiatePayment(bookingId));
      setPhase('ready');
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Could not start the payment. Please try again.');
      setPhase('failed');
    }
  }, [bookingId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    void start();
  }, [start]);

  // The booking is confirmed by the server (gateway webhook). The socket is the fast path, polling the fallback.
  useWsTopic(`booking:${bookingId}`);
  const refresh = useCallback(() => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: bookingKeys.detail(bookingId) });
  }, [qc, bookingId]);
  useWsEvent('booking:confirmed', refresh);
  useWsEvent('booking:expired', refresh);
  useWsEvent('booking:cancelled', refresh);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'CONFIRMED' || status === 'CHECKED_IN') {
      navigation.reset({ index: 1, routes: [{ name: 'Home' }, { name: 'Ticket', params: { bookingId } }] });
    } else if (status === 'EXPIRED' || status === 'FAILED' || status === 'CANCELLED') {
      setPhase('failed');
      setMessage(status === 'EXPIRED' ? 'The payment time ran out and your seats were released.' : 'This booking can no longer be paid.');
    }
  }, [status, bookingId, navigation]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'confirming') {
      setSlow(false);
      return;
    }
    const t = setTimeout(() => setSlow(true), 45_000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [phase]);

  const onCheckout = (m: CheckoutMessage): void => {
    if (m.type === 'success') {
      setPhase('confirming');
      refresh();
    } else if (m.type === 'dismiss') {
      setPhase('ready');
    } else {
      toast.show(m.description ?? 'The payment did not go through. You can try again.', 'error');
      setPhase('ready');
    }
  };

  const simulate = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (!payment) return;
    setPhase('confirming');
    try {
      await mockConfirmPayment(payment.paymentId);
      refresh();
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Could not confirm the test payment.', 'error');
      setPhase('ready');
    }
  };

  const leave = (): void => {
    if (phase === 'confirming') {
      navigation.navigate('MyTickets');
      return;
    }
    Alert.alert('Leave payment?', 'Your seats stay held until the timer ends. You can finish paying from My tickets.', [
      { text: 'Stay', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Leave', onPress: () => navigation.navigate('MyTickets') },
    ]);
  };

  if (phase === 'checkout' && payment?.gateway && !payment.gateway.mock) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: tokens.canvasBg }}>
        <AppHeader title="Secure payment" onBackPress={() => setPhase('ready')} />
        <RazorpayCheckout
          keyId={payment.gateway.keyId}
          orderId={payment.gateway.orderId}
          amountPaise={payment.amountPaise}
          description={`Booking ${formatBookingReference(reference)}`}
          {...(user?.phone ? { contact: user.phone } : {})}
          email={user?.email ?? null}
          onMessage={onCheckout}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ScreenContainer scroll padded={false}>
      <AppHeader title="Payment" onBackPress={leave} />
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.lg}>
          <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>{`Booking ${formatBookingReference(reference)}`}</Text>
          <Text style={[typography.hero, { color: tokens.textBright, marginTop: 4 }]}>{formatMoney(payment?.amountPaise ?? amountPaise)}</Text>
        </Card>

        {waiting ? <LockCountdown expiresAt={new Date(expiresAt).getTime()} onExpire={refresh} /> : null}

        {phase === 'starting' ? (
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: spacing.xl, gap: 12 }}>
            <Spinner size="lg" />
            <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>Preparing secure payment...</Text>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {phase === 'ready' && payment ? (
          payment.gateway?.mock ? (
            <>
              <Banner type="warning" message="Development mode: no real money is taken." />
              <Button onPress={() => void simulate()} fullWidth>
                Simulate successful payment
              </Button>
            </>
          ) : payment.gateway ? (
            <Button onPress={() => setPhase('checkout')} fullWidth>
              {`Pay ${formatMoney(payment.amountPaise)}`}
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Banner type="error" message="Payments are not available right now. Please try again shortly." action={{ label: 'Retry', onPress: () => void start() }} />
          )
        ) : null}

        {phase === 'confirming' ? (
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: spacing.xl, gap: 12 }}>
            <Spinner size="lg" />
            <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary, textAlign: 'center' }]}>Confirming your payment. Please do not close the app.</Text>
            {slow ? (
              <>
                <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, textAlign: 'center' }]}>
                  This is taking longer than usual. If money was deducted, your ticket will appear in My tickets shortly.
                </Text>
                <Button variant="secondary" onPress={() => navigation.navigate('MyTickets')}>
                  Go to My tickets
                </Button>
              </>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {phase === 'failed' ? (
          <>
            <Banner type="error" message={message ?? 'Something went wrong.'} />
            {status === 'PENDING' || status === undefined ? (
              <Button onPress={() => void start()} fullWidth>
                Try again
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Home')} fullWidth>
                Back to home
              </Button>
            )}
          </>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default PaymentScreen;

### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\ProfileScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import type { RootStackParamList } from '../types';
import { useTheme, type ThemePreference } from '../theme';
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth';
import { formatPhone } from '../lib/format';
import { AppHeader, ScreenContainer } from '../components/layout';
import { Avatar, Button, Card, Chip } from '../components/ui';
import { SignInRequired } from '../features/auth';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Profile'>;

const THEMES: Array<{ id: ThemePreference; label: string }> = [
  { id: 'system', label: 'Match device' },
  { id: 'light', label: 'Light' },
  { id: 'dark', label: 'Dark' },
];

export const ProfileScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing, preference, setPreference } = useTheme();
  const { user, isAuthenticated, logout } = useAuth();

  const signOut = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await logout();
    navigation.reset({ index: 0, routes: [{ name: 'Home' }] });
  };

  return (
    <ScreenContainer scroll padded={false}>
      <AppHeader title="Account" onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.sm }}>
        {!isAuthenticated || !user ? (
          <SignInRequired message="Sign in to manage your account." />
        ) : (
          <>
            <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.lg}>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>
                <Avatar name={user.name ?? user.phone} />
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{user.name ?? formatPhone(user.phone)}</Text>
                  <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>{formatPhone(user.phone)}</Text>
                  {user.email ? <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>{user.email}</Text> : null}
                </View>
              </View>
            </Card>
            <Button variant="secondary" onPress={() => navigation.navigate('MyTickets')} fullWidth>
              My tickets
            </Button>
            <Card padding={spacing.md}>
              <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 8 }]}>Appearance</Text>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                {THEMES.map((t) => (
                  <Chip key={t.id} label={t.label} selected={preference === t.id} onPress={() => setPreference(t.id)} />
                ))}
              </View>
            </Card>
            <Button variant="danger" onPress={() => void signOut()} fullWidth>
              Sign out
            </Button>
          </>
        )}
      </View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default ProfileScreen;


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\ResultsScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { ScreenContainer, AppHeader } from '../components/layout';
import { Card, Button, Skeleton, Banner } from '../components/ui';
import { useSearch, ScheduleCard } from '../features/search';
import { formatDate } from '../lib/format';
import type { RootStackParamList } from '../types';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Results'>;

export const ResultsScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation, route }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const {
    originCityId,
    destinationCityId,
    originCityName,
    destinationCityName,
    date,
    sortBy,
  } = route.params;

  const { data: schedules, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useSearch({
    originCityId,
    destinationCityId,
    date,
    ...(sortBy ? { sortBy } : {}),
  });

  const dateLabel = formatDate(`${date}T00:00:00.000Z`, 'day');
  const routeLabel = `${originCityName} to ${destinationCityName}`;

  return (
    <ScreenContainer padded={false} scroll>
      <AppHeader title={routeLabel} onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />
      <View style={{ padding: spacing.md, gap: spacing.md }}>
        <Card padding={spacing.md}>
          <View
            style={{
              flexDirection: 'row',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>
                {dateLabel}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright, marginTop: 2 }]}>
                {routeLabel}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}>
              Edit
            </Button>
          </View>
        </Card>

        {isLoading ? (
          <View style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
            {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
              <Card key={i} padding={spacing.md}>
                <Skeleton width="60%" height={16} />
                <View style={{ height: 12 }} />
                <Skeleton width="100%" height={44} />
                <View style={{ height: 12 }} />
                <Skeleton width="40%" height={20} />
              </Card>
            ))}
          </View>
        ) : isError ? (
          <Banner
            type="error"
            message="Could not load buses. Check your connection and try again."
            action={{ label: 'Retry', onPress: () => refetch() }}
          />
        ) : !schedules || schedules.length === 0 ? (
          <Card padding={spacing.lg}>
            <Text
              style={[typography.h4, { color: tokens.textBright, textAlign: 'center' }]}
            >
              No buses found
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                typography.body,
                { color: tokens.textSecondary, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 8 },
              ]}
            >
              No buses available on {dateLabel}. Try a different date or route.
            </Text>
            <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
              <Button variant="secondary" onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} fullWidth>
                Change search
              </Button>
            </View>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
            <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>
              {schedules.length} {schedules.length === 1 ? 'bus' : 'buses'} available
            </Text>
            {schedules.map((s) => (
              <ScheduleCard
                key={s.scheduleId}
                schedule={s}
                originCityName={originCityName}
                destinationCityName={destinationCityName}
                onSelect={() => {
                  navigation.navigate('SeatSelection', {
                    scheduleId: s.scheduleId,
                    farePaise: s.fare.amountPaise,
                  });
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default ResultsScreen;

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\SearchScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { ScreenContainer, AppHeader } from '../components/layout';
import { Card, Button } from '../components/ui';
import { CityAutocomplete, DateField } from '../features/search';
import type { RootStackParamList, CityDTO } from '../types';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Search'>;

function todayIst(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  const ist = new Date(now.getTime() + 5.5 * 3600 * 1000);
  return ist.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export const SearchScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation }) => {
  const { tokens, typography, spacing } = useTheme();
  const [origin, setOrigin] = useState<CityDTO | null>(null);
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState<CityDTO | null>(null);
  const [date, setDate] = useState(todayIst());

  const sameCity = origin !== null && destination !== null && origin.id === destination.id;
  const canSearch = origin !== null && destination !== null && !sameCity;

  const handleSearch = () => {
    if (!canSearch || !origin || !destination) return;
    navigation.navigate('Results', {
      originCityId: origin.id,
      destinationCityId: destination.id,
      originCityName: origin.name,
      destinationCityName: destination.name,
      date,
    });
  };

  return (
    <ScreenContainer padded={false} scroll>
      <AppHeader title="Search buses" onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />
      <View style={{ padding: spacing.md, gap: spacing.md }}>
        <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.lg}>
          <View style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
            <CityAutocomplete
              label="From"
              placeholder="Select origin city"
              value={origin}
              onChange={setOrigin}
            />
            <CityAutocomplete
              label="To"
              placeholder="Select destination city"
              value={destination}
              onChange={setDestination}
            />
            <DateField label="Travel date" value={date} onChange={setDate} />
            {sameCity && (
              <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.dangerCrimson }]}>
                Origin and destination must be different.
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
        </Card>

        <Button onPress={handleSearch} disabled={!canSearch} fullWidth size="lg">
          Search buses
        </Button>

        <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, textAlign: 'center' }]}>
          Buses are filtered by published schedules on the selected date.
        </Text>
      </View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default SearchScreen;

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\SeatSelectionScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
import { View, Text, ScrollView, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator } from 'react-native';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../theme/passengerAdapter';
import { SeatDeck, SeatLegend, LockCountdown, useSeatDeck } from '../features/seats';
import { fetchScheduleDetail } from '../features/booking';

export function SeatSelectionScreen({ route, navigation }: any) {
  const theme = useCleanTheme();
  const { scheduleId } = route.params;

  const deck = useSeatDeck(scheduleId);
  const { data: schedule } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedule', scheduleId],
    queryFn: () => fetchScheduleDetail(scheduleId),
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });

  const handleContinue = useCallback(() => {
    if (deck.locks.size === 0) return;
    const locks = Array.from(deck.locks.values());
    navigation.navigate('PassengerForm', { scheduleId, locks });
  }, [deck.locks, navigation, scheduleId]);

  if (deck.loading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.center, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.bg }]}>
        <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={theme.colors.primary} />
        <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.textMuted, marginTop: 12 }}>Loading seats…</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const totalPaise = (schedule?.farePaise ?? 0) * deck.locks.size;

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.bg }}>
      <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} hitSlop={12}>
          <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.primary, fontSize: 22 }}>←</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: theme.colors.text }]}>Choose seats</Text>
        <View style={{ width: 22 }} />
      </View>

      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, paddingBottom: 120 }}>
        {schedule && (
          <View style={[styles.infoCard, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface, borderColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.route, { color: theme.colors.text }]}>
              {schedule.originCityName} → {schedule.destinationCityName}
            </Text>
            <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, marginTop: 4 }}>
              {schedule.operatorName} · {schedule.stops.length} stops · ₹{(schedule.farePaise / 100).toFixed(0)}/seat
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}

        {deck.error && (
          <View style={[styles.err, { backgroundColor: theme.isDark ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.15)' : 'rgba(209,106,106,0.15)', borderColor: theme.colors.danger }]}>
            <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.danger, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '600' }}>{deck.error}</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <SeatLegend />
        </View>

        {deck.layout && deck.seats.length > 0 ? (
          <SeatDeck
            seats={deck.seats}
            layout={deck.layout}
            selectedSeatIds={deck.selectedSeatIds}
            onSeatClick={deck.toggle}
            disabled={!!deck.togglingSeatId}
          />
        ) : (
          <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.textMuted, textAlign: 'center', padding: 40 }}>
            No seats available.
          </Text>
        )}
      </ScrollView>

      {deck.locks.size > 0 && (
        <View style={[styles.bottomBar, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface, borderTopColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
          {deck.earliestExpiry && (
            <LockCountdown expiresAt={deck.earliestExpiry} onExpire={deck.handleExpire} />
          )}
          <View style={styles.bottomRow}>
            <View>
              <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 11 }}>{deck.locks.size} seat{deck.locks.size > 1 ? 's' : ''}</Text>
              <Text style={{ color: theme.colors.text, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '800', fontFamily: 'monospace' }}>
                ₹{(totalPaise / 100).toFixed(0)}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Pressable
              onPress={handleContinue}
              style={[styles.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary }]}
            >
              <Text style={{ color: theme.isDark ? '#000' : '#FFF', fontWeight: '800' }}>
                Continue ({deck.locks.size})
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  center: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  header: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', padding: 16, paddingTop: 56, borderBottomWidth: 1 },
  title: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '700' },
  infoCard: { padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 1, marginBottom: 16 },
  route: { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '800' },
  err: { padding: 12, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, marginBottom: 12 },
  bottomBar: { position: 'absolute', left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, padding: 16, borderTopWidth: 1, paddingBottom: 32 },
  bottomRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 8 },
  primaryBtn: { paddingHorizontal: 24, paddingVertical: 14, borderRadius: 10 },
});

================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\screens\TicketScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import type { RootStackParamList } from '../types';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth';
import { formatBookingReference, formatDate, formatMoney, formatTime } from '../lib/format';
import { AppHeader, ScreenContainer } from '../components/layout';
import { Badge, Banner, Button, Card, Skeleton } from '../components/ui';
import { SignInRequired } from '../features/auth';
import { useBooking, useTicket } from '../features/booking/hooks/useBookings';
import { canCancel, statusMeta } from '../features/booking/status';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Ticket'>;

const Row: React.FC<{ label: string; value: string; strong?: boolean }> = ({ label, value, strong = false }) => {
  const { tokens, typography } = useTheme();
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingVertical: 4 }}>
      <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary }]}>{label}</Text>
      <Text style={[strong ? typography.monoData : typography.body, { color: tokens.textBright }]}>{value}</Text>
    </View>
  );
};

export const TicketScreen: React.FC<Props> = ({ navigation, route }) => {
  const { bookingId } = route.params;
  const { tokens, typography, spacing, radius } = useTheme();
  const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();

  // While the payment is still landing, keep checking.
  const [poll, setPoll] = useState<number | false>(false);
  const booking = useBooking(bookingId, poll);
  const b = booking.data;
  useEffect(() => {
    setPoll(b?.status === 'PENDING' ? 4000 : false);
  }, [b?.status]);

  const live = b?.status === 'CONFIRMED' || b?.status === 'CHECKED_IN' || b?.status === 'COMPLETED';
  const ticket = useTicket(bookingId, live && !!b?.ticket);
  const qrVisible = ticket.data && (ticket.data.status === 'ISSUED' || ticket.data.status === 'ACTIVE');

  const body = (): React.ReactNode => {
    if (!isAuthenticated) return <SignInRequired message="Sign in to see your tickets." />;
    if (booking.isError) {
      return <Banner type="error" message="Could not load this ticket." action={{ label: 'Retry', onPress: () => void booking.refetch() }} />;
    }
    if (!b) return <Skeleton height={420} borderRadius={18} />;

    const meta = statusMeta(b.status);
    const trackable = !!b.trip && (b.status === 'CONFIRMED' || b.status === 'CHECKED_IN') && (b.trip.status === 'SCHEDULED' || b.trip.status === 'IN_PROGRESS');

    return (
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.lg}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <Text style={[typography.monoSmall, { color: tokens.textMuted }]}>{formatBookingReference(b.bookingReference)}</Text>
            <Badge variant={meta.variant}>{meta.label}</Badge>
          </View>
          <Text style={[typography.h3, { color: tokens.textBright, marginTop: 10 }]}>
            {`${b.routeSummary.originCity} \u2192 ${b.routeSummary.destinationCity}`}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[typography.body, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginTop: 2 }]}>
            {`${formatDate(b.departureTime, 'full')}, ${formatTime(b.departureTime)}`}
          </Text>
        </Card>

        {b.status === 'PENDING' ? (
          <Banner
            type="warning"
            message="Payment is not complete yet."
            action={{
              label: 'Pay now',
              onPress: () =>
                navigation.navigate('Payment', {
                  bookingId: b.bookingId,
                  amountPaise: b.totalFarePaise,
                  reference: b.bookingReference,
                  expiresAt: b.expiresAt ?? new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60_000).toISOString(),
                }),
            }}
          />
        ) : null}

        {qrVisible && ticket.data ? (
          <Card elevation="elevated" padding={spacing.lg}>
            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <View style={{ backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', padding: 14, borderRadius: radius.lg }}>
                <QRCode value={ticket.data.qrPayload} size={208} color="#0B1830" backgroundColor="#FFFFFF" />
              </View>
              <Text style={[typography.caption, { color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 10, textAlign: 'center' }]}>
                Show this code to the driver when you board.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </Card>
        ) : live && b.ticket && ticket.isLoading ? (
          <Skeleton height={260} borderRadius={18} />
        ) : null}

        <Card padding={spacing.md}>
          <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 6 }]}>Journey</Text>
          <Row label="Board at" value={b.boardingStand.name} />
          <Row label="Get off at" value={b.droppingStand.name} />
          <Row label="Arrives" value={`${formatDate(b.arrivalTime, 'short')}, ${formatTime(b.arrivalTime)}`} />
        </Card>

        <Card padding={spacing.md}>
          <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 6 }]}>Passengers</Text>
          {b.passengers.map((p) => (
            <Row
              key={p.seatId}
              label={`${p.name}${p.age ? `, ${p.age}` : ''}`}
              value={`Seat ${p.seatLabel}`}
            />
          ))}
        </Card>

        <Card padding={spacing.md}>
          <Text style={[typography.label, { color: tokens.textSecondary, marginBottom: 6 }]}>Fare</Text>
          <Row label="Base fare" value={formatMoney(b.fareBreakdown.baseFarePaise)} />
          <Row label="Taxes" value={formatMoney(b.fareBreakdown.taxPaise)} />
          <Row label="Convenience fee" value={formatMoney(b.fareBreakdown.convenienceFeePaise)} />
          <Row label="Total" value={formatMoney(b.fareBreakdown.totalPaise)} strong />
        </Card>

        {trackable && b.trip ? (
          <Button onPress={() => navigation.navigate('LiveTracking', { tripId: b.trip?.tripId ?? '', bookingId: b.bookingId })} fullWidth>
            Track my bus
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {canCancel(b) ? (
          <Button variant="danger" onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Cancel', { bookingId: b.bookingId })} fullWidth>
            Cancel booking
          </Button>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <ScreenContainer scroll padded={false}>
      <AppHeader title="Your ticket" onBackPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.xl }}>{body()}</View>
    </ScreenContainer>
  );
};

export default TicketScreen;

import { Easing } from 'react-native-reanimated';

// Motion durations from 05-UI-SPEC §15.2 (matches Design_guide.html §1 --ease-*).
// Values are milliseconds. Easing curves use Reanimated's Easing API.

export const motion = {
  duration: {
    instant: 100,
    fast: 150,
    smooth: 250,
    bounce: 400,
    long: 500,
    shimmer: 1500,
    lockedBreath: 2200,
    beaconPulse: 1200,
  },
  easing: {
    standard: Easing.bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
    smooth: Easing.bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
    bounce: Easing.bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1),
    linear: Easing.linear,
    inOut: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease),
  },
} as const;

export type Motion = typeof motion;


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\theme\passengerAdapter.ts
================================================================================
/**
 * Safe theme adapter. The existing ThemeContext might return:
 *   - { theme, mode, setMode, cycleMode }  (like driver-app)
 *   - { theme }                            (theme direct)
 *   - theme directly                       (colors object)
 * This adapter normalizes to a stable `{ isDark, colors }` shape.
 */
import { useTheme } from './ThemeContext';

const FALLBACK = {
  isDark: true,
  colors: {
    bg: '#1A1410',
    surface: '#251C16',
    surfaceElevated: '#2E251E',
    border: '#3D2E22',
    borderSubtle: '#2B2118',
    primary: '#F59E0B',
    primaryDim: '#D97706',
    text: '#FEF9F3',
    textMuted: '#C4A57B',
    danger: '#EF4444',
    warning: '#FCD34D',
    success: '#4ADE80',
  },
};

export function useCleanTheme(): typeof FALLBACK {
  try {
    const ctx: any = useTheme();
    const theme = ctx?.theme ?? ctx;
    if (theme?.colors) {
      return {
        isDark: !!theme.isDark,
        colors: {
          bg: theme.colors.bg ?? FALLBACK.colors.bg,
          surface: theme.colors.surface ?? FALLBACK.colors.surface,
          surfaceElevated: theme.colors.surfaceElevated ?? theme.colors.surface ?? FALLBACK.colors.surfaceElevated,
          border: theme.colors.border ?? FALLBACK.colors.border,
          borderSubtle: theme.colors.borderSubtle ?? theme.colors.border ?? FALLBACK.colors.borderSubtle,
          primary: theme.colors.primary ?? FALLBACK.colors.primary,
          primaryDim: theme.colors.primaryDim ?? theme.colors.primary ?? FALLBACK.colors.primaryDim,
          text: theme.colors.text ?? FALLBACK.colors.text,
          textMuted: theme.colors.textMuted ?? FALLBACK.colors.textMuted,
          danger: theme.colors.danger ?? FALLBACK.colors.danger,
          warning: theme.colors.warning ?? FALLBACK.colors.warning,
          success: theme.colors.success ?? FALLBACK.colors.success,
        },
      };
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[useCleanTheme] theme context unavailable, using fallback:', e);
  }
  return FALLBACK;
}

  '4xl': 96,
