================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\theme\ThemeContext.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useColorScheme } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { brandTokens, BrandTokens, ThemeMode } from './tokens';
import { typography, Typography } from './typography';
import { spacing, radius, Spacing, Radius } from './spacing';
import { motion, Motion } from './motion';

const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = '@busverse/theme';

export type ThemePreference = 'light' | 'dark' | 'system';

interface ThemeContextValue {
  theme: ThemeMode;              // resolved theme actually rendered
  preference: ThemePreference;   // what the user chose (system = follow OS)
  tokens: BrandTokens;
  typography: Typography;
  spacing: Spacing;
  radius: Radius;
  motion: Motion;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
  setPreference: (pref: ThemePreference) => void;
  isReady: boolean;
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

function resolveTheme(pref: ThemePreference, system: 'light' | 'dark' | null | undefined): ThemeMode {
  if (pref === 'light') return 'light';
  if (pref === 'dark') return 'dark';
  return system === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark';
}

export const ThemeProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const systemScheme = useColorScheme();
  const [preference, setPreferenceState] = useState<ThemePreference>('system');
  const [isReady, setIsReady] = useState(false);

  // Restore saved preference on mount. Per 05-UI-SPEC §22.2, dark is the
  // first-run default; system-follow applies until the user picks explicitly.
  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const stored = await AsyncStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' || stored === 'system') {
          setPreferenceState(stored);
        }
      } catch {
        /* storage failure = fall back to 'system' */
      } finally {
        setIsReady(true);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  const setPreference = useCallback((next: ThemePreference) => {
    setPreferenceState(next);
    AsyncStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, next).catch(() => {
      /* non-fatal: preference will re-fall back on next launch */
    });
  }, []);

  const theme = resolveTheme(preference, systemScheme);
  const tokens = brandTokens[theme];

  const toggleTheme = useCallback(() => {
    setPreference(theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark');
  }, [theme, setPreference]);

  const value: ThemeContextValue = {
    theme,
    preference,
    tokens,
    typography,
    spacing,
    radius,
    motion,
    toggleTheme,
    setPreference,
    isReady,
  };

  return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>;
};

export function useTheme(): ThemeContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider');
  return ctx;
}


================================================================================
### apps\passenger-app\src\theme\tokens.ts
================================================================================
// Full design-token catalog from Design_guide.html §1 + §4.
// Consumed by ThemeContext; never import tokens directly in components —
// always use useTheme().tokens so theme switching works reactively.

export type BrandTokens = {
  // ── Canvas ──
  canvasBg: string;
  canvasSubtle: string;
  // ── Surface ──
  surfaceCard: string;
  surfaceElevated: string;
  surfaceHover: string;
  surfaceActive: string;
  surfaceSunken: string;
  // ── Typography ──
  textBright: string;
  textPrimary: string;
  textSecondary: string;
  textMuted: string;
  textInverse: string;
  textBrand: string;
  // ── Borders ──
  borderHairline: string;
  borderSubtle: string;
  borderStrong: string;
  borderFocus: string;
  // ── Brand primary ──
  brandPrimary: string;
  brandPrimaryHover: string;
  brandPrimaryActive: string;
  brandPrimarySubtle: string;
  brandPrimaryBorder: string;
  // ── Semantic ──
  telemetryCyan: string;
  telemetryCyanSubtle: string;
  signalEmerald: string;
  signalEmeraldSubtle: string;
  warningAmber: string;
  warningAmberSubtle: string;
  dangerCrimson: string;
  dangerCrimsonSubtle: string;
  // ── Seats ──
  seatAvailableBg: string;
  seatAvailableBorder: string;
  seatAvailableText: string;
  seatSelectedBg: string;
  seatSelectedBorder: string;
  seatSelectedText: string;
  seatLockedBg: string;
  seatLockedBorder: string;
  seatLockedText: string;
  seatBookedBg: string;
  seatBookedBorder: string;
  seatBookedText: string;
  seatFemaleBg: string;
  seatFemaleBorder: string;
  seatFemaleText: string;
  // ── Logo mapping (Design_guide §2) ──
  logoPrimary: string;
  logoVehicle: string;
  logoPin: string;
  logoTypoBus: string;
  logoTypoVerse: string;
  logoCanvasFill: string;
  // ── Shadows (RN-compatible, Design_guide §4) ──
  shadowAmbient: {
    shadowColor: string;
    shadowOffset: { width: number; height: number };
    shadowOpacity: number;
    shadowRadius: number;
    elevation: number;
  };
  shadowFloating: {
    shadowColor: string;
    shadowOffset: { width: number; height: number };
    shadowOpacity: number;
    shadowRadius: number;

  data: T;
  meta?: { requestId?: string; nextCursor?: string | null };
}

export interface ApiErrorEnvelope {
  error: {
    code: string;
    message: string;
    details?: Record<string, unknown>;
    requestId?: string;
  };
}

================================================================================
### apps\busverse-v2\src\lib\api\client.ts
================================================================================
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public status: number,
    public code: string,
    message: string,
    public details?: unknown,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
  }
}

interface RequestOptions extends RequestInit {
  skipAuth?: boolean;
  /** Error codes expected by the caller — logged as debug, not error */
  expectedCodes?: string[];
}

let accessToken: string | null = null;
let onUnauthorized: (() => void) | null = null;
let refreshInFlight: Promise<string> | null = null;

export function setAccessToken(t: string | null) {
  accessToken = t;
  console.log('[session] accessToken ' + (t ? 'SET' : 'cleared'));
}
export function getAccessToken() { return accessToken; }
export function setUnauthorizedHandler(fn: (() => void) | null) { onUnauthorized = fn; }

interface RefreshAttemptResult {
  ok: boolean;
  token?: string;
  definitive401?: boolean;
}

async function doRefreshOnce(): Promise<RefreshAttemptResult> {
  const refreshToken = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('bv_refresh_token') : null;
  if (!refreshToken) return { ok: false };

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${CONFIG.API_BASE}/passenger/auth/refresh`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ refreshToken }),
    });

    if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
      return { ok: false, definitive401: true };
    }
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };

    const payload = await res.json();
    const data = payload?.data ?? payload;
    accessToken = data.accessToken;
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem('bv_refresh_token', data.refreshToken);
    }
    return { ok: true, token: data.accessToken };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false };
  }
}

export async function refreshAccessToken(): Promise<string> {
  if (refreshInFlight) return refreshInFlight;

  refreshInFlight = (async () => {
    let result = await doRefreshOnce();
    if (!result.ok && !result.definitive401) {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
      result = await doRefreshOnce();
    }
    if (!result.ok) {
      throw new ApiError(
        401,
        result.definitive401 ? 'REFRESH_FAILED' : 'REFRESH_UNAVAILABLE',
        result.definitive401 ? 'Session expired' : 'Could not refresh session',
      );
    }
    return result.token!;
  })().finally(() => { refreshInFlight = null; });

  return refreshInFlight;
}

async function request<T>(path: string, init: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  const url = CONFIG.API_BASE + path;
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { ...(init.headers as Record<string, string>) };
  if (init.body && !headers['Content-Type']) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  if (!init.skipAuth && accessToken) headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + accessToken;

  let res = await fetch(url, { ...init, headers });

  if (res.status === 401 && !init.skipAuth) {
    try {
      const newToken = await refreshAccessToken();
      headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + newToken;
      res = await fetch(url, { ...init, headers });
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err?.code === 'REFRESH_FAILED') {
        if (onUnauthorized) onUnauthorized();
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  const text = await res.text();
  const data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;

  if (!res.ok) {
    const code = data?.error?.code ?? 'HTTP_' + res.status;
    const msg = data?.error?.message ?? 'Request failed';
    const details = data?.error?.details;

    const isExpected = (init.expectedCodes ?? []).includes(code) || (init.expectedCodes ?? []).includes('*');

    if (isExpected) {
      // Handled by caller — suppress dev overlay
      console.debug(`[API] ${code} ${res.status} ${path} (expected)`);
    } else {
      console.error(`[API] ${code} ${res.status} ${path}`);
      if (details !== undefined) console.error('[API] Details:', details);
    }

    throw new ApiError(res.status, code, msg, details);
  }

  return (data?.data !== undefined ? data.data : data) as T;
}

export const api = {
  get: <T>(p: string, opts: RequestOptions = {}) => request<T>(p, { ...opts, method: 'GET' }),
  post: <T>(p: string, body: unknown, opts: RequestOptions = {}) => request<T>(p, { ...opts, method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  put: <T>(p: string, body: unknown, opts: RequestOptions = {}) => request<T>(p, { ...opts, method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  patch: <T>(p: string, body: unknown, opts: RequestOptions = {}) => request<T>(p, { ...opts, method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  delete: <T>(p: string, opts: RequestOptions = {}) => request<T>(p, { ...opts, method: 'DELETE' }),
};

### apps\busverse-v2\src\lib\api\search.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';

export interface City {
  cityId: string;
  name: string;
  state?: string;
}

export interface ScheduleResult {
  scheduleId: string;
  operatorName: string;
  busType?: string;
  busModel?: string;
  departureTime: string;
  arrivalTime: string;
  durationMinutes: number;
  farePaise: number;
  availableSeats?: number;
  originCityName: string;
  destinationCityName: string;
  originStandName?: string;
  destinationStandName?: string;
}

interface RawCity {
  cityId?: string;
  id?: string;
  name: string;
  state?: string;
}

interface RawSchedule {
  scheduleId?: string;
  id?: string;
  operatorName?: string;
  operator?: { companyName?: string; name?: string };
  busType?: string;
  bus?: { model?: string; busType?: string };
  departureTime?: string;
  arrivalTime?: string;
  durationMinutes?: number;
  fare?: { amountPaise?: number; currency?: string } | number;
  fareAmountPaise?: number;
  availableSeats?: number;
  seatsAvailable?: number;
  route?: {
    originCity?: { name?: string };
    destinationCity?: { name?: string };
    stands?: Array<{ name?: string; sequence?: number; stand?: { name?: string } }>;
  };
  originCity?: { name?: string };
  destinationCity?: { name?: string };
  originStand?: { name?: string };
  destinationStand?: { name?: string };
}

function mapCity(raw: RawCity): City | null {
  const id = raw.cityId ?? raw.id;
  if (!id || !raw.name) return null;
  return { cityId: id, name: raw.name, state: raw.state };
}

function mapSchedule(raw: RawSchedule): ScheduleResult | null {
  const id = raw.scheduleId ?? raw.id;
  if (!id) return null;

  const dep = raw.departureTime ?? '';
  const arr = raw.arrivalTime ?? '';
  const farePaise =
    typeof raw.fare === 'number'
      ? raw.fare
      : raw.fare?.amountPaise ?? raw.fareAmountPaise ?? 0;

  const originName =
    raw.route?.originCity?.name ??
    raw.originCity?.name ??
    raw.originStand?.name ??
    'Origin';

  const destName =
    raw.route?.destinationCity?.name ??
    raw.destinationCity?.name ??
    raw.destinationStand?.name ??
    'Destination';

  const opName =
    raw.operatorName ??
    raw.operator?.companyName ??
    raw.operator?.name ??
    'Operator';

  const seats =
    raw.availableSeats ?? raw.seatsAvailable ?? undefined;

  const busType = raw.busType ?? raw.bus?.busType;
  const busModel = raw.bus?.model;

  let duration = raw.durationMinutes ?? 0;
  if (!duration && dep && arr) {
    duration = Math.round((new Date(arr).getTime() - new Date(dep).getTime()) / 60000);
  }

  return {
    scheduleId: id,
    operatorName: opName,
    busType,
    busModel,
    departureTime: dep,
    arrivalTime: arr,
    durationMinutes: duration,
    farePaise,
    availableSeats: seats,
    originCityName: originName,
    destinationCityName: destName,
  };
}

export const searchApi = {
  async cities(q: string): Promise<City[]> {
    if (!q || q.trim().length < 2) return [];
    const data = await api.get<RawCity[] | { data?: RawCity[] }>(
      `/public/cities?q=${encodeURIComponent(q.trim())}`,
      { skipAuth: true },
    );
    const list = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.data ?? [];
    return list.map(mapCity).filter((c): c is City => !!c);
  },

  async search(params: { originCityId: string; destinationCityId: string; date: string }): Promise<ScheduleResult[]> {
    const qs = new URLSearchParams({
      originCityId: params.originCityId,
      destinationCityId: params.destinationCityId,
      date: params.date,
    }).toString();
    const data = await api.get<RawSchedule[] | { data?: RawSchedule[] }>(
      `/public/search?${qs}`,
      { skipAuth: true },
    );
    const list = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.data ?? [];
    return list.map(mapSchedule).filter((s): s is ScheduleResult => !!s);
  },
};

### apps\busverse-v2\src\lib\api\bookings.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api, ApiError } from '@/lib/api/client';

export interface Stand {
  standId: string;
  name: string;
  sequence: number;
}

export interface ScheduleDetail {
  scheduleId: string;
  departureTime: string;
  arrivalTime: string;
  farePaise: number;
  operatorName: string;
  originCityName: string;
  destinationCityName: string;
  busType?: string;
  stops: Stand[];
}

export interface CreateBookingInput {
  scheduleId: string;
  lockIds: string[];
  boardingStandId: string;
  droppingStandId: string;
  passengers: Array<{
    seatId: string;
    name: string;
    age: number;
    gender: 'MALE' | 'FEMALE' | 'OTHER';
  }>;
}

export interface CreateBookingResponse {
  bookingId: string;
  status: 'PENDING' | 'CONFIRMED';
  totalFarePaise?: number;
  expiresAt?: string;
}

export interface PassengerSummary {
  seatId: string;
  seatLabel?: string;
  name: string;
  age?: number;
  gender?: string;
}

export interface BookingDetail {
  bookingId: string;
  displayId?: string;
  bookingReference: string;
  status: string;
  scheduleId?: string;
  totalFarePaise: number;
  confirmedAt?: string;
  createdAt?: string;
  expiresAt?: string;
  gstInvoiceAvailable?: boolean;
  boardingStandId?: string;
  droppingStandId?: string;
  passengers: PassengerSummary[];
  schedule?: ScheduleDetail;
}

export interface Ticket {
  ticketId: string;
  bookingId: string;
  qrPayload: string;
  status: string;
  issuedAt: string;
}

function toFarePaise(raw: any): number {
  if (typeof raw?.fare === 'number') return raw.fare;
  if (raw?.fare?.amountPaise) return raw.fare.amountPaise;
  if (typeof raw?.fareAmountPaise === 'number') return raw.fareAmountPaise;
  if (typeof raw?.totalFarePaise === 'number') return raw.totalFarePaise;
  return 0;
}

function toStops(raw: any): Stand[] {
  const list: any[] = raw?.route?.stands ?? raw?.route?.stops ?? raw?.stands ?? raw?.stops ?? [];
  const mapped = list.map((s: any, idx: number) => {
    const nested = s?.stand ?? {};
    return {
      standId: String(s?.standId ?? nested.id ?? nested.standId ?? s?.id ?? ''),
      name: String(s?.name ?? nested.name ?? s?.standName ?? `Stop ${idx + 1}`),
      sequence: Number(s?.sequence ?? s?.seq ?? idx + 1),
    };
  }).filter((s) => !!s.standId);
  mapped.sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence);
  return mapped;
}

function toScheduleDetail(raw: any): ScheduleDetail {
  if (!raw) {
    return {
      scheduleId: '',
      departureTime: '',
      arrivalTime: '',
      farePaise: 0,
      operatorName: 'Operator',
      originCityName: 'Origin',
      destinationCityName: 'Destination',
      stops: [],
    };
  }
  return {
    scheduleId: String(raw?.scheduleId ?? raw?.id ?? ''),
    departureTime: raw?.departureTime ?? '',
    arrivalTime: raw?.arrivalTime ?? '',
    farePaise: toFarePaise(raw),
    operatorName: raw?.operatorName ?? raw?.operator?.companyName ?? raw?.operator?.name ?? 'Operator',
    originCityName:
      raw?.route?.originCity?.name ??
      raw?.originCityName ??
      raw?.originCity?.name ??
      'Origin',
    destinationCityName:
      raw?.route?.destinationCity?.name ??
      raw?.destinationCityName ??
      raw?.destinationCity?.name ??
      'Destination',
    busType: raw?.busType ?? raw?.bus?.busType,
    stops: toStops(raw),
  };
}

function toBookingDetail(raw: any): BookingDetail {
  const scheduleId =
    raw?.scheduleId ??
    raw?.schedule?.scheduleId ??
    raw?.schedule?.id ??
    raw?.trip?.scheduleId ??
    undefined;

  const embeddedSchedule =
    raw?.schedule ??
    raw?.trip?.schedule ??
    undefined;

  return {
    bookingId: String(raw?.bookingId ?? raw?.id ?? ''),
    displayId: raw?.displayId,
    bookingReference: String(raw?.bookingReference ?? raw?.reference ?? ''),
    status: String(raw?.status ?? 'PENDING'),
    scheduleId: scheduleId ? String(scheduleId) : undefined,
    totalFarePaise: toFarePaise(raw),
    confirmedAt: raw?.confirmedAt,
    createdAt: raw?.createdAt,
    expiresAt: raw?.expiresAt,
    gstInvoiceAvailable: !!raw?.gstInvoiceAvailable,
    boardingStandId: raw?.boardingStandId,
    droppingStandId: raw?.droppingStandId,
    passengers: (raw?.passengers ?? []).map((p: any) => ({
      seatId: String(p?.seatId ?? ''),
      seatLabel: p?.seatLabel ?? p?.label,
      name: String(p?.name ?? ''),
      age: p?.age,
      gender: p?.gender,
    })),
    schedule: embeddedSchedule ? toScheduleDetail(embeddedSchedule) : undefined,
  };
}

export const bookingsApi = {
  async getScheduleDetail(scheduleId: string): Promise<ScheduleDetail> {
    const raw = await api.get<any>(`/public/schedules/${scheduleId}`, { skipAuth: true });
    return toScheduleDetail(raw);
  },

  async createBooking(input: CreateBookingInput): Promise<CreateBookingResponse> {
    if (input.lockIds.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('No seat locks — please select a seat first.');
    }

    const payload = {
      lockId: input.lockIds[0],
      boardingStandId: input.boardingStandId,
      droppingStandId: input.droppingStandId,
      passengers: input.passengers.map((p) => ({
        seatId: p.seatId,
        name: p.name.trim(),
        age: p.age,
        gender: p.gender,
      })),
    };

    console.log('[bookings.api] createBooking payload:', JSON.stringify(payload));

    try {
      const raw = await api.post<any>('/passenger/bookings', payload);
      return {
        bookingId: String(raw.bookingId ?? raw.id ?? ''),
        status: (raw.status ?? 'PENDING') as 'PENDING' | 'CONFIRMED',
        totalFarePaise: raw.totalFarePaise,
        expiresAt: raw.expiresAt,
      };
    } catch (err: any) {
      const apiErr = err as ApiError;
      if ((apiErr.code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' || apiErr.status === 422) && input.lockIds.length > 1) {
        console.log('[bookings.api] Multi-seat rejected — falling back to per-seat');
        return await createPerSeatBookings(input);
      }
      throw err;
    }
  },

  async getBooking(bookingId: string): Promise<BookingDetail> {
    const raw = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}`);
    console.log('[bookings.api] getBooking raw:', JSON.stringify(raw, null, 2));
    return toBookingDetail(raw);
  },

  async getTicket(bookingId: string): Promise<Ticket> {
    const raw = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}/ticket`);
    return {
      ticketId: String(raw?.ticketId ?? raw?.id ?? ''),
      bookingId: String(raw?.bookingId ?? bookingId),
      qrPayload: String(raw?.qrPayload ?? raw?.qr_content ?? ''),
      status: String(raw?.status ?? 'ACTIVE'),
      issuedAt: String(raw?.issuedAt ?? ''),
    };
  },

  async listBookings(opts?: { limit?: number }): Promise<BookingDetail[]> {
    const limit = opts?.limit ?? 20;
    const raw = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/bookings?limit=${limit}`);
    const list = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw?.bookings ?? raw?.data ?? [];
    return list.map(toBookingDetail);
  },

  async getTripIdForBooking(bookingId: string): Promise<string | null> {
    try {
      const b = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}`);
      const candidate = b?.tripId ?? b?.trip?.id ?? b?.trip?.tripId;
      if (candidate) return String(candidate);
    } catch {}
    try {
      const t = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/bookings/${bookingId}/trip`);
      const candidate = t?.tripId ?? t?.id ?? t?.trip?.id;
      if (candidate) return String(candidate);
    } catch {}
    return null;
  },
};

async function createPerSeatBookings(input: CreateBookingInput): Promise<CreateBookingResponse> {
  const results: CreateBookingResponse[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < input.passengers.length; i++) {
    const passenger = input.passengers[i];
    const lockId = input.lockIds[i];
    if (!lockId) continue;
    const raw = await api.post<any>('/passenger/bookings', {
      lockId,
      boardingStandId: input.boardingStandId,
      droppingStandId: input.droppingStandId,
      passengers: [{
        seatId: passenger.seatId,
        name: passenger.name.trim(),
        age: passenger.age,
        gender: passenger.gender,
      }],
    });
    results.push({
      bookingId: String(raw.bookingId ?? raw.id ?? ''),
      status: (raw.status ?? 'PENDING') as 'PENDING' | 'CONFIRMED',
      totalFarePaise: raw.totalFarePaise,
      expiresAt: raw.expiresAt,
    });
  }
  if (results.length === 0) throw new Error('No bookings created.');
  return results[0];
}

### apps\busverse-v2\src\lib\api\payments.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';

export interface InitiatePaymentResponse {
  paymentId: string;
  checkoutUrl?: string;
  status: 'INITIATED' | 'PENDING' | 'SUCCEEDED' | 'FAILED';
  amountPaise?: number;
  expiresAt?: string;
}

export interface PaymentStatusResponse {
  paymentId: string;
  status: 'INITIATED' | 'PENDING' | 'SUCCEEDED' | 'FAILED';
  bookingId?: string;
  amountPaise?: number;
  method?: string;
}

/**
 * Generates a UUID v4 to use as an Idempotency-Key.
 * Backend requires min 8 chars; UUID is 36 chars.
 */
function generateIdempotencyKey(): string {
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof (crypto as any).randomUUID === 'function') {
    return (crypto as any).randomUUID();
  }
  // Fallback for older browsers — RFC 4122 v4
  return 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, (c) => {
    const r = (Math.random() * 16) | 0;
    const v = c === 'x' ? r : (r & 0x3) | 0x8;
    return v.toString(16);
  });
}

function normalizePayment(raw: any): PaymentStatusResponse {
  return {
    paymentId: String(raw?.paymentId ?? raw?.id ?? ''),
    status: (raw?.status ?? 'INITIATED') as PaymentStatusResponse['status'],
    bookingId: raw?.bookingId,
    amountPaise: raw?.amountPaise ?? raw?.amount,
    method: raw?.method,
  };
}

export const paymentsApi = {
  async initiate(bookingId: string): Promise<InitiatePaymentResponse> {
    // ⭐ Backend requires a fresh Idempotency-Key per payment attempt
    const idempotencyKey = generateIdempotencyKey();
    console.log(
      '[payments] Initiating payment with Idempotency-Key:',
      idempotencyKey.slice(0, 8) + '…',
    );

    const raw = await api.post<any>(
      '/passenger/payments',
      { bookingId },
      {
        headers: {
          'Idempotency-Key': idempotencyKey,
        },
      },
    );

    return {
      paymentId: String(raw.paymentId ?? raw.id ?? ''),
      checkoutUrl: raw.checkoutUrl,
      status: (raw.status ?? 'INITIATED') as InitiatePaymentResponse['status'],
      amountPaise: raw.amountPaise ?? raw.amount,
      expiresAt: raw.expiresAt,
    };
  },

  async getStatus(paymentId: string): Promise<PaymentStatusResponse> {
    const raw = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/payments/${paymentId}`);
    return normalizePayment(raw);
  },

  /** Dev-only: simulates the payment.captured webhook */
  async mockConfirm(paymentId: string): Promise<void> {
    await api.post('/dev/mock-confirm-payment', { paymentId });
  },
};

### apps\busverse-v2\src\lib\ws\socket.ts
================================================================================
import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client';
import { refreshAccessToken } from '@/lib/api/client';

const WS_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WS_URL || 'http://localhost:4310';

let socket: Socket | null = null;
let currentToken: string | null = null;

export function connectSocket(token: string): Socket {
  currentToken = token;

  if (socket?.connected) {
    return socket;
  }

  socket = io(WS_URL, {
    path: '/v1/ws',
    transports: ['websocket', 'polling'],
    reconnectionDelay: 1000,
    reconnectionDelayMax: 16000,
    reconnectionAttempts: 5,
  });

  socket.on('connect', () => {
    console.log('[ws] connected, authenticating…');
    socket!.emit('message', { action: 'auth', token: currentToken });
  });

  socket.on('auth:ok', (payload: any) => {
    console.log('[ws] auth ok:', payload?.role);
  });

  socket.on('auth:expiring', async () => {
    console.log('[ws] token expiring, refreshing…');
    try {
      const newToken = await refreshAccessToken();
      currentToken = newToken;
      const refreshToken =
        typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('bv_refresh_token') : null;
      socket!.emit('message', { action: 'auth:refresh', refreshToken });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[ws] auth refresh failed:', err);
    }
  });

  socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
    console.log('[ws] disconnected:', reason);
  });

  socket.on('connect_error', (err) => {
    console.warn('[ws] connect error:', err.message);
  });

  return socket;
}

export function subscribe(topics: string[]) {
  socket?.emit('message', { action: 'subscribe', topics });
  console.log('[ws] subscribe →', topics);
}

export function unsubscribe(topics: string[]) {
  socket?.emit('message', { action: 'unsubscribe', topics });
  console.log('[ws] unsubscribe →', topics);
}

export function getSocket(): Socket | null {
  return socket;
}

export function disconnectSocket() {
  if (socket) {
    socket.disconnect();
    socket = null;
  }
}

### apps\busverse-v2\src\lib\ws\useWsEvent.ts
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { getSocket } from './socket';

/**
 * Subscribe to a specific event on a specific topic.
 * The server publishes events as:
 *   socket.emit('message', { event, topic, data, ts })
 */
export function useWsEvent<T = any>(
  topic: string,
  eventName: string,
  handler: (data: T) => void,
  enabled = true,
) {
  const handlerRef = useRef(handler);
  handlerRef.current = handler;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const socket = getSocket();
    if (!socket) return;

    const listener = (msg: any) => {
      if (msg?.topic === topic && msg?.event === eventName) {
        handlerRef.current(msg.data as T);
      }
    };

    socket.on('message', listener);
    return () => {
      socket.off('message', listener);
    };
  }, [topic, eventName, enabled]);
}

### apps\busverse-v2\src\features\seats\api\seats.api.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from '@/lib/api/client';

export type SeatStatus = 'AVAILABLE' | 'LOCKED' | 'BOOKED' | 'MINE';
export type SeatType = 'SEATER' | 'SLEEPER' | 'SEMI_SLEEPER';

/** Single source of truth for the seat shape used by every component. */
export interface Seat {
  id: string;
  seatLabel: string;
  seatRow: string;
  seatColumn: number;
  seatType: SeatType;
  isFemaleOnly: boolean;
  status: SeatStatus;
  lockedUntil?: string | null;
}

export interface SeatLayout {
  rowsCount: number;
  columnsCount: number;
  aisleCells: Array<{ row: string; column: number }>;
}

export interface SeatLock {
  lockId: string;
  seatId: string;
  seatLabel: string;
  expiresAt: string;
}

export interface AvailabilityResponse {
  seats: Seat[];
  layout: SeatLayout;
}

function toRowLetter(v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v === 'number') return String.fromCharCode(64 + v);
  return String(v ?? '');
}

function mapSeat(raw: any): Seat {
  return {
    id: String(raw.id ?? ''),
    seatLabel: String(raw.label ?? raw.seatLabel ?? ''),
    seatRow: toRowLetter(raw.row ?? raw.seatRow ?? ''),
    seatColumn: Number(raw.column ?? raw.seatColumn ?? 0),
    seatType: (raw.type ?? raw.seatType ?? 'SEATER') as SeatType,
    isFemaleOnly: !!raw.isFemaleOnly,
    // ⭐ The API sends `state`; we normalize to `status` — all consumers read `.status`
    status: (raw.state ?? raw.status ?? 'AVAILABLE') as SeatStatus,
    lockedUntil: raw.lockedUntil ?? null,
  };
}

export const seatsApi = {
  async getAvailability(scheduleId: string): Promise<AvailabilityResponse> {
    const raw = await api.get<any>(`/passenger/schedules/${scheduleId}/availability`);
    const layoutRaw = raw?.layout ?? raw ?? {};
    const seatsRaw: any[] = raw?.seats ?? [];
    const seats = seatsRaw.map(mapSeat);

    const aisleCells = (layoutRaw?.aisleCells ?? []).map((c: any) => ({
      row: toRowLetter(c?.row ?? ''),
      column: Number(c?.column ?? 0),
    }));

    return {
      seats,
      layout: {
        rowsCount: Number(layoutRaw?.rowsCount ?? 0),
        columnsCount: Number(layoutRaw?.columnsCount ?? 0),
        aisleCells,
      },
    };
  },

  async lock(scheduleId: string, seatId: string): Promise<SeatLock> {
    const raw = await api.post<any>('/passenger/seat-locks', { scheduleId, seatId });
    return {
      lockId: String(raw.lockId ?? ''),
      seatId,
      seatLabel: String(raw.seatLabel ?? ''),
      expiresAt: String(raw.expiresAt ?? ''),
    };
  },

  async release(lockId: string): Promise<void> {
    await api.delete(`/passenger/seat-locks/${lockId}`);
  },
};

### apps\busverse-v2\src\components\seats\SeatDeck.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import type { Seat, SeatLayout } from '@/features/seats/api/seats.api';
import { SeatCell } from './SeatCell';

interface SeatDeckProps {
  seats: Seat[];
  layout: SeatLayout;
  selectedSeatIds: string[];
  onSeatClick: (seat: Seat) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function SeatDeck({
  seats,
  layout,
  selectedSeatIds,
  onSeatClick,
  disabled = false,
}: SeatDeckProps) {
  const seatByKey = new Map<string, Seat>();
  seats.forEach((s) => seatByKey.set(`${s.seatRow}${s.seatColumn}`, s));

  const rowLetters: string[] = [];
  for (let r = 0; r < layout.rowsCount; r++) {
    rowLetters.push(String.fromCharCode(65 + r));
  }

  const cols = layout.columnsCount;
  const leftCols = Math.min(2, Math.max(1, cols - 1));
  const rightCols = cols - leftCols;
  const selectedSet = new Set(selectedSeatIds);

  return (
    <div className="inline-block p-6 rounded-3xl bg-surface border-2 border-border-strong shadow-floating">
      <div className="mb-5 pb-4 border-b-2 border-dashed border-border-subtle flex items-center gap-8 justify-between">
        <div className="h-6 px-3 rounded-t-lg bg-[var(--telemetry-cyan-subtle)] border border-[var(--telemetry-cyan)] flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-[var(--telemetry-cyan)] uppercase tracking-wider">
          Windshield
        </div>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold uppercase text-text-muted">
          Driver
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2.5">
        {rowLetters.map((rowChar) => {
          const leftSeats: Seat[] = [];
          const rightSeats: Seat[] = [];
          for (let c = 1; c <= leftCols; c++) {
            const s = seatByKey.get(`${rowChar}${c}`);
            if (s) leftSeats.push(s);
          }
          for (let c = leftCols + 1; c <= cols; c++) {
            const s = seatByKey.get(`${rowChar}${c}`);
            if (s) rightSeats.push(s);
          }

          return (
            <div key={rowChar} className="flex items-center gap-1 justify-center">
              <span className="w-5 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-text-muted text-center">
                {rowChar}
              </span>
              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                {leftSeats.length > 0 ? leftSeats.map((s) => (
                  <SeatCell
                    key={s.id}
                    seat={s}
                    isSelectedByMe={selectedSet.has(s.id)}
                    onToggle={onSeatClick}
                    disabled={disabled}
                  />
                )) : <div className="w-10 h-11" aria-hidden="true" />}
              </div>
              <div className="w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                {rightSeats.length > 0 ? rightSeats.map((s) => (
                  <SeatCell
                    key={s.id}
                    seat={s}
                    isSelectedByMe={selectedSet.has(s.id)}
                    onToggle={onSeatClick}
                    disabled={disabled}
                  />
                )) : <div className="w-10 h-11" aria-hidden="true" />}
              </div>
              <span className="w-5 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-text-muted text-center">
                {rowChar}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 pt-3 border-t-2 border-border-subtle text-center">
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-text-muted uppercase tracking-widest">
          Rear exit
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
### apps\busverse-v2\src\components\seats\SeatCell.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import type { Seat } from '@/features/seats/api/seats.api';

interface SeatCellProps {
  seat: Seat;
  isSelectedByMe: boolean;
  onToggle: (seat: Seat) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function SeatCell({ seat, isSelectedByMe, onToggle, disabled = false }: SeatCellProps) {
  // ⭐ Read `.status` — the field our API mapper produces. Never `.state`.
  const isBooked = seat.status === 'BOOKED';
  const isLockedByOther = seat.status === 'LOCKED' && !isSelectedByMe;
  const isInactive = isBooked || isLockedByOther || disabled;

  let cls =
    'bg-[var(--seat-available-bg)] border-[var(--seat-available-border)] text-[var(--seat-available-text)]';

  if (isBooked) {
    cls =
      'bg-[var(--seat-booked-bg)] border-[var(--seat-booked-border)] text-[var(--seat-booked-text)] cursor-not-allowed';
  } else if (isSelectedByMe) {
    cls =
      'bg-[var(--seat-selected-bg)] border-[var(--seat-selected-border)] text-[var(--seat-selected-text)] -translate-y-0.5';
  } else if (isLockedByOther) {
    cls =
      'bg-[var(--seat-locked-bg)] border-[var(--seat-locked-border)] text-[var(--seat-locked-text)] cursor-not-allowed animate-pulse';
  } else if (seat.isFemaleOnly) {
    cls =
      'bg-[var(--seat-female-bg)] border-[var(--seat-female-border)] text-[var(--seat-female-text)]';
  }

  const height =
    seat.seatType === 'SLEEPER'
      ? 'h-16'
      : seat.seatType === 'SEMI_SLEEPER'
      ? 'h-14'
      : 'h-11';

  const ariaStatus =
    isBooked ? 'booked' :
    isLockedByOther ? 'locked by another user' :
    isSelectedByMe ? 'selected by you' :
    'available';

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      disabled={isInactive}
      onClick={() => onToggle(seat)}
      aria-pressed={isSelectedByMe}
      aria-label={`Seat ${seat.seatLabel}, ${ariaStatus}${seat.isFemaleOnly ? ', female preferred' : ''}`}
      title={`${seat.seatLabel} · ${ariaStatus}${seat.isFemaleOnly ? ' · female only' : ''}`}
      className={`relative flex w-10 ${height} flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-lg border-2 font-mono text-[11px] font-bold transition-all ${cls}`}
    >
      <span>{seat.seatLabel}</span>
      <div className="absolute bottom-1 h-0.5 w-6 rounded bg-black/20 dark:bg-white/20" />
    </button>
  );
}

### apps\busverse-v2\src\components\tracking\LiveMap.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useMemo } from 'react';

interface Stop {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  name: string;
}

interface LiveMapProps {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  stops?: Stop[];
  height?: number;
}

export function LiveMap({ lat, lng, stops = [], height = 400 }: LiveMapProps) {
  const html = useMemo(() => {
    const stopsJs = stops
      .map((s) => {
        const safe = (s.name || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '\\"');
        return `L.circleMarker([${s.lat}, ${s.lng}], { radius: 6, color: '#3B82F6', fillColor: '#3B82F6', fillOpacity: 1, weight: 2 }).addTo(map).bindPopup('${safe}');`;
      })
      .join('\n');

    return `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head>
      <meta charset="utf-8">
      <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.css" />
      <style>
        body,html,#map{height:100%;margin:0;background:#0A0F18}
        .leaflet-control-attribution{background:rgba(6,10,18,0.9)!important;color:#94A3B8!important;font-size:9px!important}
        .leaflet-control-attribution a{color:#3B82F6!important}
      </style>
    </head><body>
      <div id="map"></div>
      <script src="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js"></script>
      <script>
        var map = L.map('map', { zoomControl: false }).setView([${lat}, ${lng}], 11);
        L.tileLayer('https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', { attribution: '© OSM' }).addTo(map);

        var busIcon = L.divIcon({
          html: '<div style="width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:11px;background:#10B981;border:3px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 0 14px #10B981,0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);"></div>',
          iconSize: [22, 22],
          className: 'bus-marker'
        });
        L.marker([${lat}, ${lng}], { icon: busIcon }).addTo(map);

        ${stopsJs}

        // Enable map interactions on mobile
        map.touchZoom.enable();
        map.doubleClickZoom.enable();
        map.scrollWheelZoom.enable();
      </script>
    </body></html>`;
  }, [lat, lng, stops]);

  return (
    <div style={{ height, borderRadius: 16, overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid var(--border-strong)' }}>
      <iframe
        srcDoc={html}
        style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', border: 'none' }}
        title="Live bus tracking map"
        sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
### apps\busverse-v2\src\features\booking\PassengerForm.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { Stand } from '@/lib/api/bookings.api';
import type { SeatLock } from '@/features/seats/api/seats.api';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input';
import { formatINR } from '@/lib/utils/formatters';

export interface PassengerInput {
  seatId: string;
  seatLabel: string;
  name: string;
  age: string;
  gender: 'MALE' | 'FEMALE' | 'OTHER' | '';
}

interface PassengerFormProps {
  scheduleId: string;
  locks: SeatLock[];
  stops: Stand[];
  farePerSeatPaise: number;
  onSubmit: (data: {
    boardingStandId: string;
    droppingStandId: string;
    passengers: PassengerInput[];
  }) => Promise<void>;
  submitting: boolean;
  error: string | null;
}

export function PassengerForm({
  locks,
  stops,
  farePerSeatPaise,
  onSubmit,
  submitting,
  error,
}: PassengerFormProps) {
  const sortedStops = useMemo(() => [...stops].sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence), [stops]);

  const [boardingStandId, setBoardingStandId] = useState<string>(sortedStops[0]?.standId ?? '');
  const [droppingStandId, setDroppingStandId] = useState<string>(
    sortedStops[sortedStops.length - 1]?.standId ?? '',
  );
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState<PassengerInput[]>(
    locks.map((l) => ({
      seatId: l.seatId,
      seatLabel: l.seatLabel,
      name: '',
      age: '',
      gender: '',
    })),
  );
  const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const boardingStops = sortedStops.slice(0, Math.max(1, sortedStops.length - 1));
  const droppingStops = sortedStops.slice(1);

  const updatePassenger = (idx: number, patch: Partial<PassengerInput>) => {
    setPassengers((prev) => prev.map((p, i) => (i === idx ? { ...p, ...patch } : p)));
  };

  const validate = (): string | null => {
    if (!boardingStandId) return 'Select a boarding stand.';
    if (!droppingStandId) return 'Select a dropping stand.';
    const bIdx = sortedStops.findIndex((s) => s.standId === boardingStandId);
    const dIdx = sortedStops.findIndex((s) => s.standId === droppingStandId);
    if (bIdx < 0 || dIdx < 0) return 'Invalid stand selection.';
    if (bIdx >= dIdx) return 'Dropping stand must come after boarding stand.';

    for (let i = 0; i < passengers.length; i++) {
      const p = passengers[i];
      if (!p.name.trim() || p.name.trim().length < 2) return `Passenger ${i + 1}: enter a valid name.`;
      const age = parseInt(p.age, 10);
      if (isNaN(age) || age < 1 || age > 119) return `Passenger ${i + 1}: enter a valid age (1–119).`;
      if (!p.gender) return `Passenger ${i + 1}: select a gender.`;
    }
    return null;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLocalError(null);
    const err = validate();
    if (err) {
      setLocalError(err);
      return;
    }
    try {
      await onSubmit({ boardingStandId, droppingStandId, passengers });
    } catch (err) {
      /* parent handles error state */
    }
  };

  const totalPaise = farePerSeatPaise * locks.length;
  const shownError = localError ?? error;

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-surface border border-border-hairline rounded-lg p-4">
        <p className="text-xs font-mono uppercase text-text-muted tracking-wider mb-2">
          Boarding stand
        </p>
        <select
          value={boardingStandId}
          onChange={(e) => setBoardingStandId(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full h-11 px-3 rounded-lg bg-surface text-text-primary border border-border-subtle focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand/30 focus:border-border-focus"
        >
          {boardingStops.map((s) => (
            <option key={s.standId} value={s.standId}>
              {s.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <p className="text-xs font-mono uppercase text-text-muted tracking-wider mb-2 mt-4">
          Dropping stand
        </p>
        <select
          value={droppingStandId}
          onChange={(e) => setDroppingStandId(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full h-11 px-3 rounded-lg bg-surface text-text-primary border border-border-subtle focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand/30 focus:border-border-focus"
        >
          {droppingStops.map((s) => (
            <option key={s.standId} value={s.standId}>
              {s.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      {passengers.map((p, idx) => (
        <div
          key={p.seatId}
          className="bg-surface border border-border-hairline rounded-lg p-5 space-y-4"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-border-hairline">
            <span className="text-xs font-mono uppercase text-text-muted">
              Passenger {idx + 1}
            </span>
            <span className="text-sm font-bold font-mono text-brand px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-subtle border border-brand-border">
              Seat {p.seatLabel}
            </span>
          </div>

          <Input
            label="Full name"
            value={p.name}
            onChange={(e) => updatePassenger(idx, { name: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="e.g. Alice Sharma"
            autoComplete="name"
          />

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <Input
              label="Age"
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              value={p.age}
              onChange={(e) => updatePassenger(idx, { age: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="e.g. 28"
              min={1}
              max={119}
            />
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-text-primary mb-2">
                Gender
              </label>
              <select
                value={p.gender}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  updatePassenger(idx, { gender: e.target.value as PassengerInput['gender'] })
                }
                className="w-full h-11 px-3 rounded-lg bg-surface text-text-primary border border-border-subtle focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand/30 focus:border-border-focus"
              >
                <option value="">Select</option>
                <option value="MALE">Male</option>
                <option value="FEMALE">Female</option>
                <option value="OTHER">Other</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}

      {shownError && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-[var(--danger-crimson-subtle)] border border-[var(--danger-crimson)] text-sm text-[var(--danger-crimson)]"
        >
          {shownError}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-surface border border-border-strong rounded-lg p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <span className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
            {locks.length} {locks.length === 1 ? 'seat' : 'seats'} · fare per seat
          </span>
          <span className="text-sm font-mono text-text-primary">
            {formatINR(farePerSeatPaise)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-4 border-b border-border-hairline">
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-text-bright">Total</span>
          <span className="text-lg font-bold font-mono text-text-bright">
            {formatINR(totalPaise)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" loading={submitting} fullWidth className="mt-4">
          Continue to payment
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

### apps\driver-app\src\components\TripMap.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { WebView } from 'react-native-webview';
import { theme } from '../theme/tokens';

export interface TripMapRef {
  recenter: () => void;
}

interface Props {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  zoom?: number;
  height?: number | string;
  markers?: any[];
  routeGeometry?: any;
  onReady?: (ref: TripMapRef) => void;
}

export function TripMap({ lat, lng, zoom = 12, height = 250, markers = [], routeGeometry = null, onReady }: Props) {
  const webViewRef = useRef<WebView>(null);

  const html = useMemo(() => {
    // Safely escape single quotes in marker labels
    const markersJs = markers.map((m: any) => {
      const safeLabel = (m.label || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");
      return `L.circleMarker([${m.lat}, ${m.lng}], { radius: 6, color: '${theme.colors.textMuted}', fillColor: '${theme.colors.textMuted}', fillOpacity: 1 }).addTo(map).bindPopup('${safeLabel}');`;
    }).join('\n');
    
    const routeJs = routeGeometry ? `L.geoJSON(${JSON.stringify(routeGeometry)}, { style: { color: '${theme.colors.primary}', weight: 5, opacity: 0.8 } }).addTo(map);` : '';

    return `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head>
      <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.css" />
      <style>
        body,html,#map{height:100%;margin:0;background:${theme.colors.bg}}
        /* This CSS filter makes standard OSM tiles look soft, light, and pastel */
        .leaflet-layer {
          filter: grayscale(100%) brightness(1.05) contrast(0.85) opacity(0.85);
        }
        .leaflet-control-attribution{background:rgba(255,255,255,0.92)!important;color:${theme.colors.textMuted}!important;font-size:9px!important}
        .leaflet-control-attribution a{color:${theme.colors.primary}!important}
      </style>
    </head><body><div id="map"></div>
      <script src="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js"></script>
      <script>
        var map = L.map('map', { zoomControl: false }).setView([${lat}, ${lng}], ${zoom});
        
        // Standard OpenStreetMap tiles (100% reliable, no API key needed)
        L.tileLayer('https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', { attribution: '&copy; OSM' }).addTo(map);
        
        var busIcon = L.divIcon({ html: '<div style="width:16px;height:16px;border-radius:8px;background:${theme.colors.primary};border:2px solid ${theme.colors.surface};box-shadow:0 0 10px rgba(196,135,135,0.6);"></div>', iconSize:[16,16] });
        L.marker([${lat}, ${lng}], {icon: busIcon}).addTo(map);
        
        ${routeJs}
        ${markersJs}
        
        window.recenterMap = function() {
          map.setView([${lat}, ${lng}], ${zoom}, { animate: true });
        };
      </script></body></html>`;
  }, [lat, lng, zoom, markers, routeGeometry]);

  return (
    <View style={[styles.wrap, { height, borderColor: theme.colors.border }]}>
      <WebView 
        ref={webViewRef}
        source={{ html }} 
        style={{ backgroundColor: theme.colors.bg }} 
        javaScriptEnabled 
        domStorageEnabled 
        onLoad={() => {
          if (onReady) {
            onReady({
              recenter: () => {
                webViewRef.current?.injectJavaScript('window.recenterMap(); true;');
              }
            });
          }
        }}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { borderRadius: 16, overflow: 'hidden', borderWidth: 1 },
});

### apps\driver-app\src\components\ErrorBoundary.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { theme } from '../theme/tokens';

type State = { hasError: boolean; error: Error | null };

export class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component<{ children: React.ReactNode }, State> {
  state: State = { hasError: false, error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: React.ErrorInfo) {
    console.error('[ErrorBoundary]', error?.message, info?.componentStack);
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <View style={s.root}>
          <Text style={s.title}>App crashed</Text>
          <Text style={s.hint}>Screenshot this screen and send to the dev.</Text>
          <ScrollView style={s.box}>
            <Text style={s.errText}>{this.state.error?.message ?? 'Unknown'}</Text>
            {this.state.error?.stack ? (
              <Text style={s.stackText}>{this.state.error.stack}</Text>
            ) : null}
          </ScrollView>
          <Pressable style={s.btn} onPress={() => this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null })}>
            <Text style={s.btnText}>Try again</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#1a0a0a', padding: 24, paddingTop: 60 },
  title: { color: '#F87171', fontSize: 22, fontWeight: '800', marginBottom: 6 },
  hint: { color: '#FCA5A5', fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 16 },
  box: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#0a0505', borderRadius: 8, padding: 12, marginBottom: 16 },
  errText: { color: '#F87171', fontSize: 14, marginBottom: 12, fontWeight: '700' },
  stackText: { color: '#86EFAC', fontSize: 11, fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  btn: { backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: 'center' },
  btnText: { color: '#000', fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 16 },
});


================================================================================
### apps\driver-app\src\theme\ThemeContext.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useColorScheme } from 'react-native';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { brandTokens, ThemeMode, Theme } from './brandTokens';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'theme_mode';

interface ThemeCtx {
  mode: ThemeMode;
  theme: Theme;
  setMode: (m: ThemeMode) => void;
  cycleMode: () => void;
}

const Ctx = createContext<ThemeCtx | undefined>(undefined);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const systemScheme = useColorScheme();
  const [mode, setModeState] = useState<ThemeMode>('auto');

  useEffect(() => {
    SecureStore.getItemAsync(STORAGE_KEY)
      .then((saved) => {
        if (saved === 'light' || saved === 'dark' || saved === 'auto') {
          setModeState(saved);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const theme = useMemo(() => {
    const resolved =
      mode === 'auto' ? (systemScheme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light') : mode;
    return resolved === 'dark' ? (brandTokens.dark as Theme) : (brandTokens.light as Theme);
  }, [mode, systemScheme]);

  const setMode = useCallback((m: ThemeMode) => {
    setModeState(m);
    SecureStore.setItemAsync(STORAGE_KEY, m).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const cycleMode = useCallback(() => {
    setModeState((prev) => {
      const next: ThemeMode =
        prev === 'auto' ? 'light' : prev === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'auto';
      SecureStore.setItemAsync(STORAGE_KEY, next).catch(() => {});
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ mode, theme, setMode, cycleMode }),
    [mode, theme, setMode, cycleMode],
  );

  return <Ctx.Provider value={value}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export function useTheme() {
  const ctx = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider');
  return ctx;
}

================================================================================
### apps\driver-app\src\theme\brandTokens.ts
================================================================================
export type ThemeMode = 'auto' | 'light' | 'dark';

export interface ThemeColors {
  bg: string;
  surface: string;
  surfaceElevated: string;
  border: string;
  borderSubtle: string;
  primary: string;
  primaryDim: string;
  text: string;
  textMuted: string;
  danger: string;
  warning: string;
  success: string;
}

export interface Theme {
  isDark: boolean;
  colors: ThemeColors;
}

================================================================================
### apps\driver-app\src\lib\api\client.ts
================================================================================
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';

const FALLBACK_URL = 'http://10.57.155.179:4310/v1';

function resolveBaseUrl(): string {
  const env = (process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE ?? '').trim();
  if (env) {
    return env.replace(/\/+$/, '');
  }
  return FALLBACK_URL;
}

const BASE_URL = resolveBaseUrl();
console.log('[API] Base URL = ' + BASE_URL);

let memoryToken: string | null = null;

export async function setToken(token: string | null) {
  memoryToken = token;
  try {
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      if (token) await SecureStore.setItemAsync('access_token', token);
      else await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync('access_token');
    } else {
      if (token) localStorage.setItem('access_token', token);
      else localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
    }
  } catch (e) { console.log('setToken error:', e); }
}

export async function getToken(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (memoryToken) return memoryToken;
  try {
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      memoryToken = await SecureStore.getItemAsync('access_token');
    } else {
      memoryToken = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    }
  } catch { memoryToken = null; }
  return memoryToken;
}

export const api = {
  request: async (path: string, options: RequestInit = {}) => {
    const token = await getToken();
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...options.headers as Record<string, string>,
    };
    if (token) {
      headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + token;
    }

    console.log('[API] -> ' + (options.method || 'GET') + ' ' + BASE_URL + path, options.body || '');

    try {
      const url = BASE_URL + path;
      const res = await fetch(url, { ...options, headers });
      if (res.status === 204) return null;

      const text = await res.text();
      const data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;

      console.log('[API] <- ' + res.status + ' ' + url, data);

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((data && data.error && data.error.message) || ('HTTP ' + res.status));
      return (data && data.data !== undefined) ? data.data : data;
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.log('[API] ERROR ' + BASE_URL + path, err.message);
      throw err;
    }
  },
  get: (path: string) => api.request(path),
  post: (path: string, body: any) => api.request(path, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
};

### apps\driver-app\src\lib\api\trips.api.ts

import { api } from './client';

export const listTrips = (date?: string) => api.get(`/driver/trips${date ? `?date=${date}` : ''}`);
export const getTrip = (tripId: string) => api.get(`/driver/trips/${tripId}`);
export const startTrip = (tripId: string) => api.post(`/driver/trips/${tripId}/start`, {});
export const endTrip = (tripId: string) => api.post(`/driver/trips/${tripId}/end`, {});
export const getManifest = (tripId: string) => api.get(`/driver/trips/${tripId}/manifest`);
export const postScan = (tripId: string, body: any) => api.post(`/driver/trips/${tripId}/scan`, body);
export const getPublicSchedule = (scheduleId: string) => api.get(`/public/schedules/${scheduleId}`);

export type GpsSample = {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  speedKmh: number;
  accuracyM: number;
  recordedAt: string;
};

export const postGps = (tripId: string, sample: GpsSample) =>
  api.post(`/driver/trips/${tripId}/gps`, sample);

import { api } from './client';

export const requestOtp = (phone: string) => api.post('/driver/auth/otp/request', { phone });
export const verifyOtp = (phone: string, otp: string, deviceFingerprint: string) => 
  api.post('/driver/auth/otp/verify', { phone, otp, deviceFingerprint });
export const fetchDriverMe = () => api.get('/driver/me');
export const fetchDriverConfig = () => api.get('/driver/config');

================================================================================
### apps\driver-app\src\lib\auth\AuthContext.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { setToken, getToken } from '../api/client';
import * as authApi from '../api/auth.api';

const Ctx = createContext<any>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const token = await getToken();
        if (token) {
          try {
            const me = await authApi.fetchDriverMe();
            setUser(me);
          } catch (e) {
            console.log('fetchDriverMe failed:', e);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.log('getToken failed:', e);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  const login = async (phone: string, otp: string) => {
    const res = await authApi.verifyOtp(phone, otp, 'demo-device-fingerprint');
    await setToken(res.accessToken);
    setUser(res.user);
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    await setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  };

  return <Ctx.Provider value={{ user, login, logout, loading }}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export const useAuth = () => {
  const ctx = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
};
